📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期狂犬病病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 吉尔福德县于 2026 年确认了第 18 起动物 狂犬病 病例,涉及斯托克斯代尔麦克罗里路 (McCrory Road) 的一只臭鼬。该报告由吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务机构 - 公共卫生部发布。 [news-ac219f8cf25497bd]。
  • 据 Contagion Live 报道,欧盟批准赛诺菲的 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于所有年龄段。 [news-6383850bfa37c9d1]。
  • 欧盟委员会批准赛诺菲的 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于所有年龄段个体的暴露前预防 (PrEP) 和 暴露后预防 (PEP)。这是基于人用药品委员会的积极意见,疫苗 的首次营销授权。 [news-a66fcd17211ed132]。
  • 确诊一例人类 狂犬病 病例,卫生部正在调查可能的暴露情况。该妇女情况危急,卫生部正在努力查明其他可能接触过该感染患者的人。 [news-90e434d707d3988e]。

  • Guilford County confirmed an eighteenth case of animal rabies in 2026, involving a skunk on McCrory Road in Stokesdale.
  • The EU approved Sanofi's Rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for all age groups, as reported by Contagion Live. [news-6383850bfa37c9d1].
  • The European Commission approved Sanofi's rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) in individuals of all ages.
  • A human rabies case was diagnosed, and the Health Ministry is investigating possible exposures.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Research Square  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-20  |  DOI: 10.21203/rs.3.rs-11327433/v1  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

宿主 感染的内部 宿主 数学模型,具有结构化轴突运输和暴露后控制策略

An intra-host mathematical model for rabies infection with structured axonal transport and post-exposure control strategies
作者： Hippolyte Djimramadji, Siguy Dawé, Hisseine Bachar Mahamat, Siguy Dawe
摘要中文翻译我们提出了一种用于 狂犬病病毒 感染的新型 宿主 内数学模型,该模型从机械上整合了从接种部位到唾液腺的病毒进展的主要阶段。该模型被公式化为非线性常微分方程系统,耦合局部肌肉感染的动力学、周围神经末梢的非线性病毒捕获(由希尔函数建模)、作为连续隔室链的结构化逆行轴突运输、中枢神经系统入侵、唾液传播、局部效应器免疫应答以及三种依赖时间的暴露后控制策略。一个关键的新颖之处在于通过离散的轴突步骤明确表示病毒的神经营养进展,为研究治疗窗口和优化干预措施提供了合适的框架。数学分析确定解的正性和有界性以及全局解的存在性和唯一性。确定无病平衡,并导出基本繁殖数 R 0,定义局部感染建立的阈值。规定了局部稳定性条件,并构建了 Lya-punov 函数来证明当 R 0 < 1 时无病平衡的全局渐近稳定性,对于任何 Hill 协同性 q ≥ 1 都有效。还提出了机械神经侵袭指数 R n 来量化病毒通过轴突链进展到中枢神经系统的概率。最后,制定最优控制问题,以在干预成本约束下最大限度地减少病毒载量和唾液传播,通过庞特里亚金极大值原理导出必要的最优性条件,并通过前向-后向扫描方法对所得最优系统进行数值求解,其性能以不断努力策略为基准。该模型构成了评估暴露后预防策略的严格基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种数学模型,捕获 宿主 内 狂犬病病毒 感染的复杂动态,满足更详细地了解病毒进展和暴露后控制策略的需要。
设计与对象
该研究提出了 狂犬病病毒 感染的数学模型,重点关注病毒从接种部位到唾液腺的 宿主 内部动态,并包括三种依赖时间的暴露后控制策略。
核心方法
该模型被表述为非线性常微分方程组,包含结构化逆行轴突传输、局部效应器 免疫应答 和三种依赖时间的暴露后控制策略。制定了最佳控制问题,以在干预成本限制下最大限度地减少病毒载量和唾液传播。
主要结果
数学分析确认解的正性和有界性,建立全局解的存在性和唯一性,确定无病平衡,并导出基本繁殖数 R0,定义局部感染建立的阈值。
解释与创新
作者将该模型解释为评估暴露后预防策略的严格基础,其关键新颖之处在于通过离散轴突步骤明确表示神经营养进展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解 狂犬病病毒 感染的动态和制定优化的暴露后干预措施提供了一个框架,这可以为 监测 和控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于数学模型,不包括经验数据,这可能会影响证据的强度。该证据被认为是中等的,因为它基于理论分析和模拟。

An intra-host mathematical model for rabies infection with structured axonal transport and post-exposure control strategies

Authors: Hippolyte Djimramadji, Siguy Dawé, Hisseine Bachar Mahamat, Siguy Dawe
Original abstract (English)
We propose a novel intra-host mathematical model for rabies virus infection, which mechanistically integrates the main stages of viral progression from the inoculation site to the salivary glands. The model is formulated as a system of nonlinear ordinary differential equations coupling the dynamics of local muscle infection, nonlinear viral capture by peripheral nerve endings (modeled by a Hill function), structured retrograde axonal transport as a chain of successive compartments, central nervous system invasion, salivary dissemination, a local effector immune response, and three time-dependent post-exposure control strategies. A key novelty lies in the explicit representation of the neurotropic progression of the virus via discrete axonal steps, offering a suitable framework to study therapeutic windows and optimized interventions. Mathematical analysis establishes the positivity and boundedness of solutions as well as the existence and uniqueness of a global solution. A disease-free equilibrium is identified, and a basic reproduction number R 0 is derived, defining a threshold for local infection establishment. A local stability condition is stated, and a Lya-punov function is constructed to demonstrate the global asymptotic stability of the disease-free equilibrium when R 0 < 1, valid for any Hill cooperativity q ≥ 1. A mechanistic neuroinvasion index R n is also proposed to quantify the probability of virus progression to the central nervous system via the axonal chain. Finally, an optimal control problem is formulated to minimize viral load and salivary dissemination under intervention cost constraints, necessary optimality conditions are derived via Pontryagin’s maximum principle, and the resulting optimality system is solved numerically via a forward–backward sweep method, whose performance is benchmarked against a constant-effort strategy. This model constitutes a rigorous basis for evaluating post-exposure prophylactic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a mathematical model that captures the complex dynamics of rabies virus infection within a host, addressing the need for a more detailed understanding of viral progression and post-exposure control strategies.
Design and population
The study presents a mathematical model of rabies virus infection, focusing on the intra-host dynamics of the virus from the inoculation site to the salivary glands, and includes three time-dependent post-exposure control strategies.
Core methods
The model is formulated as a system of nonlinear ordinary differential equations, incorporating structured retrograde axonal transport, local effector immune response, and three time-dependent post-exposure control strategies. An optimal control problem is formulated to minimize viral load and salivary dissemination under intervention cost constraints.
Main results
Mathematical analysis confirms the positivity and boundedness of solutions, establishes the existence and uniqueness of a global solution, identifies a disease-free equilibrium, and derives a basic reproduction number R0 defining a threshold for local infection establishment.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the model as a rigorous basis for evaluating post-exposure prophylactic strategies, with a key novelty being the explicit representation of neurotropic progression via discrete axonal steps.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a framework for understanding the dynamics of rabies virus infection and for developing optimized post-exposure interventions, which can inform surveillance and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mathematical model and does not include empirical data, which may affect the strength of the evidence. The evidence is considered moderate as it is based on theoretical analysis and simulations.

使用机器学习、分子对接和分子动力学对针对 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白的假定肽抑制剂进行计算设计和优先排序

Computational design and prioritization of putative peptide inhibitors targeting rabies virus glycoprotein using machine learning, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics
作者： Harshada Sawant, Ashish Asrondkar, Vrushali Patil
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是全球最致命的病毒性人畜共患疾病。狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 (RABV-G) 介导 宿主 细胞识别和病毒进入;它代表了开发新型抗病毒药物的治疗靶点。在本研究中,采用计算肽设计策略来设计针对 RABV-G 的肽抑制剂。通过 突变 和优化,从 α-银环蛇毒素的受体结合区域设计肽序列,同时消除神经毒性。使用混合机器学习深度框架进行毒性筛查。通过不同工具的框架对所选的无毒肽进行理化表征、抗病毒药物 活性预测、分子对接、结合亲和力和分子动力学模拟来分析肽 RABV-G 相互作用。使用 SWISS MODEL 生成 RABV-G 的三维模型,并通过 Ramchandran 图分析进行验证,其中 92.5% 的残基位于有利区域内。结合口袋分析鉴定了参与肽结合的关键残基,包括 Glu272、Glu279、Asp283、Lys276 和 Arg297。在评估的候选者中,MKMKCHTTATMKIV (P40) 显示出最有利的对接,HADDOCK 得分为 − 90 ± 3.8,结合自由能为 -10.3kcal/mol,其次是 CLDLGYTIVCHTTATSPI (P3)、MKIVCHTTATSPMKV (P21) 和 MKIVCHTTMKSPMK (P25)。分子动力学模拟(100ns)分析表明,P40和P25与RABV-G形成高度稳定的复合物,在分子间相互作用下表现出低RMSD和RMSF波动,并在整个模拟过程中表现出良好的自由能景观和稳定的构象。这项研究设计表明,α-金环蛇毒素衍生肽可以靶向 RABV-G,而 P440 可能成为开发基于肽的药物递送的有前途的主要候选药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是全球最致命的病毒性人畜共患疾病。狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 (RABV-G) 介导 宿主 细胞识别和病毒进入;它代表了开发新型抗病毒药物的治疗靶点。
设计与对象
在本研究中,采用计算肽设计策略来设计针对 RABV-G 的肽抑制剂。通过 突变 和优化,从 α-银环蛇毒素的受体结合区域设计肽序列,同时消除神经毒性。
核心方法
使用混合机器学习深度框架进行毒性筛查。通过不同工具的框架对所选的无毒肽进行理化表征、抗病毒药物 活性预测、分子对接、结合亲和力和分子动力学模拟来分析肽 RABV-G 相互作用。
主要结果
在评估的候选者中,MKMKCHTTATMKIV (P40) 显示出最有利的对接,HADDOCK 得分为 − 90 ± 3.8,结合自由能为 -10.3kcal/mol,其次是 CLDLGYTIVCHTTATSPI (P3)、MKIVCHTTATSPMKV (P21) 和 MKIVCHTTMKSPMK (P25)。分子动力学模拟(100ns)分析表明,P40和P25与RABV-G形成高度稳定的复合物,在分子间相互作用下表现出低RMSD和RMSF波动,并在整个模拟过程中表现出良好的自由能景观和稳定的构象。
解释与创新
这项研究设计表明,α-金环蛇毒素衍生肽可以靶向 RABV-G,而 P440 可能成为开发基于肽的药物递送的有前途的主要候选药物。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为开发针对 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白的新治疗策略提供信息,以预防 狂犬病 感染和 治疗 感染。该计算方法为设计在 抗病毒药物 药物开发中具有潜在应用的肽抑制剂提供了一个框架。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。该研究基于计算方法,不包括实验验证,这可能会影响证据的强度。

Computational design and prioritization of putative peptide inhibitors targeting rabies virus glycoprotein using machine learning, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics

Authors: Harshada Sawant, Ashish Asrondkar, Vrushali Patil
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is the most lethal viral zoonotic diseases worldwide. The rabies virus glycoprotein (RABV-G) which mediates host cell recognition and viral entry; it represents a therapeutic target for the development of novel anti-viral agents. In the present study, a computational peptide design strategy was employed to design peptide inhibitors against RABV-G. Peptide sequences were designed from the receptor binding region of α-bungarotoxin through mutation and optimisation while eliminating neurotoxic properties. A hybrid machine learning-deep framework was used for toxicity screening. The selected non-toxic peptides were analysed through framework of different tools for Physiochemical characterization, antiviral activity prediction, molecular docking, binding affinity and molecular dynamic simulation were performed to analyse peptide RABV-G interactions. A three-dimensional model of RABV-G was generated using SWISS MODEL and validated through Ramchandran plot analysis with 92.5% residues within favoured regions. Binding pocket analysis identified key residues involved in peptide binding including Glu272, Glu279., Asp283, Lys276 and Arg297. Among the evaluated candidates, MKMKCHTTATMKIV (P40) shown the most favourable docking with a HADDOCK scores − 90 ± 3.8, and binding free energy of -10.3kcal/mol, followed by CLDLGYTIVCHTTATSPI (P3), MKIVCHTTATSPMKV (P21), and MKIVCHTTMKSPMK (P25). Molecular dynamic simulations (100ns) analysed that P40 and P25 formed highly stable complexes with RABV-G, exhibiting low RMSD and RMSF fluctuation with intermolecular interaction, and favourable free energy landscapes and stable conformational through simulation period. This study design shows the α-bungarotoxin derived peptides can target RABV-G and P440 can be promising lead candidate for development of peptide-based drug delivery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is the most lethal viral zoonotic diseases worldwide. The rabies virus glycoprotein (RABV-G) which mediates host cell recognition and viral entry; it represents a therapeutic target for the development of novel anti-viral agents.
Design and population
In the present study, a computational peptide design strategy was employed to design peptide inhibitors against RABV-G. Peptide sequences were designed from the receptor binding region of α-bungarotoxin through mutation and optimisation while eliminating neurotoxic properties.
Core methods
A hybrid machine learning-deep framework was used for toxicity screening. The selected non-toxic peptides were analysed through framework of different tools for Physiochemical characterization, antiviral activity prediction, molecular docking, binding affinity and molecular dynamic simulation were performed to analyse peptide RABV-G interactions.
Main results
Among the evaluated candidates, MKMKCHTTATMKIV (P40) shown the most favourable docking with a HADDOCK scores − 90 ± 3.8, and binding free energy of -10.3kcal/mol, followed by CLDLGYTIVCHTTATSPI (P3), MKIVCHTTATSPMKV (P21), and MKIVCHTTMKSPMK (P25). Molecular dynamic simulations (100ns) analysed that P40 and P25 formed highly stable complexes with RABV-G, exhibiting low RMSD and RMSF fluctuation with intermolecular interaction, and favourable free energy landscapes and stable conformational through simulation period.
Interpretation and novelty
This study design shows the α-bungarotoxin derived peptides can target RABV-G and P440 can be promising lead candidate for development of peptide-based drug delivery.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of novel therapeutic strategies targeting rabies virus glycoprotein for the prevention and treatment of rabies infection. The computational approach provides a framework for designing peptide inhibitors with potential applications in antiviral drug development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength. The study is based on computational methods and does not include experimental validation, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

Journal: Journal of Public Health in Africa  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-18  |  DOI: 10.4102/jphia.v17i2.2262  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 17, 2
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

1990-2023 年埃塞俄比亚人类 狂犬病 的负担和趋势:2023 年全球疾病负担研究的国家和地方系统分析

Burden and trends of human rabies in Ethiopia, 1990–2023: A national and subnational systematic analysis of the Global Burden of Disease Study 2023
作者： Taye Abuhay Zewale, Alemtsehay Mekonnen, Ally E. Walker, Awoke Misganaw, Dereje Bedane Hunde, Gedefaw Abeje Fekadu, Jemal Beksisa, Mesafint Molla Adane, Mohsen Naghavi, Tesfaye Mersha Baye
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是低收入和中等收入国家的重大公共卫生威胁。埃塞俄比亚是非洲受影响第二严重的国家,每年报告超过 2700 人死亡和 193 748 伤残调整生命年 (DALY)。
设计与对象
本研究重点关注埃塞俄比亚的 11 个地区州和两个市政府,研究了 1990 年至 2023 年 狂犬病 负担的趋势。
核心方法
本研究利用了 2023 年全球疾病负担 (GBD) 研究的数据。估计了年龄标准化的 发病率、死亡率 和 DALY 率。使用总百分比和年度百分比变化以及相应的 95% 不确定性区间 (UI) 检查随时间变化的趋势。比较了不同年龄组、地区和年份的变化。
主要结果
Between 1990 and 2023, the burden of rabies in Ethiopia declined significantly. The incidence rate decreased from 8.4 (95% UI: 1.1, 28.7) to 1.4 (95% UI: 0.18, 4.7); mortality declined from 7.4 (95% UI: 0.79, 27.3) to 1.4 (95% UI: 0.18, 4.6); and DALYs dropped from 439.4 (95% UI: 49.3, 1605.6) to 78.3 (95% UI: 9.5, 257.9) per 100 000 population. Individuals aged 5–14 years bore the highest rabies burden in all metrics. The Southwest, Southern Nations, Nationalities, and Peoples’ Regional State, Amhara, and Oromia regions exhibited the highest rabies burden in Ethiopia.
解释与创新
尽管 1990 年至 2023 年间,埃塞俄比亚人类 狂犬病 的负担显着减轻,但问题仍然存在,不同年龄组、地区和年份之间存在巨大差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调,埃塞俄比亚需要通过确定高负担地理区域和风险最大的年龄组,制定有针对性的人类 狂犬病 预防和控制策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Burden and trends of human rabies in Ethiopia, 1990–2023: A national and subnational systematic analysis of the Global Burden of Disease Study 2023

Authors: Taye Abuhay Zewale, Alemtsehay Mekonnen, Ally E. Walker, Awoke Misganaw, Dereje Bedane Hunde, Gedefaw Abeje Fekadu, Jemal Beksisa, Mesafint Molla Adane, Mohsen Naghavi, Tesfaye Mersha Baye
Original abstract (English)
Background: Rabies remains a significant public health threat in low- and middle-income countries. Ethiopia ranks as the second most affected country in Africa, reporting over 2700 human deaths and 193 748 disability-adjusted life years (DALYs) annually. Despite existing control measures, challenges related to treatment accessibility and implementation persist. Aim: To estimate rabies-associated incidence, mortality and DALYs rate in Ethiopia from 1990 to 2023. Setting: Eleven regional states and two city administrations in Ethiopia. Methods: This study utilised data from Global Burden of Disease (GBD) 2023 study. Age-standardised incidence, mortality and DALY rates were estimated. Trends over time were examined using total percentage and annual percentage change with corresponding 95% uncertainty intervals (UIs). Variations by age groups, regions and year were compared. Results: Between 1990 and 2023, the burden of rabies in Ethiopia declined significantly. The incidence rate decreased from 8.4 (95% UI: 1.1, 28.7) to 1.4 (95% UI: 0.18, 4.7); mortality declined from 7.4 (95% UI: 0.79, 27.3) to 1.4 (95% UI: 0.18, 4.6); and DALYs dropped from 439.4 (95% UI: 49.3, 1605.6) to 78.3 (95% UI: 9.5, 257.9) per 100 000 population. Individuals aged 5–14 years bore the highest rabies burden in all metrics. The Southwest, Southern Nations, Nationalities, and Peoples’ Regional State, Amhara, and Oromia regions exhibited the highest rabies burden in Ethiopia. Conclusion: Despite the burden of human rabies decreasing significantly between 1990 and 2023 in Ethiopia, the problem persists with substantial variations across age groups, regions and years. Contribution: These findings underscore the need for targeted human rabies prevention and control strategies in Ethiopia by identifying high-burden geographic areas and the age groups at greatest risk.

通过 N 基因重新编码设计低细胞毒性重组 狂犬病病毒 神经示踪剂

Engineering a low cytotoxicity recombinant rabies virus neurotracer via N-gene recoding
作者： You Hu, Qinghan Wang, Kangyixin Sun, Li Li, Zhaoyang Yin, Qing Liu, Ke Xiao, Xiuqing Xiao, Haizhou Liu, Dejuan Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于 Viral-媒介 的示踪剂已被证明对于绘制神经回路具有无价的价值。基于 狂犬病病毒 (RV) 的逆行跨单突触示踪剂已广泛用于描绘目标神经元群体的输入神经回路。然而,RV示踪剂的细胞毒性限制了其应用。在这里,我们采用合成减毒病毒工程技术,通过密码子对去优化重新编码RV SAD-B19毒株的N基因,产生减毒RV示踪剂。重新编码的 RV 示踪剂的特性在体外和体内进行了测试。全长重新编码的N基因消除了病毒拯救,而部分重新编码的N基因支持成功的病毒恢复。与其亲本相比,重新编码的 RV 跨单突触示踪剂表现出较低的细胞毒性并标记了更多的输入神经元。它还可以稳健地表达蛋白质,以可视化神经元形态并操纵神经回路活动。总的来说,我们的工作产生了一种新的逆行跨单突触工具,它补充了现有的神经回路示踪剂。重要性 病毒 媒介 在生物医学的多个领域发挥着重要作用。在神经科学界,基于 狂犬病病毒 (RV) 的逆行跨单突触示踪剂已被广泛用于绘制目标神经元群体的输入神经回路。然而,基于 RV 的示踪剂固有的细胞毒性限制了其更广泛的应用。在本研究中,我们利用合成减毒病毒工程技术重新编码RV N基因,生成减毒神经示踪剂,这将极大有利于未来的神经科学研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于 狂犬病病毒 (RV) 的逆行跨单突触示踪剂已广泛用于描绘目标神经元群体的输入神经回路。然而,RV示踪剂的细胞毒性限制了其应用。
设计与对象
该研究采用合成减毒病毒工程技术,通过密码子对去优化重新编码RV SAD-B19毒株的N基因,产生减毒RV示踪剂。重新编码的 RV 示踪剂的特性在体外和体内进行了测试。
核心方法
全长重新编码的N基因消除了病毒拯救,而部分重新编码的N基因支持成功的病毒恢复。
主要结果
与其亲本相比,重新编码的 RV 跨单突触示踪剂表现出较低的细胞毒性并标记了更多的输入神经元。
解释与创新
重新编码的 RV 跨单突触示踪剂提供了一种新工具,用于绘制具有降低细胞毒性的神经回路,它补充了现有的神经回路示踪剂。
科研与公卫意义
这一进展可以实现更精确、危害更小的神经回路映射,从而极大地有益于未来的神经科学研究。

该研究没有报告局限性,但证据基于体外和体内测试,可能无法完全代表现实世界的应用。

Engineering a low cytotoxicity recombinant rabies virus neurotracer via N-gene recoding

Authors: You Hu, Qinghan Wang, Kangyixin Sun, Li Li, Zhaoyang Yin, Qing Liu, Ke Xiao, Xiuqing Xiao, Haizhou Liu, Dejuan Wang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Viral-vector-based tracers have proven invaluable for mapping neural circuits. Rabies virus (RV)-based retrograde trans-monosynaptic tracers have been widely used for depicting the input neural circuit of targeted neuronal populations. However, the cytotoxicity of the RV tracer limits its application. Here, we employed the synthetic attenuated virus engineering technology to recode the N gene of the RV SAD-B19 strain by codon pair deoptimization to generate attenuated RV tracers. The characteristics of the recoded RV tracers were tested both in vitro and in vivo . The full-length recoded N gene abolished viral rescue, whereas the partial recoded N gene supported successful viral recovery. Compared to its parent, the recoded RV trans-monosynaptic tracer exhibited lower cytotoxicity and labeled more input neurons. It can also robustly express proteins to visualize the neuronal morphology and manipulate the neural circuit activity. Collectively, our work generated a new retrograde trans-monosynaptic tool, which complements existing neural circuit tracers. IMPORTANCE Viral vectors play an important role across multiple fields in biomedicine. In the neuroscience community, rabies virus (RV)-based retrograde trans-monosynaptic tracers have been widely used to map the input neural circuits of targeted neuronal populations. However, the inherent cytotoxicity of RV-based tracers limits their broader application. In this study, we utilized synthetic attenuated virus engineering technology to recode the RV N gene, generating an attenuated neurotracer that will significantly benefit future neuroscience research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies virus (RV)-based retrograde trans-monosynaptic tracers have been widely used for depicting the input neural circuit of targeted neuronal populations. However, the cytotoxicity of the RV tracer limits its application.
Design and population
The study employed synthetic attenuated virus engineering technology to recode the N gene of the RV SAD-B19 strain by codon pair deoptimization to generate attenuated RV tracers. The characteristics of the recoded RV tracers were tested both in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
The full-length recoded N gene abolished viral rescue, whereas the partial recoded N gene supported successful viral recovery.
Main results
Compared to its parent, the recoded RV trans-monosynaptic tracer exhibited lower cytotoxicity and labeled more input neurons.
Interpretation and novelty
The recoded RV trans-monosynaptic tracer provides a new tool for mapping neural circuits with reduced cytotoxicity, which complements existing neural circuit tracers.
Scientific and public-health significance
This development can significantly benefit future neuroscience research by enabling more precise and less harmful mapping of neural circuits.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on in vitro and in vivo testing, which may not fully represent real-world applications.

不完全 暴露后预防 后的非典型 狂犬病 脑脊髓炎:青少年血清学 暴露后预防 和存活延迟。

Atypical Rabies Encephalomyelitis Following Incomplete Post-Exposure Prophylaxis: Delayed Serological Diagnosis and Survival in an Adolescent.
作者： Jagriti Yadav, Rohitha Jaikumar, Narendra Kumar Arya, Karan Singh Garbyal
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是一种几乎总是致命的病毒性脑炎,其典型特征是恐水和恐气。缺乏这些标志性特征的非典型表现并不常见,并且经常导致诊断延迟,特别是对于部分免疫的个体。据报道,一名原本健康的 15 岁男孩在被 III 类豺狼咬伤 25 天后患上进行性脑脊髓炎。尽管他完成了暴露后 狂犬病 狂犬病,但未施用 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白。他出现四肢瘫痪和感觉改变,但没有恐水或恐气症状。最初的 狂犬病 逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 和脑脊液 (CSF) 抗体检测均为阴性。随后的系列评估表明,血清和脑脊液中的 狂犬病病毒 中和抗体滴度上升,证实了 狂犬病 脑炎。在重症支持护理下,患者从急性疾病中幸存下来,但因持续的神经功能缺损而出院。该病例凸显了非典型 狂犬病 的诊断挑战,并强调了完整 暴露后预防 的重要性。

Atypical Rabies Encephalomyelitis Following Incomplete Post-Exposure Prophylaxis: Delayed Serological Diagnosis and Survival in an Adolescent.

Authors: Jagriti Yadav, Rohitha Jaikumar, Narendra Kumar Arya, Karan Singh Garbyal
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is an almost invariably fatal viral encephalitis classically characterized by hydrophobia and aerophobia. Atypical presentations lacking these hallmark features are uncommon and frequently result in diagnostic delay, particularly in partially immunized individuals. Reported is a 15-year-old previously healthy boy who developed progressive encephalomyelitis 25 days after sustaining a Category III jackal bite. Although he completed post-exposure rabies vaccination, rabies immunoglobulin was not administered. He presented with quadriparesis and altered sensorium without hydrophobia or aerophobia. Initial rabies reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) antibody testing were negative. Serial evaluation subsequently demonstrated rising rabies virus-neutralizing antibody titers in both serum and CSF, confirming rabies encephalitis. With intensive supportive care, the patient survived the acute illness but was discharged with persistent neurological deficits. This case highlights the diagnostic challenges of atypical rabies and underscores the importance of complete post-exposure prophylaxis.
View research seven-element analysis

柬埔寨未来的 疫苗 优先事项:为新出现的传染病做好准备。

Future vaccine priorities in Cambodia: preparedness for emerging infectious diseases.
作者： Sao Puth, Vandara Loeurng
摘要中文翻译过去二十年来,柬埔寨在免疫接种方面取得了长足进步,最终促成了东南亚最成功的 COVID-19 疫苗接种 运动之一。然而,流行病学格局正在迅速变化。气候变化、环境退化、城市化、区域流动性增加以及人与动物相互作用的加剧正在增加新发传染病的风险,使柬埔寨处于未来流行病和大流行威胁的前线。尽管国家 疫苗 政策传统上侧重于儿童免疫,但为应对新出现的传染病,需要制定更广泛的战略,将病原体 疫苗、基因组监测、监管准备、快速 疫苗 平台和 One Health 协作结合起来。在此,我们强调柬埔寨需要从 疫苗 交付议程过渡到更广泛的 疫苗 准备议程。我们提出了一个以 疫苗 优先顺序、加强实验室和基因组能力、有弹性的 疫苗 交付系统、可持续融资和区域伙伴关系为中心的国家框架。这种方法可以加强柬埔寨对 H5N1、登革热、日本脑炎、狂犬病 和未来 X 病等病原体的防范,同时加强其对区域卫生安全的贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了柬埔寨需要调整其 疫苗 优先事项以应对新出现的传染病的问题,强调了不断变化的流行病学格局和做好准备的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究是在柬埔寨进行的一项政策分析,重点关注国家 疫苗 战略及其对应对新发传染病的公共卫生准备的影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查柬埔寨当前的 疫苗 政策、评估影响疾病出现的流行病学和环境因素,以及制定拟议的 疫苗 防备国家框架。
主要结果
该研究报告称,柬埔寨的国家 疫苗 政策传统上侧重于儿童免疫接种,但针对新出现的传染病的准备需要更广泛的战略,包括 疫苗、基因组监测、监管准备、快速 疫苗 平台和 One Health 合作。
解释与创新
作者解释说,柬埔寨需要从 疫苗 交付议程转向 疫苗 准备议程,提出一个整合 疫苗、实验室能力和区域伙伴关系的国家框架,以应对未来的流行病和大流行威胁。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为柬埔寨制定更全面的疫苗战略提供信息,增强其应对H5N1、登革热、日本脑炎、狂犬病等病原体和未来X疾病情景的能力,同时为区域卫生安全做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于政策分析,不包括实证数据或统计分析,这限制了证据的强度。然而,其影响是基于当前的公共卫生挑战和拟议的策略。

Future vaccine priorities in Cambodia: preparedness for emerging infectious diseases.

Authors: Sao Puth, Vandara Loeurng
Original abstract (English)
Cambodia has achieved substantial progress in immunisation over the past two decades, culminating in one of Southeast Asia's most successful COVID-19 vaccination campaigns. However, the epidemiological landscape is rapidly changing. Climate change, environmental degradation, urbanisation, increasing regional mobility, and intensified human-animal interactions are increasing the risk of emerging infectious diseases, placing Cambodia at the frontline of future epidemic and pandemic threats. Although national vaccine policy has traditionally focused on childhood immunisation, preparedness for emerging infectious diseases requires a broader strategy that integrates pathogen surveillance, genomic monitoring, regulatory readiness, rapid vaccine platforms, and One Health collaboration. Here, we highlight the need for Cambodia to transition from a vaccine delivery agenda toward a broader vaccine preparedness agenda. We propose a national framework centred around surveillance-informed vaccine prioritisation, strengthened laboratory and genomic capacity, resilient vaccine delivery systems, sustainable financing, and regional partnerships. Such an approach could enhance Cambodia's preparedness for pathogens including H5N1, dengue, Japanese encephalitis, rabies, and future Disease X scenarios, while strengthening its contribution to regional health security.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for Cambodia to adapt its vaccine priorities to address emerging infectious diseases, highlighting the changing epidemiological landscape and the importance of preparedness.
Design and population
The study is a policy analysis conducted in Cambodia, focusing on the national vaccine strategy and its implications for public health preparedness against emerging infectious diseases.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of Cambodia's current vaccine policies, an assessment of the epidemiological and environmental factors influencing disease emergence, and the development of a proposed national framework for vaccine preparedness.
Main results
The study reports that Cambodia's national vaccine policy has traditionally focused on childhood immunisation, but preparedness for emerging infectious diseases requires a broader strategy that includes surveillance, genomic monitoring, regulatory readiness, rapid vaccine platforms, and One Health collaboration.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that Cambodia needs to shift from a vaccine delivery agenda to a vaccine preparedness agenda, proposing a national framework that integrates surveillance, laboratory capacity, and regional partnerships to address future epidemic and pandemic threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform Cambodia's development of a more comprehensive vaccine strategy, enhancing its ability to respond to pathogens such as H5N1, dengue, Japanese encephalitis, rabies, and future Disease X scenarios, while contributing to regional health security.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a policy analysis and does not include empirical data or statistical analysis, which limits the strength of the evidence. However, the implications are grounded in current public health challenges and proposed strategies.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

中国各级医院的传染病诊断能力:全国横断面调查。

Infectious Disease Diagnostic Capacity Across Hospital Tiers in China: National Cross-Sectional Survey.
作者： Ruyi Zhang, Yu Wu, Ziyang Ren, Lieyu Huang, Yihao Zhao, Xiaoying Zheng, Jie Qian, Yajie Wang, 郑小英
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估中国各级医院的传染病诊断能力,解决可能影响病例确定和报告疾病负担解释的医院能力参差不齐的问题。
设计与对象
2023年对中国2689家医院进行了全国横断面网络调查,其中一级医院124家,二级医院1478家,三级医院1087家。
核心方法
该调查涉及集中质量控制和资格筛选、评估内部和发送测试的可用性、诊断方式和实施障碍。
主要结果
内部诊断覆盖率从一级医院的11.6%提高到二级医院的27.9%和三级医院的39.5%。包括发送测试在内的总体覆盖率分别为 14%、32.6% 和 44.2%。乙型肝炎检测已广泛应用,但肺结核检测显示出明显的分级梯度。对于低 发病率 但后果严重的感染,特定检测方式的可用性有限,并且地理差异部分与已知的疾病流行情况一致。
解释与创新
该研究发现,不同级别医院的诊断能力存在显着差异,差异不仅限于疾病覆盖范围,还包括特定的检测方式以及能力扩张的障碍。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持分层的、区域响应的诊断网络,该网络提供更接近首次接触护理的基本测试,同时确保通过可靠、有质量保证的转诊途径及时获得专业诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性,包括对自我报告数据的依赖和潜在的报告偏差,可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Infectious Disease Diagnostic Capacity Across Hospital Tiers in China: National Cross-Sectional Survey.

Authors: Ruyi Zhang, Yu Wu, Ziyang Ren, Lieyu Huang, Yihao Zhao, Xiaoying Zheng, Jie Qian, Yajie Wang, 郑小英
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: China's notifiable infectious disease surveillance relies on clinical recognition and diagnostic testing, but uneven hospital capacity may affect case ascertainment and interpretation of reported burden. OBJECTIVE: This study aims to assess infectious disease diagnostic capacity across hospital tiers in China, including alignment with notified disease burden, regional variation, diagnostic modalities, and implementation barriers. METHODS: We conducted a national cross-sectional web-based survey of hospitals in China in 2023. After centralized quality control and eligibility screening, 2689 hospitals were included: 124 (4.6%) primary, 1478 (55%) secondary, and 1087 (40.4%) tertiary hospitals. We assessed in-house and send-out testing availability; compared diagnostic availability with national notified incidence and mortality; and examined regional variation, selected diagnostic modalities, and reported barriers. RESULTS: Median in-house diagnostic coverage increased from 11.6% (IQR 9.3%-18.6%) in primary hospitals to 27.9% (IQR 18.6%-37.2%) in secondary hospitals and 39.5% (IQR 27.9%-48.8%) in tertiary hospitals; corresponding overall coverage, including send-out testing, was 14% (IQR 9.3%-23.3%), 32.6% (IQR 23.3%-44.2%), and 44.2% (IQR 30.2%-55.8%), respectively. Tier differences persisted in sensitivity analyses. Diagnostic availability was not consistently aligned with notified disease burden. In-house hepatitis B testing was available in 82.3% (102/124), 98% (1449/1478), and 99.3% (1079/1087) of primary, secondary, and tertiary hospitals, respectively. Pulmonary tuberculosis showed a pronounced tier gradient (26/124, 21% in primary vs 910/1087, 83.7% in tertiary hospitals). Hand, foot, and mouth disease and other infectious diarrheal diseases had limited availability even in tertiary hospitals (380/1087, 35% and 456/1087, 42%, respectively) despite high incidence. Some lower-incidence but high-consequence infections also had limited local capacity, including hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome (151/1087, 13.9%) and rabies (43/1087, 4%) in tertiary hospitals. Geographic variation was partly consistent with known disease endemicity, with higher testing availability for hemorrhagic fever with renal syndrome in northeastern hospitals, although only 35.9% (55/153) of tertiary hospitals in this region offered in-house testing. Diagnostic depth also varied across tiers: hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA testing increased from 8.1% (10/124) in primary hospitals to 61.1% (664/1087) in tertiary hospitals. For COVID-19, primary hospitals more frequently reported antigen than nucleic acid testing (57/124, 46% vs 27/124, 21.8%). Lower-tier hospitals more often reported workforce and infrastructure constraints, whereas tertiary hospitals more frequently cited reagent-licensing and fee-schedule barriers. CONCLUSIONS: Among surveyed hospitals, infectious disease diagnostic capacity differed substantially across hospital tiers, varied geographically, and was not consistently aligned with notified disease burden. These disparities extended beyond disease coverage to specific testing modalities and barriers to capacity expansion. These findings support tiered, regionally responsive diagnostic networks that provide essential testing closer to first-contact care while ensuring timely access to specialized diagnostics through reliable, quality-assured referral pathways.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess infectious disease diagnostic capacity across hospital tiers in China, addressing the uneven hospital capacity that may affect case ascertainment and interpretation of reported disease burden.
Design and population
A national cross-sectional web-based survey of 2689 hospitals in China was conducted in 2023, including 124 primary, 1478 secondary, and 1087 tertiary hospitals.
Core methods
The survey involved centralized quality control and eligibility screening, assessing in-house and send-out testing availability, diagnostic modalities, and implementation barriers.
Main results
In-house diagnostic coverage increased from 11.6% in primary hospitals to 27.9% in secondary hospitals and 39.5% in tertiary hospitals. Overall coverage, including send-out testing, was 14%, 32.6%, and 44.2% respectively. Hepatitis B testing was widely available, but pulmonary tuberculosis showed a pronounced tier gradient. Limited availability of specific testing modalities was noted for lower-incidence but high-consequence infections, and geographic variation was partly consistent with known disease endemicity.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found substantial differences in diagnostic capacity across hospital tiers, with disparities extending beyond disease coverage to specific testing modalities and barriers to capacity expansion.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support tiered, regionally responsive diagnostic networks that provide essential testing closer to first-contact care while ensuring timely access to specialized diagnostics through reliable, quality-assured referral pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations, including the reliance on self-reported data and potential reporting biases, may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

比较非政府组织和政府管理的狗 疫苗接种:对坦桑尼亚梅鲁动物和人类的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 狂犬病 结果的影响

Comparing NGO and government-managed dog vaccination: Implications for vaccination coverage and rabies outcomes in animals and humans in Meru, Tanzania
作者： Gresha Mdeka, Jens Fissenebert, Maganga Burton Sambo, Esther G. Kimaro, Kennedy Selestin Lushasi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种致命但可预防的人畜共患疾病,主要由家犬传播。群体犬 疫苗接种 (MDV) 是消除 狂犬病 的核心,但 疫苗接种 在流行地区的覆盖率往往仍然不足且不均匀。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了坦桑尼亚梅鲁两种截然不同的交付安排下狗 疫苗接种 的覆盖率:有组织的、非政府 (NGO) 管理的年度 MDV 活动和在政府管理的病房中主要由主人发起的 疫苗接种 服务。疫苗接种 在非政府组织和政府管理的病房中的覆盖率是通过在非政府组织管理的运动之后进行的家庭调查以及在没有实施有组织的年度 MDV 运动的政府管理的病房中进行的家庭调查来估计的。
核心方法
非政府组织管理的 MDV 运动(2020-2024 年)的 疫苗接种 数据是从 Mbwa wa Africa 记录中获得的,2025 年的数据是在运动实施期间收集的。有关人类暴露和疑似动物病例(2023-2025 年)的数据是从综合咬伤病例管理系统中提取的。使用广义线性混合模型 (GLMM) 比较两种设置之间的 疫苗接种 覆盖率,并评估 疫苗接种 覆盖率、人类暴露和动物病例之间的关联。
主要结果
Vaccination coverage in NGO-managed wards increased from 45.1% in 2020 to 71% in 2025, while government-managed wards remained low, peaking at 21.9% in 2023 before declining to 8.8% in 2025. Human exposures and animal cases declined in 2024 but increased in 2025. Animal rabies cases were associated with human exposures. Vaccination coverage was not associated with human exposures (IRR = 1.0, 95% CI: 0.998–1.010, p = 0.730) but was associated with animal cases (IRR = 1.02, 95% CI: 1.01–1.04, p = 0.008).
解释与创新
GLMM analysis confirmed higher vaccination coverage in NGO-managed wards (IRR = 12.1, 95% CI: 12.1–12.2; p < 0.05). The findings highlight the importance of accessible, coordinated, and equitable dog vaccination services integrated with One Health surveillance.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供的证据表明,协调一致的跨部门规划——将兽医 疫苗接种 运动、人类健康 监测 和社区参与联系起来——对于实现和维持消除目标至关重要。通过将大量狗 疫苗接种 数据与综合咬伤案例管理 (IBCM) 监测 数据相结合,我们展示了动物卫生服务提供的差异如何直接转化为人类 狂犬病 暴露风险。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的局限性。由于该研究提供了详细的统计分析并使用多个数据源,因此证据强度很高。

Comparing NGO and government-managed dog vaccination: Implications for vaccination coverage and rabies outcomes in animals and humans in Meru, Tanzania

Authors: Gresha Mdeka, Jens Fissenebert, Maganga Burton Sambo, Esther G. Kimaro, Kennedy Selestin Lushasi
Original abstract (English)
Background : Rabies is a fatal but preventable zoonotic disease transmitted primarily by domestic dogs. Mass dog vaccination (MDV) is central to rabies elimination, yet vaccination coverage in endemic settings often remains insufficient and uneven. This study assessed dog vaccination coverage under two contrasting delivery arrangements in Meru, Tanzania: Organised, non-government (NGO)-managed annual MDV campaigns and predominantly owner-initiated vaccination services in government-managed wards. It also examined human rabies exposures and animal rabies cases and identified barriers and facilitators affecting vaccination delivery. Methods : Vaccination data from NGO-managed MDV campaigns (2020–2024) were obtained from Mbwa wa Africa records, and 2025 data were collected during campaign implementation. Vaccination coverage in both NGO and government-managed wards was estimated using household surveys conducted after NGO-managed campaigns and in government-managed wards where no organised annual MDV campaigns were implemented. Data on human exposures and suspected animal cases (2023–2025) were extracted from the Integrated Bite Case Management System. Generalized linear mixed models (GLMMs) were used to compare vaccination coverage between the two settings and to assess the association among vaccination coverage, human exposures, and animal cases. Qualitative interviews were conducted to explore barriers to and facilitators of MDV. Results : Vaccination coverage in NGO-managed wards increased from 45.1% in 2020 to 71% in 2025, while government-managed wards remained low, peaking at 21.9% in 2023 before declining to 8.8% in 2025. GLMM analysis confirmed higher vaccination coverage in NGO-managed wards (IRR = 12.1, 95% CI: 12.1–12.2; p < 0.05). Human exposures and animal cases declined in 2024 but increased in 2025. Animal rabies cases were associated with human exposures. Vaccination coverage was not associated with human exposures (IRR = 1.0, 95% CI: 0.998–1.010, p = 0.730) but was associated with animal cases (IRR =1.02, 95% CI: 1.01–1.04, p = 0.008). Barriers included limited veterinary outreach, vaccination fees, dog handling difficulties, low awareness, and free-roaming dogs. Facilitators included decentralised delivery, free services, and community mobilisation. Conclusion : Organised NGO-managed campaigns were associated with higher vaccination coverage than the predominantly owner-initiated vaccination services available in government-managed wards. However, uneven spatial coverage remained a challenge for district-wide rabies control. The findings highlight the importance of accessible, coordinated, and equitable dog vaccination services integrated with One Health surveillance. One Health impact statement Rabies remains a One Health challenge at the human-animal interface. This study demonstrates that fragmented vaccination delivery and uneven spatial coverage undermine ward-level rabies control, even where high coverage is achieved locally. By integrating mass dog vaccination data with Integrated Bite Case Management (IBCM) surveillance data, we show how disparities in animal health service delivery directly translate into human rabies exposure risk. Importantly, the IBCM datasets analysed in this study were developed through routine cross-sector collaboration between the human health and animal health sectors, illustrating the practical application of the One Health approach in rabies surveillance. The findings provide evidence that coordinated, cross-sectoral planning – linking veterinary vaccination campaigns, human health surveillance, and community engagement – is essential to achieve and sustain elimination targets. This work illustrates how aligning NGO and government strategies within a unified One Health framework can strengthen policy implementation, improve equity in service delivery, and accelerate progress toward “Zero by 30” targets.

RVG29 修饰的还原响应纳米胶束,用于在阿尔茨海默病中脑部靶向递送谷氨酰胺酰环化酶抑制剂。

RVG29-modified reduction-responsive nanomicelles for brain-targeted delivery of a glutaminyl cyclase inhibitor in Alzheimer's disease.
作者： Tao Zhou, Yuanyuan Xu, Qingqing Zhou, Sohrab Khan, Jingyue Li, Jingying Wu, Ziying Xu, Junmin Quan, Wei Xiong, Haiqiang Wu
摘要中文翻译尽管谷氨酰胺酰环化酶 (QC) 介导的焦谷氨酸修饰的淀粉样蛋白 -β (pE-Aβ) 的形成被认为是阿尔茨海默病 (AD) 的上游病理事件,但小分子 QC 抑制剂的治疗转化仍然受到大脑暴露不足的限制。在这里,我们开发了一种基于聚乙二醇(PEG)-聚(天冬氨酸)(PAsp)-聚(缬氨酸)(PVal)共聚物的还原响应聚合物胶束平台(指定为PIC)。该平台使用 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白衍生肽 29 (RVG29) 进行功能化,以获得 RVG29-PIC-B13,用于增强 B13(一种基于咪唑的 QC 抑制剂)的脑部递送,并释放其活性硫醇形式。B13 通过二硫键与 PEG-PAsp-PVal 胶束缀合,而 RVG29 则作为脑靶向配体引入,以增强血脑屏障转运。RVG29-PIC-B13形成球形纳米胶束,平均直径为169.9 nm,载药量为6.2%,胶体稳定性高,谷胱甘肽触发释放B13-SH,保留了QC抑制活性,IC 50 为1.17 μM。与游离 Cy5 和未修饰的胶束相比,RVG29 修饰显着增强了 bEND.3、PC12 和 SH-SY5Y 细胞中的细胞摄取,使穿过 bEND.3 单层的转运增加了 3.12 倍,并使体内脑荧光积累分别增加了 2.58 倍和 1.96 倍。在 8 个月大的 3 × Tg-AD 小鼠中,RVG29-PIC-B13 给药 45 天显着改善了 Morris 水迷宫、Y 迷宫、开放场和高架十字迷宫测定的性能。从机制上讲,RVG29-PIC-B13 抑制大脑 QC 活性,但不显着改变 QC 表达,减少 Aβ 和 pE-Aβ 负担,减弱 AT8/AT100 相关 tau 磷酸化,并保留树突复杂性和树突棘密度。初步的血液学和组织学分析表明具有可接受的生物相容性。这些发现支持 RVG29-PIC-B13 作为一种有前途的还原响应纳米递送策略,用于增强 QC 抑制剂的大脑递送并改善 AD 的治疗结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于大脑暴露不足而导致小分子谷氨酰胺酰环化酶抑制剂在阿尔茨海默病中的治疗转化有限的问题。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种还原响应纳米胶束平台,使用 8 个月大的 3 × Tg-AD 小鼠,在阿尔茨海默病中脑部靶向递送谷氨酰胺酰环化酶抑制剂。
核心方法
该平台使用 RVG29 进行功能化,以通过二硫键连接体增强 B13(一种基于咪唑的 QC 抑制剂)的活性硫醇形式的大脑递送和释放。
主要结果
与游离 Cy5 和未修饰的胶束相比,RVG29 修饰增加了 bEND.3、PC12 和 SH-SY5Y 细胞中的细胞摄取,将跨 bEND.3 单层的转运增强了 3.12 倍,并将体内脑荧光积累分别提高了 2.58 倍和 1.96 倍。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RVG29-PIC-B13 是一种有前途的策略,可增强 QC 抑制剂的大脑递送并改善阿尔茨海默病的治疗效果。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为阿尔茨海默病靶向药物输送系统的开发提供信息,从而有可能提高谷氨酰胺酰环化酶抑制剂的功效。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用动物模型和缺乏长期安全数据,但由于清楚地证明了增强的大脑输送和治疗效果,证据是强有力的。

RVG29-modified reduction-responsive nanomicelles for brain-targeted delivery of a glutaminyl cyclase inhibitor in Alzheimer's disease.

Authors: Tao Zhou, Yuanyuan Xu, Qingqing Zhou, Sohrab Khan, Jingyue Li, Jingying Wu, Ziying Xu, Junmin Quan, Wei Xiong, Haiqiang Wu
Original abstract (English)
Although glutaminyl cyclase (QC)-mediated formation of pyroglutamate-modified amyloid-β (pE-Aβ) has been implicated as an upstream pathological event in Alzheimer's disease (AD), the therapeutic translation of small-molecule QC inhibitors remains limited by insufficient brain exposure. Here, we developed a reduction-responsive polymeric micellar platform (designated PIC) based on poly(ethylene glycol) (PEG)-poly(aspartic acid) (PAsp)-poly(valine) (PVal) copolymers. This platform was functionalized with rabies virus glycoprotein-derived peptide 29 (RVG29) to obtain RVG29-PIC-B13 for enhanced brain delivery of B13, an imidazole-based QC inhibitor, and release of its active thiol form. B13 was conjugated to PEG-PAsp-PVal micelles through a disulfide linker, whereas RVG29 was introduced as a brain-targeting ligand to enhance blood-brain barrier transport. RVG29-PIC-B13 formed spherical nanomicelles with an average diameter of 169.9 nm, a drug-loading content of 6.2%, high colloidal stability, and glutathione-triggered release of B13-SH, which retained QC inhibitory activity with an IC 50 of 1.17 μM. RVG29 modification significantly enhanced cellular uptake in bEND.3, PC12, and SH-SY5Y cells, increased transport across a bEND.3 monolayer by 3.12-fold, and elevated brain fluorescence accumulation in vivo by 2.58-fold and 1.96-fold compared with free Cy5 and unmodified micelles, respectively. In 8-month-old 3 × Tg-AD mice, 45-day administration of RVG29-PIC-B13 significantly improved performance in the Morris water maze, Y-maze, open-field, and elevated plus maze assays. Mechanistically, RVG29-PIC-B13 inhibited brain QC activity without markedly altering QC expression, reduced Aβ and pE-Aβ burden, attenuated AT8/AT100-associated tau phosphorylation, and preserved dendritic complexity and spine density. Preliminary hematological and histological analyses indicated acceptable biocompatibility. These findings support RVG29-PIC-B13 as a promising reduction-responsive nanodelivery strategy for enhancing brain delivery of QC inhibitors and improving therapeutic outcomes in AD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited therapeutic translation of small-molecule glutaminyl cyclase inhibitors in Alzheimer's disease due to insufficient brain exposure.
Design and population
The study developed a reduction-responsive nanomicellar platform for brain-targeted delivery of a glutaminyl cyclase inhibitor in Alzheimer's disease using 8-month-old 3 × Tg-AD mice.
Core methods
The platform was functionalized with RVG29 to enhance brain delivery and release of the active thiol form of B13, an imidazole-based QC inhibitor, through a disulfide linker.
Main results
RVG29 modification increased cellular uptake in bEND.3, PC12, and SH-SY5Y cells, enhanced transport across a bEND.3 monolayer by 3.12-fold, and elevated brain fluorescence accumulation in vivo by 2.58-fold and 1.96-fold compared with free Cy5 and unmodified micelles, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that RVG29-PIC-B13 is a promising strategy for enhancing brain delivery of QC inhibitors and improving therapeutic outcomes in Alzheimer's disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform the development of targeted drug delivery systems for Alzheimer's disease, potentially improving the efficacy of glutaminyl cyclase inhibitors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of animal models and the lack of long-term safety data, but the evidence is strong due to the clear demonstration of enhanced brain delivery and therapeutic effects.

YF17D 载体 狂犬病 疫苗 赋予血清保护和改善的多功能免疫力

Seroprotection and improved polyfunctional immunity conferred by a YF17D-vectored rabies vaccine
作者： Lorena Sánchez-Felipe, Yeranddy Aguiar Alpizar, Sanne Terryn, Guang-Hui Wu, Babs E. Verstrepen, Giresse Tima, Pauline Percier-Lehebel, Gerrit Koopman, Kinga P. Böszörményi, Niraj Mishra
摘要中文翻译摘要 狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,暴露前 狂犬病 策略因需要多次剂量和重复医疗就诊而受到限制。在这里,我们描述了 YF-RabG,一种基于黄热病 17D (YF17D) 疫苗 主链的减毒活 狂犬病 疫苗 候选药物,可稳定表达 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 (RabG)。在多种临床前模型(包括新生小鼠和免疫功能低下小鼠)中,与亲本 YF17D 相比,YF-RabG 表现出减弱的表型和良好的安全性。单次免疫在非人灵长类动物中诱导了强大的体液和细胞介导的 免疫应答。狂犬病病毒 中和抗体滴度很容易超过小鼠、仓鼠和非人类灵长类动物中广泛接受的保护相关性几个数量级。因此,疫苗接种 在一系列严格的小鼠模型中提供针对致命 狂犬病 挑战的保护,维持血清保护抗体水平至少一年,并在使用许可的 狂犬病 疫苗 加强后增加。此外,YF17D 媒介 很容易适应来自远缘狂犬病病毒的糖蛋白,产生针对 Mokola、Lagos 蝙蝠和 Lleida 蝙蝠狂犬病病毒的保护性 疫苗 候选病毒。总的来说,这些发现将 YF-RabG 确定为一种有前途的单剂量 狂犬病 疫苗 候选药物,并强调了 YF17D 平台用于狂犬病病毒 疫苗 开发的多功能性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,暴露前 狂犬病 策略受到多次剂量和重复医疗就诊的需要的限制。
设计与对象
在这里,我们描述了 YF-RabG,一种基于黄热病 17D (YF17D) 疫苗 主链的减毒活 狂犬病 疫苗 候选药物,可稳定表达 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 (RabG)。
核心方法
因此,疫苗接种 在一系列严格的小鼠模型中提供针对致命 狂犬病 挑战的保护,维持血清保护抗体水平至少一年,并在使用许可的 狂犬病 疫苗 加强后增加。
主要结果
单次免疫在非人灵长类动物中诱导了强大的体液和细胞介导的 免疫应答。
解释与创新
狂犬病病毒 中和抗体滴度很容易超过小鼠、仓鼠和非人类灵长类动物中广泛接受的保护相关性几个数量级。
科研与公卫意义
此外,YF17D 媒介 很容易适应来自远缘狂犬病病毒的糖蛋白,产生针对 Mokola、Lagos 蝙蝠和 Lleida 蝙蝠狂犬病病毒的保护性 疫苗 候选病毒。
局限与证据强度
总的来说,这些发现将 YF-RabG 确定为一种有前途的单剂量 狂犬病 疫苗 候选药物,并强调了 YF17D 平台用于狂犬病病毒 疫苗 开发的多功能性。

Seroprotection and improved polyfunctional immunity conferred by a YF17D-vectored rabies vaccine

Authors: Lorena Sánchez-Felipe, Yeranddy Aguiar Alpizar, Sanne Terryn, Guang-Hui Wu, Babs E. Verstrepen, Giresse Tima, Pauline Percier-Lehebel, Gerrit Koopman, Kinga P. Böszörményi, Niraj Mishra
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Rabies remains a major public-health challenge, with pre-exposure vaccination strategies constrained by the need for multiple doses and repeated healthcare visits. Here we describe YF-RabG, a live-attenuated rabies vaccine candidate based on the yellow fever 17D (YF17D) vaccine backbone that stably expresses the rabies virus glycoprotein (RabG). YF-RabG exhibited an attenuated phenotype and favourable safety profile compared with parental YF17D across several preclinical models, including neonatal and immunocompromised mice. A single immunization induced robust humoral and cell-mediated immune responses in non-human primates. Rabies virus-neutralizing antibody titres readily exceeded the widely accepted correlate of protection in mice, hamsters and non-human primates by several orders. Accordingly, vaccination conferred protection against lethal rabies challenge in a range of stringent mouse models, maintained seroprotective antibody levels for at least one year, and increased following a boost using a licensed rabies vaccine. Furthermore, the YF17D vector was readily adapted to accommodate glycoproteins from distant lyssaviruses, generating protective vaccine candidates against Mokola, Lagos bat and Lleida bat lyssaviruses. Collectively, these findings identify YF-RabG as a promising single-dose rabies vaccine candidate and highlight the versatility of the YF17D platform for lyssavirus vaccine development.

Rabies remains a major public-health challenge, with pre-exposure vaccination strategies constrained by the need for multiple doses and repeated healthcare visits.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

使用灭活的 狂犬病 疫苗 对所有三个鳍足类家族进行三年一次的 疫苗接种 评估

Evaluation of all three pinniped families to triannual vaccination with an inactivated rabies vaccine
作者： Ilse Jenkinson, Chris M. Perkins, Drienie D. Claassen, Mmantshuruge J. Miyen, Paolo Martelli, Brett R. Gardner
摘要中文翻译最近,狂犬病病毒 的 暴发 已被确定为南部非洲海岸线海角海豹(Arctocephalus pusillus pusillus)中的新流行病。这标志着首次在海洋哺乳动物中大规模检测到 狂犬病病毒,并引发了对其他哺乳动物和人类潜在 传播 的严重担忧。人们越来越担心该病毒跨越国家和大陆边界传播的能力,可能会在亚南极地区发生 跨物种溢出 事件。野生种群的 疫苗接种 将是控制该 暴发 的基石。本研究调查了香港海洋公园公司 (OPC) 饲养的鳍足类动物是否将 免疫应答 发展为市售的灭活 狂犬病 疫苗(Rabisin ® 和 Nobivac ® )。代表所有三个鳍足类家族的 16 只鳍足类动物,包括加州海狮 (Zalophus californianus)、太平洋海象 (Odobenus rosmarus)、斑海豹 (Phoca vitulina) 和斑海豹 (Phoca largha),每三年接受 1 mL 剂量的灭活注射剂 疫苗。对常规健康检查期间收集的五年历史血清样本进行分析,以评估 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的持续时间。抗体滴度表明所有个体在 疫苗接种 后均发生血清转化并保持血清阳性。尽管在三年间隔内给予第二次 疫苗 剂量之前抗体滴度下降,但所有记录值仍高于 0.5 IU/L 阈值,被认为表明 免疫应答 至 狂犬病 疫苗接种 具有足够的(假定保护性)。该研究得出的结论是,胃肠外注射剂 疫苗 对于所研究物种的所有三个鳍足科家族来说都是安全的,并且单剂量可提供约三年的保护性免疫力,之后推荐使用加强剂 疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
最近,狂犬病病毒 的 暴发 已被确定为南部非洲海岸线海角海狗(Arctocephalus pusillus pusillus)中的一种新流行病。这标志着首次在海洋哺乳动物中大规模检测到 狂犬病病毒,并引发了对其他哺乳动物和人类潜在 传播 的严重担忧。
设计与对象
本研究调查了香港海洋公园公司 (OPC) 饲养的鳍足类动物是否将 免疫应答 发展为市售的灭活 狂犬病 疫苗(Rabisin ® 和 Nobivac ® )。代表所有三个鳍足类家族的 16 只鳍足类动物,包括加州海狮 (Zalophus californianus)、太平洋海象 (Odobenus rosmarus)、斑海豹 (Phoca vitulina) 和斑海豹 (Phoca largha),每三年接受 1 mL 剂量的灭活注射剂 疫苗。
核心方法
对常规健康检查期间收集的五年历史血清样本进行分析,以评估 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的持续时间。
主要结果
抗体滴度表明所有个体在 疫苗接种 后均发生血清转化并保持血清阳性。尽管在三年间隔内给予第二次 疫苗 剂量之前抗体滴度下降,但所有记录值仍高于 0.5 IU/L 阈值,被认为表明 免疫应答 至 狂犬病 疫苗接种 具有足够的(假定保护性)。
解释与创新
该研究得出的结论是,胃肠外注射剂 疫苗 对于所研究物种的所有三个鳍足科家族来说都是安全的,并且单剂量可提供约三年的保护性免疫力,之后推荐使用加强剂 疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,三年一次的 狂犬病 与灭活的 狂犬病 疫苗 可能是控制鳍足类种群中 狂犬病 暴发 的可行策略,这可能有助于防止其他哺乳动物和人类感染 传播。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告 疫苗 在三年间隔之后的长期安全性或有效性,也没有提供用于测量抗体滴度的具体测定的详细信息。由于依赖历史血清样本且缺乏详细的随访数据,证据强度中等。

Evaluation of all three pinniped families to triannual vaccination with an inactivated rabies vaccine

Authors: Ilse Jenkinson, Chris M. Perkins, Drienie D. Claassen, Mmantshuruge J. Miyen, Paolo Martelli, Brett R. Gardner
Original abstract (English)
A recent outbreak of rabies virus has been identified as an emerging epidemic among Cape fur seals ( Arctocephalus pusillus pusillus ) along the southern African coastline. This marks the first large-scale detection of rabies virus in marine mammals and raises significant concerns about potential transmission to other mammals and humans. There is also growing apprehension about the virus’s capacity to spread across national and continental borders, with a potential spillover event occurring into the sub-Antarctic region. Vaccination of wild populations would be a cornerstone to controlling this outbreak. This study investigated whether pinnipeds housed at Ocean Park Corporation (OPC) in Hong Kong developed an immune response to a commercially available inactivated rabies vaccine (Rabisin ® and Nobivac ® ). Sixteen pinnipeds, representing all three pinniped family groups including Californian sea lion ( Zalophus californianus ), Pacific walrus ( Odobenus rosmarus ), Harbor seal ( Phoca vitulina ), and Spotted seal ( Phoca largha ), received a 1 mL dose of an inactivated parenteral vaccine every three years. Historical serum samples collected over five years during routine health examinations were analyzed to assess the duration of the vaccine-induced immune response. The antibody titers indicated that all individuals seroconverted and remained seropositive following vaccination. Although antibody titers declined before administration of the second vaccine dose at the three-year interval, all recorded values remained above the 0.5 IU/L threshold considered indicative of an adequate (presumed protective) immune response to rabies vaccination. The study concluded that the parenteral vaccine was safe for use across all three pinniped families in the species studied and that a single dose provided protective immunity for approximately three years, after which booster vaccination was recommended.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A recent outbreak of rabies virus has been identified as an emerging epidemic among Cape fur seals (Arctocephalus pusillus pusillus) along the southern African coastline. This marks the first large-scale detection of rabies virus in marine mammals and raises significant concerns about potential transmission to other mammals and humans.
Design and population
This study investigated whether pinnipeds housed at Ocean Park Corporation (OPC) in Hong Kong developed an immune response to a commercially available inactivated rabies vaccine (Rabisin ® and Nobivac ® ). Sixteen pinnipeds, representing all three pinniped family groups including Californian sea lion (Zalophus californianus), Pacific walrus (Odobenus rosmarus), Harbor seal (Phoca vitulina), and Spotted seal (Phoca largha), received a 1 mL dose of an inactivated parenteral vaccine every three years.
Core methods
Historical serum samples collected over five years during routine health examinations were analyzed to assess the duration of the vaccine-induced immune response.
Main results
The antibody titers indicated that all individuals seroconverted and remained seropositive following vaccination. Although antibody titers declined before administration of the second vaccine dose at the three-year interval, all recorded values remained above the 0.5 IU/L threshold considered indicative of an adequate (presumed protective) immune response to rabies vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concluded that the parenteral vaccine was safe for use across all three pinniped families in the species studied and that a single dose provided protective immunity for approximately three years, after which booster vaccination was recommended.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that triannual vaccination with an inactivated rabies vaccine could be a viable strategy for controlling rabies outbreaks in pinniped populations, which may help prevent transmission to other mammals and humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the long-term safety or efficacy of the vaccine beyond the three-year interval, nor does it provide details on the specific assays used to measure antibody titers. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on historical serum samples and the lack of detailed follow-up data.

赞比亚 Mungwi 区狗主人的社会经济因素与 狂犬病 相关知识、态度和实践之间的关联

Association between socioeconomic factors and rabies-related knowledge, attitudes and practices among dog owners in Mungwi District, Zambia
作者： Sydney Kalenga, Liywalii Mataa, Mulenga Malata, Benjamin Mubemba
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是许多低收入和中等收入国家的重要公共卫生问题,其中包括赞比亚,家犬是人类 狂犬病 传播 的主要来源。本研究评估了赞比亚 Mungwi 区选定高风险社区的注册狗主人的社会经济特征与 狂犬病 相关知识、态度和预防措施之间的关联。一项分析性横断面研究在 162 名注册狗主人中进行,使用预先测试的结构化问卷,通过面对面访谈进行。使用描述性统计和多变量线性回归分析来评估社会经济特征与知识、态度、实践和整体 KAP 分数之间的关联。在 162 名受访者中,60.5% 的人有较高的知识,39.5% 的人有良好的态度,26.5% 的人表示有良好的预防措施。大多数受访者正确地将狗咬伤视为 狂犬病 传播 的主要途径,并将狗 疫苗接种 视为重要的预防措施。然而,对社区 狂犬病 控制活动的参与仍然有限,一些受访者报告说,在被狗咬伤后,他们依赖传统的 狂犬病。多变量分析显示了特定领域的关联。高等教育与较高的知识、态度和整体 KAP 得分相关,而较高的收入则与较高的知识、态度、实践和整体 KAP 得分相关。就业状况与知识呈负相关,但与态度和总体 KAP 得分呈正相关,并且与调整后的预防实践得分不显着相关。居住地类型与知识、预防实践和总体 KAP 相关,农村受访者的知识得分较低,农村和城郊受访者的预防实践和总体 KAP 得分均低于城市受访者。在选定的高风险社区中注册的狗主人中,社会经济和地理特征与 狂犬病 相关 KAP 存在差异相关。研究结果表明,良好的知识和态度并不总是符合理想的预防措施,特别是在农村和低收入受访者中。这些发现可能会为有针对性的 狂犬病 预防策略提供信息,而横截面设计意味着观察到的关联不应被解释为因果关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是许多低收入和中等收入国家的重要公共卫生问题,其中包括赞比亚,家犬是人类 狂犬病 传播 的主要来源。
设计与对象
一项分析性横断面研究在 162 名注册狗主人中进行,使用预先测试的结构化问卷,通过面对面访谈进行。
核心方法
使用描述性统计和多变量线性回归分析来评估社会经济特征与知识、态度、实践和整体 KAP 分数之间的关联。
主要结果
在 162 名受访者中,60.5% 的人有较高的知识,39.5% 的人有良好的态度,26.5% 的人表示有良好的预防措施。
解释与创新
高等教育与较高的知识、态度和整体 KAP 得分相关,而较高的收入则与较高的知识、态度、实践和整体 KAP 得分相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,良好的知识和态度并不总是符合理想的预防措施,特别是在农村和低收入受访者中。
局限与证据强度
这些发现可能会为有针对性的 狂犬病 预防策略提供信息,而横截面设计意味着观察到的关联不应被解释为因果关系。

Association between socioeconomic factors and rabies-related knowledge, attitudes and practices among dog owners in Mungwi District, Zambia

Authors: Sydney Kalenga, Liywalii Mataa, Mulenga Malata, Benjamin Mubemba
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains an important public health concern in many low- and middle-income countries, including Zambia, where domestic dogs are the principal source of human rabies transmission. This study assessed the association between socioeconomic characteristics and rabies-related knowledge, attitudes, and preventive practices among registered dog owners in selected high-risk communities of Mungwi District, Zambia. An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted among 162 registered dog owners using a pretested structured questionnaire administered through face-to-face interviews. Descriptive statistics and multivariable linear regression analyses were used to assess associations between socioeconomic characteristics and knowledge, attitude, practice, and overall KAP scores. Of the 162 respondents, 60.5% had high knowledge, 39.5% had favourable attitudes, and 26.5% reported desirable preventive practices. Most respondents correctly identified dog bites as the principal route of rabies transmission and recognised dog vaccination as an important preventive measure. However, participation in community rabies-control activities remained limited, and some respondents reported reliance on traditional treatment following dog bites. Multivariable analyses showed domain-specific associations. Higher education was associated with higher knowledge, attitude, and overall KAP scores, while higher income was associated with higher knowledge, attitude, practice, and overall KAP scores. Employment status was negatively associated with knowledge but positively associated with attitude and overall KAP scores, and was not significantly associated with preventive-practice scores after adjustment. Residence type was associated with knowledge, preventive practices, and overall KAP, with rural respondents having lower knowledge scores and both rural and peri-urban respondents having lower preventive-practice and overall KAP scores than urban respondents. Socioeconomic and geographic characteristics were differentially associated with rabies-related KAP among registered dog owners in the selected high-risk communities. The findings indicate that favourable knowledge and attitudes did not consistently correspond with desirable preventive practices, particularly among rural and lower-income respondents. These findings may inform targeted rabies-prevention strategies, while the cross-sectional design means that the observed associations should not be interpreted as causal.

塞内加尔 4S 网络内基于社区的综合 监测 的评估:对早期发现流行病和人畜共患事件的影响

Evaluation of integrated community-based surveillance within the 4S network in Senegal: implications for early detection of epidemic and zoonotic events
作者： Mame Yacine, Anankpo Gildas YAHOUEDO, Rokhaya Diop, Hamidou Thiam, Mam Coumba, Rabiétou LY1, Abdoulaye Sam, Alioune Sylla, Boly Diop, Christine M Halleux
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 基于社区的 监测 融入 4S 网络(塞内加尔的综合症 监测 系统)是一种创新方法,旨在通过社区利益相关者的积极参与,加强对具有流行潜力的疾病、人畜共患病和异常事件的早期发现和报告。该战略在塞内加尔的三个地点进行了试点:Darou Salam(Pikine)、Agnam Civol(Matam)和 Dalaba(Kédougou),已证明其在动员社区参与者、补充现有卫生系统以及检测人类和动物健康事件方面的有效性。方法 评估结合了对 83 个社区利益相关者和医疗保健专业人员的定量调查和包括 44 次半结构化访谈和 9 个焦点小组的定性研究。对收集的定量数据和社区利益相关者发布的警报数据库进行了描述性分析。对定性数据进行了主题分析,以将定量结果结合起来。结果结果显示,社区参与度很高,综合社区 监测 和警报委员会 (CVAC.i) 得到重振,健康指标的检测得到改善,包括孕产妇死亡、疑似麻疹、狂犬病 和出血热。评估期间,该系统共报告了 179 条警报,其中 88.3% 得到了医疗保健专业人员的验证。然而,物流、人力、数字和财务方面的限制仍然存在,引发了人们对该模型的大规模可行性和长期可持续性的质疑。结论 塞内加尔基于社区的 监测 说明了参与性跨部门方法对于加强卫生系统复原力的重要性。其长期可持续性将取决于创新的融资机制、CVAC 角色的正式化以及可持续的跨部门合作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于社区的 监测 融入塞内加尔 4S 网络的有效性,通过社区利益相关者的积极参与,及早发现具有流行潜力的疾病、人畜共患病和异常事件。
设计与对象
该评估在塞内加尔的三个地点进行:Darou Salam (Pikine)、Agnam Civol (Matam) 和 Dalaba (Kédougou),通过定量和定性方法涉及 83 个社区利益相关者和医疗保健专业人员。
核心方法
该评估结合了定量调查和定性研究,包括 44 次半结构化访谈和 9 个焦点小组,以评估社区参与度和健康指标检测。
主要结果
结果显示,社区参与度很高,对孕产妇死亡、疑似麻疹、狂犬病 和出血热等健康指标的检测有所改善,并报告了 179 个警报,其中 88.3% 得到了医疗保健专业人员的验证。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明了参与性跨部门方法对于加强卫生系统复原力的重要性,该系统的长期可持续性取决于创新的融资机制和可持续的跨部门合作。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为其他地区实施类似的基于社区的 监测 系统提供参考,加强疾病和人畜共患病的早期发现和报告,并提高卫生系统的复原力。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括后勤、人力、数字和财务限制,这引发了对该模型的大规模可行性和长期可持续性的质疑。

Evaluation of integrated community-based surveillance within the 4S network in Senegal: implications for early detection of epidemic and zoonotic events

Authors: Mame Yacine, Anankpo Gildas YAHOUEDO, Rokhaya Diop, Hamidou Thiam, Mam Coumba, Rabiétou LY1, Abdoulaye Sam, Alioune Sylla, Boly Diop, Christine M Halleux
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Community-based surveillance integrated into the 4S network (Syndromic Surveillance System in Senegal) is an innovative approach designed to strengthen the early detection and reporting of diseases with epidemic potential, zoonoses, and unusual events through the active involvement of community stakeholders. Piloted in Senegal across three sites, Darou Salam (Pikine), Agnam Civol (Matam), and Dalaba (Kédougou), this strategy has proven its effectiveness in mobilizing community actors, complementing existing health systems, and detecting human and animal health events. Method The evaluation combined quantitative surveys of 83 community stakeholders and healthcare professionals with qualitative research comprising 44 semi-structured interviews and 9 focus groups. A descriptive analysis was conducted on the collected quantitative data and the database of alerts issued by community stakeholders. A thematic analysis was conducted on the qualitative data to contextualize the quantitative results. Results The results show strong community engagement, a revitalisation of the Integrated Community Surveillance and Alert Committees (CVAC.i), and improved detection of health indicators, including maternal deaths, suspected measles, rabies, and haemorrhagic fevers. The system facilitated the reporting of 179 alerts during the evaluation period, of which 88.3% were verified by healthcare professionals. However, logistical, human, digital, and financial constraints persist, raising questions about the model’s large-scale feasibility and longer-term sustainability. Conclusion The Community-based surveillance in Senegal illustrates the importance of a participatory, cross-sectoral approach to strengthening health system resilience. Its long-term sustainability will depend on innovative funding mechanisms, the formalization of the CVACs’ role, and sustainable cross-sectoral collaboration.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of community-based surveillance integrated into the 4S network in Senegal for early detection of diseases with epidemic potential, zoonoses, and unusual events through the active involvement of community stakeholders.
Design and population
The evaluation was conducted across three sites in Senegal: Darou Salam (Pikine), Agnam Civol (Matam), and Dalaba (Kédougou), involving 83 community stakeholders and healthcare professionals through quantitative and qualitative methods.
Core methods
The evaluation combined quantitative surveys and qualitative research, including 44 semi-structured interviews and 9 focus groups, to assess community engagement and the detection of health indicators.
Main results
The results showed strong community engagement, improved detection of health indicators such as maternal deaths, suspected measles, rabies, and haemorrhagic fevers, and the reporting of 179 alerts, 88.3% of which were verified by healthcare professionals.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the importance of a participatory, cross-sectoral approach to strengthening health system resilience, with the system’s long-term sustainability depending on innovative funding mechanisms and sustainable cross-sectoral collaboration.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the implementation of similar community-based surveillance systems in other regions, enhancing early detection and reporting of diseases and zoonoses, and improving health system resilience.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include logistical, human, digital, and financial constraints, which raise questions about the model’s large-scale feasibility and longer-term sustainability.

克什米尔山谷三级抗 狂犬病 诊所就诊的动物咬伤病例的流行病学模式和分布:基于记录的回顾性研究

Epidemiological pattern and distribution of animal-bite cases presenting to a tertiary anti-rabies clinic in Kashmir valley: a retrospective record-based study
作者： Saba Shaheen, Amrit Sudershan, S. Muhammad Salim Khan, Seerat Sultan, Agar Chander Pushap, Shaista Jan, Feroze Ahmed Dar, Kouser Sideeq Lone, Srishty Sudershan, Showkat Ahmad Malik
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 仍然是全球范围内的一个重要公共卫生问题,特别是在印度,死亡率 发病率和社会经济影响仍然很高。尽管 暴露后预防 (PEP) 有效,但由于报告不足和医疗保健服务有限,病例常常未被报告或管理不善。在查谟和克什米尔克什米尔地区,狗咬伤事件显着增加,但有关其他类型动物咬伤和趋势的数据有限。本研究旨在对克什米尔山谷的动物咬伤事件进行全面分析,包括涉及的动物类型、暴露部位和受伤严重程度。该研究旨在解决数据差距和漏报问题,以改善公共卫生应对措施和预防措施。使用斯利那加 Shri Maharaja Hari Singh (SMHS) 医院 Anti-狂犬病 诊所 (ARC) 2023 年的数据进行了一项基于回顾性记录的研究。数据手动复制并转录到 Microsoft Excel 2021 中进行分析。使用描述性统计技术来评估人口趋势、就诊的动物咬伤病例以及动物咬伤的特征。该研究记录了 8,269 起动物咬伤案例,其中以男性(73.12%)和 25-54 岁成年人(60.23%)为主。狗是最常见的咬伤源(5,512 例),其次是猫(2,318 例)。大多数咬伤发生在下肢(4,758 例),其中 3 类伤害最常见(72.58%)。该研究表明,克什米尔山谷的动物咬伤负担很重,其中狗是主要来源,三类伤害占大多数。有效措施应包括通过捕捉和绝育计划定期控制流浪狗数量,开展避免流浪狗的社区教育,加强针对流浪狗的 疫苗接种 计划,以及改善废物管理以减少流浪狗的吸引力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是全球范围内的一个重要公共卫生问题,特别是在印度,死亡率 发病率和社会经济影响仍然很高。
设计与对象
使用斯利那加 Shri Maharaja Hari Singh (SMHS) 医院 Anti-狂犬病 诊所 (ARC) 2023 年的数据进行了一项基于回顾性记录的研究。
核心方法
使用描述性统计技术来评估人口趋势、就诊的动物咬伤病例以及动物咬伤的特征。
主要结果
该研究记录了 8,269 起动物咬伤案例,其中以男性(73.12%)和 25-54 岁成年人(60.23%)为主。
解释与创新
该研究表明,克什米尔山谷的动物咬伤负担相当重,其中狗是主要咬伤源,而三类伤害占大部分接触伤害。
科研与公卫意义
有效措施应包括通过捕捉和绝育计划定期控制流浪狗数量、避免流浪狗的社区教育、加强针对流浪狗的疫苗接种举措以及改进废物管理以减少流浪狗的吸引力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Epidemiological pattern and distribution of animal-bite cases presenting to a tertiary anti-rabies clinic in Kashmir valley: a retrospective record-based study

Authors: Saba Shaheen, Amrit Sudershan, S. Muhammad Salim Khan, Seerat Sultan, Agar Chander Pushap, Shaista Jan, Feroze Ahmed Dar, Kouser Sideeq Lone, Srishty Sudershan, Showkat Ahmad Malik
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains a critical public health issue globally, particularly in India where high mortality rates and socio-economic impacts persist. Despite effective post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), cases often go unreported or poorly managed due to underreporting and limited healthcare access. In the Kashmir region of Jammu & Kashmir, there has been a notable increase in dog bites, but data on other types of animal bites and trends is limited. This study aims to provide a comprehensive analysis of animal bite incidents in the Kashmir Valley, including the types of animals involved, the sites of exposure, and the severity of injuries. The research seeks to address data gaps and underreporting issues to improve public health responses and preventive measures. A retrospective record based study was conducted using data from the Anti-Rabies Clinic (ARC) at Shri Maharaja Hari Singh (SMHS) Hospital, Srinagar, for the year 2023. Data was hand-copied and transcribed into Microsoft Excel 2021 for analysis. Descriptive statistical techniques were used to assess demographic trends, clinic-attending animal-bite case, and characteristics of animal bites. The study recorded 8,269 animal bite cases, with a predominance of males (73.12%) and adults aged 25–54 years (60.23%). Dogs were the most frequent source of bites (5,512 cases), followed by cats (2,318 cases). The majority of bites were on the lower limbs (4,758 cases), with Category 3 injuries being the most common (72.58%). The study demonstrates a substantial burden of animal bites in the Kashmir Valley, with dogs being the predominant source and Category III injuries representing the majority of exposures.. Effective measures should include regular stray dog population control through capture and sterilization programs, community education on avoiding stray dogs, enhanced vaccination initiatives for stray populations, and improved waste management to reduce stray dog attraction.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a critical public health issue globally, particularly in India where high mortality rates and socio-economic impacts persist.
Design and population
A retrospective record based study was conducted using data from the Anti-Rabies Clinic (ARC) at Shri Maharaja Hari Singh (SMHS) Hospital, Srinagar, for the year 2023.
Core methods
Descriptive statistical techniques were used to assess demographic trends, clinic-attending animal-bite case, and characteristics of animal bites.
Main results
The study recorded 8,269 animal bite cases, with a predominance of males (73.12%) and adults aged 25–54 years (60.23%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates a substantial burden of animal bites in the Kashmir Valley, with dogs being the predominant source and Category III injuries representing the majority of exposures.
Scientific and public-health significance
Effective measures should include regular stray dog population control through capture and sterilization programs, community education on avoiding stray dogs, enhanced vaccination initiatives for stray populations, and improved waste management to reduce stray dog attraction.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞的可视化与形态学分析

Visualization and Morphological Analysis of Individual Oligodendrocytes in the Mouse White Matter.
作者： Osanai Y, Looprasertkul S, Battulga B, Yamazaki R, Ohno N
摘要中文翻译少突胶质细胞形成髓鞘对于调节传导速度和维持正常脑功能至关重要。为了确保对如感觉刺激和学习等经验的准确信息处理,少突胶质细胞会调整其数量和形态。此外,少突胶质细胞的形态也会随着衰老和神经退行性疾病的发生而发生变化。因此,对少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析对于理解我们的大脑在这些条件下的变化至关重要。本文描述了在光学显微镜水平上对小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞进行标记和形态学分析的方法。PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP和PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP小鼠使我们能够可视化和分析晚期出生或早期出生的少突胶质细胞的形态。此外,使用减弱的狂犬病病毒表达GFP的稀疏少突胶质细胞标记方法,可以在无需转基因动物的情况下,对各种脑白质区域中的单个少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析。此外,与厚组织免疫染色相结合,可以识别标记的少突胶质细胞和髓鞘,以及它们与神经元轴突的相互作用。这些方法适用于揭示少突胶质细胞如何根据环境刺激或病理条件调整其形态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解少突胶质细胞如何适应其形态以响应环境刺激或病理条件,因为少突胶质细胞的髓磷脂形成对于调节传导速度和正常的大脑功能至关重要。少突胶质细胞调整其数量和形态,以确保响应感官刺激和学习等体验而进行准确的信息处理。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP 和 PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP 小鼠来可视化和分析晚生或早生的少突胶质细胞形态。用表达 GFP 的减毒 狂犬病病毒 标记稀疏少突胶质细胞,无需转基因动物即可对各个脑白质区域中的单个少突胶质细胞进行可视化和形态学分析。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用表达 GFP 的减毒 狂犬病病毒 标记单个少突胶质细胞,并将其与厚组织中的免疫染色相结合,以识别标记的少突胶质细胞和髓鞘,以及它们与神经元轴突的相互作用。
主要结果
该研究直接观察了在光学显微镜水平上可视化和分析小鼠白质中单个少突胶质细胞形态的能力。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明这些方法适用于揭示少突胶质细胞如何根据环境刺激或病理条件调整其形态。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助理解少突胶质细胞如何适应其形态以响应环境刺激或病理条件,这可能对神经科学研究和神经系统疾病的研究产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅基于抽象信息,这限制了分析的深度。由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,研究结果的可信度较低。

Visualization and Morphological Analysis of Individual Oligodendrocytes in the Mouse White Matter.

Authors: Osanai Y, Looprasertkul S, Battulga B, Yamazaki R, Ohno N
Original abstract (English)
Myelin formation by oligodendrocytes is essential for the regulation of the conduction velocity and proper brain function. To ensure accurate information processing in response to experiences such as sensory stimuli and learning, oligodendrocytes adjust their number and morphology. In addition, oligodendrocyte morphology changes with senescence and in the presence of neurodegenerative diseases. Thus, visualizing oligodendrocytes and analyzing their morphology is crucial for understanding how our brains change under such conditions. Herein, we describe the methods for labeling and analyzing the morphologies of individual oligodendrocytes in mouse white matter at the light microscopic level. PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP and PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP mice enable us to visualize and analyze later-born or early-born oligodendrocyte morphology. In addition, sparse oligodendrocyte labeling with attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP enables the visualization and morphological analysis of individual oligodendrocytes in various brain white matter regions without the need for transgenic animals. Furthermore, the combination with immunostaining in thick tissues enables the identification of labeled oligodendrocytes and myelin sheaths, as well as their interactions with neuronal axons. These methods are suitable for revealing how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies depending on environmental stimuli or pathological conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies in response to environmental stimuli or pathological conditions, as myelin formation by oligodendrocytes is essential for the regulation of conduction velocity and proper brain function. Oligodendrocytes adjust their number and morphology to ensure accurate information processing in response to experiences such as sensory stimuli and learning.
Design and population
The study involved the use of PDGFRa-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP and PLP-CreERT2:Tau-mGFP mice to visualize and analyze later-born or early-born oligodendrocyte morphology. Sparse oligodendrocyte labeling with attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP enabled the visualization and morphological analysis of individual oligodendrocytes in various brain white matter regions without the need for transgenic animals.
Core methods
The methods included labeling individual oligodendrocytes using attenuated rabies virus expressing GFP and combining this with immunostaining in thick tissues to identify labeled oligodendrocytes and myelin sheaths, as well as their interactions with neuronal axons.
Main results
The study directly observed the ability to visualize and analyze the morphologies of individual oligodendrocytes in mouse white matter at the light microscopic level.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the suitability of these methods for revealing how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies depending on environmental stimuli or pathological conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the understanding of how oligodendrocytes adapt their morphologies in response to environmental stimuli or pathological conditions, which may have implications for neuroscience research and the study of neurological disorders.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on abstract-only information, which limits the depth of analysis. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analyses.
