小白蛋白中间神经元电路通过 cat V1 中的超经典环绕增强方向调整

Parvalbumin interneuron circuit sharpens orientation tuning via the extraclassical surround in cat V1
作者： Andrzej T. Foik, Yong-Jun Liu, Huan-Jun Shao, Georgina Lean, Li I. Zhang, Leo R. Scholl, Roberto Jappelli, David C. Lyon
摘要中文翻译表达小白蛋白(PV)的抑制性神经元是皮质增益控制的核心,但光遗传学研究对于它们是否会增强方向调节或仅仅重新调整反应存在分歧。由于这项工作几乎完全是在啮齿动物身上进行的,这些动物缺乏方向柱和方向匹配的超古典环绕声,因此环绕电路的贡献尚未经过测试。在这里,我们将细胞类型特异性 狂犬病 病毒追踪与光遗传学结合起来,激活或抑制猫初级视觉皮层中的 PV 回路,并测量单神经元方向调整,以限制经典感受野或延伸到超经典周围的刺激。激活 PV 电路会随着经典感受场刺激的照明功率逐渐锐化调谐,但一旦启用环绕声,调谐就会保持不变;相反,抑制它们只会扩大对周围环境刺激的调谐,并使经典感受野调谐保持不变。这种双重解离将 PV 电路置于因果路径中,该路径设置调谐宽度并将环绕声抑制识别为它提供的抑制:在没有招募环绕声的地方,几乎没有 PV 介导的抑制需要消除,而在有这种抑制的地方,消除该抑制会扩大调谐范围。因此,PV 相关的锐化受到招募群体的感受野架构的限制,而不是受到 PV 群体本身的固定属性的限制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究表达小清蛋白(PV)的抑制性神经元在皮质增益控制中的作用,特别是它们是否会增强方向调整或仅仅重新调整反应。背景强调,之前的光遗传学研究在这个问题上产生了相互矛盾的结果。
设计与对象
该研究在猫的初级视觉皮层中进行,利用细胞类型特异性 狂犬病 病毒追踪结合光遗传学来激活或抑制 PV 回路。该研究的重点是测量仅限于经典感受野或延伸到超经典周围环境的刺激的单神经元方向调整。
核心方法
这些方法涉及将细胞类型特异性 狂犬病 病毒追踪与光遗传学相结合,以操纵猫初级视觉皮层中的 PV 回路。针对仅限于经典感受野或延伸到超经典周围环境的刺激,测量单神经元方向调整。
主要结果
激活 PV 电路会逐渐锐化经典感受场刺激的方向调整,但一旦环绕声接合,调整就会保持不变。相反,抑制 PV 电路仅拓宽了对周围环境刺激的调谐,并且对经典感受野调谐没有影响。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PV 电路与设置调谐宽度有因果关系,而环绕抑制是它提供的特定抑制。这项研究引入了对 PV 神经元如何与感受野结构相互作用以影响方向调整的新理解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于理解视觉皮层定向调节的神经机制,这可能对研究神经系统疾病的感觉处理和潜在的治疗干预具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于猫,这可能会影响对其他物种的推广。证据是强有力的,因为它是基于相关模型系统中光伏电路的直接操作和测量。

Parvalbumin interneuron circuit sharpens orientation tuning via the extraclassical surround in cat V1

Authors: Andrzej T. Foik, Yong-Jun Liu, Huan-Jun Shao, Georgina Lean, Li I. Zhang, Leo R. Scholl, Roberto Jappelli, David C. Lyon
Original abstract (English)
Parvalbumin-expressing (PV) inhibitory neurons are central to cortical gain control, yet optogenetic studies disagree on whether they sharpen orientation tuning or merely rescale responses. Because this work has been performed almost exclusively in rodents, which lack orientation columns and an orientation-matched extraclassical surround, the contribution of surround circuitry has remained untested. Here we combine cell-type-specific rabies-virus tracing with optogenetics to activate or suppress PV circuits in cat primary visual cortex, and measure single-neuron orientation tuning for stimuli confined to the classical receptive field or extending into the extraclassical surround. Activating PV circuits sharpened tuning progressively with illumination power for classical-receptive-field stimuli, but left tuning unchanged once the surround was engaged; conversely, suppressing them broadened tuning only for surround-engaging stimuli and left classical-receptive-field tuning unchanged. This double dissociation places PV circuitry in the causal path that sets tuning width and identifies surround suppression as the inhibition it supplies: where the surround is not recruited there is little PV-mediated inhibition to remove, and where it is, removing that inhibition widens tuning. PV-dependent sharpening is therefore bounded by the receptive-field architecture through which the population is recruited, not by a fixed property of the PV population itself.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of parvalbumin-expressing (PV) inhibitory neurons in cortical gain control, specifically whether they sharpen orientation tuning or merely rescale responses. The background highlights that previous optogenetic studies have yielded conflicting results on this matter.
Design and population
The study was conducted in the primary visual cortex of cats, utilizing cell-type-specific rabies-virus tracing combined with optogenetics to activate or suppress PV circuits. The research focused on measuring single-neuron orientation tuning for stimuli confined to the classical receptive field or extending into the extraclassical surround.
Core methods
The methods involved combining cell-type-specific rabies-virus tracing with optogenetics to manipulate PV circuits in the cat primary visual cortex. Single-neuron orientation tuning was measured for stimuli restricted to the classical receptive field or extending into the extraclassical surround.
Main results
Activating PV circuits progressively sharpened orientation tuning for classical-receptive-field stimuli but left tuning unchanged once the surround was engaged. Conversely, suppressing PV circuits broadened tuning only for surround-engaging stimuli and had no effect on classical-receptive-field tuning.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that PV circuitry is causally involved in setting tuning width, with surround suppression being the specific inhibition it provides. This study introduces a novel understanding of how PV neurons interact with receptive-field architecture to influence orientation tuning.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to the understanding of neural mechanisms underlying orientation tuning in the visual cortex, which may have implications for studying sensory processing and potential therapeutic interventions in neurological disorders.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to cats, which may affect generalizability to other species. The evidence is strong, as it is based on direct manipulation and measurement of PV circuits in a relevant model system.

野生动物中大肠杆菌和奇异变形杆菌的四环素抗性表型分布

Distribution of tetracycline-resistant phenotypes in Escherichia coli and Proteus mirabilis isolates from wildlife
作者： Alex Cristian Moza, Iulia-Maria Bucur, A Ivan, I. Nichita, Ingrid Dolores Moldovan, Elöd Gyorgy, Emil Tîrziu
摘要中文翻译野生动物中的抗菌素耐药性 (AMR) 越来越受到人们的重视,这反映了环境中抗菌剂的暴露情况。在野生食肉动物中,红狐(Vulpes vulpes)被认为是监测耐药细菌环境传播的有用哨兵。大肠杆菌和奇异变形杆菌是常见的肠道共生菌,可以获得和转移耐药性决定因素。在罗马尼亚,土霉素作为生物标志物被包含在分发给狐狸种群的口服抗 狂犬病 疫苗 诱饵中,可能有助于这些动物中耐药细菌的选择。本研究旨在评估从罗马尼亚野生红狐中回收的奇异果分离株对四环素的表型抗性,并评估环境暴露于抗 狂犬病 疫苗接种 诱饵中的四环素的潜在影响。根据标准解释标准,使用 Kirby-Bauer 纸片扩散法检查了先前使用 Vitek 2 Compact 系统鉴定的 30 种细菌分离株(包括 20 种大肠杆菌和 10 种奇异变形杆菌菌株)的四环素敏感性。大肠杆菌分离株中,11 株(55%)对四环素耐药,而 9 株(45%)敏感。奇异果分离株(100%)表现出对四环素的抗性。这些发现证实了野生红狐肠道细菌中存在四环素抗性表型。在野生红狐中检测到的四环素抗性奇异奇异果可能反映了内在和环境影响的抗性,可能与 疫苗接种 诱饵中的土霉素暴露有关。对野生动物持续进行的抗菌药物耐药性监测支持“同一个健康”方法来了解环境抗菌药物的传播。

Distribution of tetracycline-resistant phenotypes in Escherichia coli and Proteus mirabilis isolates from wildlife

Authors: Alex Cristian Moza, Iulia-Maria Bucur, A Ivan, I. Nichita, Ingrid Dolores Moldovan, Elöd Gyorgy, Emil Tîrziu
Original abstract (English)
Antimicrobial resistance (AMR) is increasingly recognized in wildlife, reflecting environmental exposure to antimicrobial agents. Among wild carnivores, red foxes (Vulpes vulpes) are considered useful sentinels for monitoring environmental dissemination of resistant bacteria. Escherichia coli and Proteus mirabilis are common intestinal commensals that can acquire and transfer resistance determinants. In Romania, oxytetracycline is included as a biomarker in oral anti-rabies vaccine baits distributed to fox populations, potentially contributing to the selection of resistant bacteria in these animals. This study aimed to evaluate the phenotypic resistance to tetracycline in E. mirabilis isolates recovered from wild red foxes in Romania and to assess the potential influence of environmental exposure to tetracyclines from anti-rabies vaccination baits. Thirty bacterial isolates, including twenty E. coli, respectively ten P. mirabilis strains, previously identified using the Vitek 2 Compact system were examined for tetracycline susceptibility using the Kirby-Bauer disk diffusion method, according to standard interpretative criteria. coli isolates, 11 (55%) were resistant to tetracycline, while 9 (45%) were susceptible. mirabilis isolates (100%) exhibited resistance to tetracycline. These findings confirm the presence of tetracycline-resistant phenotypes among enteric bacteria from wild red foxes. The detection of tetracycline-resistant E. mirabilis in wild red foxes may reflect both intrinsic and environmentally influenced resistance, possibly linked to oxytetracycline exposure from vaccination baits. Ongoing antimicrobial resistance monitoring in wildlife supports the One Health approach to understanding environmental antimicrobial dissemination.

哥斯达黎加首次检测到犬瘟热病毒在斑纹松鼠(Sciurus variegatoides)和豪猪(Coendou mexicanus)中的感染

First detection of canine distemper virus infection in varie¬gated squirrels (Sciurus variegatoides) and porcupines (Coendou mexicanus) in Costa Rica
作者： B. León, C. Wiederkehr, R. Cruz, M. Cordero, A. Chinchilla, T. Solorzano-Scott, L. Brenes, F. Aguilar-Vargas
摘要中文翻译犬瘟热病毒(CDV)是一种多宿主病原体,能够感染多种家养和野生动物,然而,关于其在松鼠和豪猪中的感染报告仍然很少。作为哥斯达黎加野生动物监测项目的一部分,对六只表现出神经系统症状的野生哺乳动物(四只Sciurus variegatoides和两只Coendou mexicanus)进行了评估以排除某种病原体。所有脑组织样本通过直接免疫荧光和RT-PCR检测均未发现该病原体。对豪猪的结膜液和鼻分泌物进行的快速犬瘟热抗原检测呈阳性,随后这些豪猪被安乐死。五只动物因严重的自溶作用而无法进行组织病理学评估;然而,一只松鼠表现出严重的中性粒细胞性肺炎伴有坏死和出血,肺间质中有中度的淋巴浆细胞浸润,支气管上皮细胞中有胞浆内包涵体,中脑区域有局灶性中性粒细胞性浸润。这些发现与CDV感染伴随严重的系统性细菌感染一致。通过RT-PCR和测序确认了其中两只松鼠和两只豪猪感染了CDV。系统发育分析显示,哥斯达黎加的序列分为两个不同的簇:一个簇包含松鼠,另一个簇包含豪猪。所有四个序列都与美国2012–2013年检测到的犬瘟热病毒株有共同祖先,并且与阿根廷2005年检测到的犬瘟热病毒株有较远的亲缘关系。这些病例是全球少数记录的CDV在松鼠和豪猪中的感染案例之一,也是哥斯达黎加首次报告,强调了继续进行分子监测以明确病毒谱系动态和传播途径的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
犬瘟热病毒(CDV)是一种多宿主病原体,能够感染多种家养和野生动物,然而,关于其在松鼠和豪猪中的感染报告仍然很少。
设计与对象
作为哥斯达黎加野生动物监测项目的一部分,对六只表现出神经系统症状的野生哺乳动物(四只Sciurus variegatoides和两只Coendou mexicanus)进行了评估以排除某种病原体。
核心方法
所有脑组织样本通过直接免疫荧光和RT-PCR检测均未发现该病原体。通过RT-PCR和测序确认了其中两只松鼠和两只豪猪感染了CDV。
主要结果
对豪猪的结膜液和鼻分泌物进行的快速犬瘟热抗原检测呈阳性,随后这些豪猪被安乐死。五只动物因严重的自溶作用而无法进行组织病理学评估;然而,一只松鼠表现出严重的中性粒细胞性肺炎伴有坏死和出血,肺间质中有中度的淋巴浆细胞浸润,支气管上皮细胞中有胞浆内包涵体,中脑区域有局灶性中性粒细胞性浸润。
解释与创新
这些发现与CDV感染伴随严重的系统性细菌感染一致。这些病例是全球少数记录的CDV在松鼠和豪猪中的感染案例之一,也是哥斯达黎加首次报告,强调了继续进行分子监测以明确病毒谱系动态和传播途径的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现突显了分子监测在理解CDV谱系动态和传播途径中的重要性,这可以为疾病预防和控制的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告研究的局限性,但这些发现得到了一种动物的分子确认和组织病理学分析的支持,表明结果具有中等程度的可信度。

First detection of canine distemper virus infection in varie¬gated squirrels (Sciurus variegatoides) and porcupines (Coendou mexicanus) in Costa Rica

Authors: B. León, C. Wiederkehr, R. Cruz, M. Cordero, A. Chinchilla, T. Solorzano-Scott, L. Brenes, F. Aguilar-Vargas
Original abstract (English)
Canine distemper virus (CDV) is a multi-host pathogen capable of infecting a wide range of do-mestic and wild mammals, however, reports of infection in squirrels and porcupines remain scarce. As part of the wildlife rabies surveillance programme in Costa Rica, six wild mammals (four Sciurus variegatoides and two Coendou mexicanus) with neurological signs were evaluated to rule out rabies. All brain samples tested negative for rabies by direct immunofluorescence and RT-PCR. Rapid distemper antigen tests of ocular and nasal secretions were positive in both porcupines, which were subsequently euthanised. Advanced autolysis prevented histopathological evaluation in five of the animals; however, one squirrel showed severe neutrophilic pneumonia with necrosis and haemorrhage, a moderate lymphoplasmacytic infiltrate in the pulmonary interstitium, intracytoplasmic inclusion bodies in bronchial epithelium, and a focal neutrophilic infiltrate in the midbrain. The findings were consistent with CDV infection accompanied by severe systemic bacterial infection. CDV was confirmed in two of the four squirrels and in the two porcupines by RT-PCR and sequencing. Phylogenetic analysis revealed that the Costa Rican sequences grouped into two distinct clusters: one containing both squirrels and another with both porcupines. All four sequences shared a common ancestor with CDV strains detected in domestic dogs in the United States (2012–2013), and more distantly with a dog strain from Argentina (2005). These cases represent one of the few documented occurrences of CDV in squirrels and porcupines worldwide and the first reported in Costa Rica, underscoring the need for continued molecular surveillance to clarify lineage dynamics and spillover pathways.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

“一项研究评估视频辅助教学计划对浦那部分农村地区人们有关狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防的知识和实践的影响”。

“A STUDY TO ASSESS THE EFFECT OF A VIDEO ASSISTED TEACHING PROGRAMME ON KNOWLEDGE AND PRACTICE REGARDING DOG BITE MANAGEMENT AND RABIES PREVENTION AMONG PEOPLE IN SELECTED RURAL AREAS OF PUNE”.
作者： Reena Chavan, Shivcharan Singh Gandhar, Omkar Goratkar, Abhay Giram, Vaishnavi Harale
摘要中文翻译简介:狗咬伤是指狗咬伤人或其他动物。连续不止一次的咬伤通常被称为狗攻击,尽管狗的攻击可能包括击倒和抓伤。虽然有些狗咬伤不会造成伤害,但可能会导致感染、毁容、暂时或永久残疾或死亡。另一种类型的狗咬伤是训练有素的狗、小狗和非攻击性玩耍时表现出的“软咬合”。1 研究标题:一项研究评估视频辅助教学项目对浦那选定农村地区人们有关狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防的知识和实践的影响。研究方法:在浦那农村地区的 100 名成年人中进行了一项实验前一组预测后测试研究,以评估视频辅助教学项目在狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防方面的知识和实践的有效性。使用结构化工具收集数据,并使用描述性和推论性统计进行分析。该干预措施提高了参与者的知识和实践,并使用经过验证和可靠的工具确保道德合规性。结果:在 100 名参与者中(73% 年龄在 18.1-30 岁,65% 女性,46% 毕业生,54% 私人工作,88% 农村),71% 的人测试前知识和实践较差(知识平均值 6.6 ± 1.93;实践平均值 3.62 ± 1.55)。测试后,60% 的人知识良好,38% 为平均水平,2% 为差(平均 14.2 ± 3.01),53% 的人实践良好,30% 为中等,17% 为差(平均 7.05 ± 2.07)。改善具有统计显着性(知识 t = 21.05,p = 0.00001;实践 t = 14.29,p = 0.00001),与人口统计数据没有显着关联(p > 0.05)。结论:研究得出结论,视频辅助教学计划可有效提高农村参与者有关狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防的知识和实践。测试后分数显示出显着改善,但与人口统计学变量没有发现显着关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估视频辅助教学计划对浦那选定农村地区人们有关狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防的知识和实践的影响。
设计与对象
一项实验前的单组预测后测试研究在浦那农村地区的 100 名成年人中进行,其中 73% 年龄在 18.1-30 岁,65% 为女性,46% 为毕业生,54% 为私人工作人员,88% 居住在农村地区。
核心方法
使用结构化工具收集数据,并通过描述性和推论性统计进行分析,以评估干预措施的有效性。
主要结果
71% 的参与者测试前知识和实践较差(知识平均值 6.6 ± 1.93;实践平均值 3.62 ± 1.55)。测试后成绩显示出显着提高,60% 的人拥有良好的知识(平均 14.2 ± 3.01),53% 的人拥有良好的实践(平均 7.05 ± 2.07)。
解释与创新
研究得出的结论是,视频辅助教学计划可以有效提高农村参与者有关狗咬伤管理和 狂犬病 预防的知识和实践。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的教育干预措施可以提高农村地区与 狂犬病 预防和狗咬伤管理相关的社区意识和实践。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量有限,且采用预实验设计,可能会影响结果的普适性。然而,这些改进具有统计显着性(知识 t = 21.05,p = 0.00001;实践 t = 14.29,p = 0.00001),并且未发现与人口统计变量存在显着关联。

“A STUDY TO ASSESS THE EFFECT OF A VIDEO ASSISTED TEACHING PROGRAMME ON KNOWLEDGE AND PRACTICE REGARDING DOG BITE MANAGEMENT AND RABIES PREVENTION AMONG PEOPLE IN SELECTED RURAL AREAS OF PUNE”.

Authors: Reena Chavan, Shivcharan Singh Gandhar, Omkar Goratkar, Abhay Giram, Vaishnavi Harale
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: A dog bite is a bite upon a person or other animal by a dog. More than one successive bite is often called a dog attack, although dog attacks can include knock-downs and scratches. Though some dog bites do not result in injury, they can result in infection, disfigurement, temporary or permanent disability, or death. Another type of dog bite is the "soft bite" displayed by well-trained dogs, by puppies, and in non-aggressive play.1Title of the study: A study to assess the effect of a video assisted teaching Programme on knowledge and practice regarding dog bite management and rabies prevention among people in selected rural areas of Pune. Research Methodology: A pre-experimental one-group pretest post-test study was conducted among 100 adults in rural Pune to assess the effectiveness of a Video-Assisted Teaching Programme on knowledge and practice regarding dog bite management and rabies prevention. Data were collected using a structured tool and analyzed with descriptive and inferential statistics. The intervention improved participants’ knowledge and practices, with validated and reliable tools ensuring ethical compliance. Result: Among 100 participants (73% aged 18.1–30 years, 65% female, 46% graduates, 54% private job, 88% rural), pre-test knowledge and practice were poor in 71% (knowledge mean 6.6 ± 1.93; practice mean 3.62 ± 1.55). Post-test, 60% had good knowledge, 38% average, 2% poor (mean 14.2 ± 3.01), and 53% had good practice, 30% moderate, 17% poor (mean 7.05 ± 2.07). Improvements were statistically significant (knowledge t = 21.05, p = 0.00001; practice t = 14.29, p = 0.00001), with no significant association with demographics (p > 0.05). Conclusion: The study concluded that the Video-Assisted Teaching Programme was effective in improving knowledge and practice regarding dog bite management and rabies prevention among rural participants. Post-test scores showed significant improvement, while no significant association was found with demographic variables.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the effect of a video-assisted teaching programme on knowledge and practice regarding dog bite management and rabies prevention among people in selected rural areas of Pune.
Design and population
A pre-experimental one-group pretest post-test study was conducted among 100 adults in rural Pune, with 73% aged 18.1–30 years, 65% female, 46% graduates, 54% private job holders, and 88% residing in rural areas.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured tool and analyzed with descriptive and inferential statistics to evaluate the effectiveness of the intervention.
Main results
Pre-test knowledge and practice were poor in 71% of participants (knowledge mean 6.6 ± 1.93; practice mean 3.62 ± 1.55). Post-test scores showed significant improvement, with 60% having good knowledge (mean 14.2 ± 3.01) and 53% having good practice (mean 7.05 ± 2.07).
Interpretation and novelty
The study concluded that the Video-Assisted Teaching Programme was effective in improving knowledge and practice regarding dog bite management and rabies prevention among rural participants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational interventions can enhance community awareness and practices related to rabies prevention and dog bite management in rural settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a limited sample size and used a pre-experimental design, which may affect the generalizability of the results. However, the improvements were statistically significant (knowledge t = 21.05, p = 0.00001; practice t = 14.29, p = 0.00001), and no significant association was found with demographic variables.

从龙葵中提取生物活性植物化学物质并进行表征:使用计算机方法研究抗狂犬病化合物

Bioactive phytochemical extraction and characterisation from Solanum nigrum: an investigation of antirabies compounds using an in silico approach
作者： Dasan Perumal Pulamai, NPMT Mohamed
摘要中文翻译本研究包括龙葵甲醇提取物的植物化学评价;1.64 % (0.82 g),主要植物化学测试的阳性检出率为 80 %。甲醇和己烷溶剂获得的柱洗脱级分分别以3:7和2:8的比例有效分离化合物。对收集的馏分进行气相色谱质谱研究表明,总共 30 个峰中存在 19 种不同的化合物。鉴定出的主要化合物包括3,3-二甲基哌啶、3-甲基己-2-胺、N-dl-丙氨酰甘氨酸、(S)-(+)-1-环己基乙胺、四三十烷、月桂酰胺、哌嗪、氨基酸及其衍生物。提取物的 2,2-二苯基-2-苦基肼清除生物测定显示出浓度依赖性活性,在 100 μg/mL 时记录的最大值为 68.041 %,抗炎活性为 67.4 %。对目标 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白的 3D 结构研究表明,G 链包含 65.4 Å3 的空腔体积,没有任何隧道,这意味着转运功能较低。发现链 A 具有相对较高的口袋体积(565.1 Å3)以及一个或多个隧道,这表明存在重要的配体结合位点。与 G 链的对接显示,N-dl-丙氨酰甘氨酸具有最高的结合亲和力,对接分数为 -4.86 kcal/mol。另一方面,与A链的对接表明月桂酰胺是两者中最有效的化合物,对接分数为-5.583 kcal/mol。所选分子的结合亲和力表明,两者都可以抑制感染期间病毒的结合能力。目前的结果表明,候选药物的选择和支持植物化合物对抗 狂犬病 有效性的假设可能需要通过体内研究进行额外的探索。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用计算机模拟方法研究抗狂犬病化合物,重点关注龙葵植物化学物质对抗 狂犬病病毒 的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对从龙葵中提取的植物化学化合物进行计算机分析,主要关注 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白及其结构特征。
核心方法
研究人员使用气相色谱-质谱法分析提取的化合物,并进行对接研究以评估它们与 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白的结合亲和力。
主要结果
该分析从 30 个峰中鉴定出 19 种化合物,对接研究表明,N-dl-丙氨酰甘氨酸与 G 链的对接得分为 -4.86 kcal/mol,而月桂酰胺与 A 链的对接得分为 -5.583 kcal/mol。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,某些植物化学物质可能具有潜在的抗狂犬病特性,因为它们与 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白具有显着的结合亲和力。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为进一步的体内研究奠定了基础,以探索这些植物化学物质对抗 狂犬病病毒 感染的有效性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算机分析,可能无法完全反映生物活性,并且由于缺乏实验验证,证据不充分。

Bioactive phytochemical extraction and characterisation from Solanum nigrum: an investigation of antirabies compounds using an in silico approach

Authors: Dasan Perumal Pulamai, NPMT Mohamed
Original abstract (English)
The present study includes the phytochemical evaluation of Solanum nigrum methanol extract; 1.64 % (0.82 g), revealing an 80 % positive detection rate for major phytochemical tests. The column-eluted fractions obtained by methanol and hexane solvents were efficient in separating the compounds with a ratio of 3 : 7 and 2 : 8 respectively. The gas chromatography mass spectrometry study of the collected fractions showed the presence of 19 different compounds from a total of 30 peaks. Major compounds identified included 3,3-dimethylpiperidine, 3-methylhexan-2-amine, N-dl-Alanylglycine, (S)-(+)-1-cyclohexylethylamine, tetratriacontane, lauramide, piperazine, amino acids and their derivatives. A bioassay of the extract for 2,2-diphenyl-2-picryl hydrazyl scavenging showed concentration-dependent activity, recording a maximum of 68.041 % and anti-inflammatory activity of 67.4 % at 100 μg/mL. The 3D structural study of the targeted rabies virus glycoprotein revealed that the G-chain contained a cavity volume of 65.4 Å3 without any tunnels, implying a low transportability function. Chain A was found to have a relatively high pocket volume of 565.1 Å3 along with one or several tunnels, which indicates the existence of significant ligand binding sites. Docking against G-chain revealed that N-dl-Alanylglycine had the highest binding affinity with a docking score of −4.86 kcal/mol. On the other hand, docking against Chain A indicated that lauramide is the most effective compound among the two, with a docking score of −5.583 kcal/mol. The binding affinities of the selected molecules revealed that both could inhibit the binding capacity of the virus during infection. The current results indicate that the selection of candidates and the hypothesis supporting the effectiveness of phytocompounds against rabies might require additional exploration through in vivo research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate antirabies compounds using an in silico approach, focusing on the potential of phytochemicals from Solanum nigrum to combat rabies virus.
Design and population
The study involved an in silico analysis of phytochemical compounds extracted from Solanum nigrum, with the primary focus on the rabies virus glycoprotein and its structural characteristics.
Core methods
The researchers used gas chromatography-mass spectrometry to analyze the extracted compounds and performed docking studies to assess their binding affinity with the rabies virus glycoprotein.
Main results
The analysis identified 19 compounds from 30 peaks, and docking studies showed that N-dl-Alanylglycine had a docking score of −4.86 kcal/mol against the G-chain, while lauramide had a docking score of −5.583 kcal/mol against Chain A.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that certain phytochemicals may have potential antirabies properties, as they demonstrated significant binding affinities with the rabies virus glycoprotein.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further in vivo research to explore the effectiveness of these phytochemicals in combating rabies virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico analysis, which may not fully reflect biological activity, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of experimental validation.

从接种基于羊毛脂的 Rabisin® 疫苗 的新西兰白兔中收集的 疫苗接种 位点的组织病理学结果

Histopathology results of vaccination sites collected from New Zealand white rabbits vaccinated with a lanolin-based Rabisin® vaccine
作者： Leandra Wiid
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在评估缓释油包水乳液 狂犬病 疫苗 是否有可能提供一种安全有效的方法来改善未来犬类 狂犬病 的控制。我们在 16 只新西兰白兔身上评估了单剂量羊毛脂佐剂与市售 狂犬病 疫苗 (Rabisin®) 的安全性和有效性,并与两剂 Rabisin® 进行比较。注射部位的组织病理学评估显示,羊毛脂或 Rabisin® 组仅出现轻微的炎症微观迹象,没有脓肿或肉芽肿形成。从 Rabisin® 组收集的八个样本中,有六个显示出轻度炎症浸润,主要由嗜酸性粒细胞组成,淋巴细胞和浆细胞较少,而其余两个样本则显示出极少的炎症变化或没有炎症变化。在研究的第 89 天,从 Rabisin® 组的一名女性身上采集的活检样本被发现在正常范围内(没有微观病变),而一份样本(来自 21 号男性)在真皮浅层内有一个局部广泛的纤维结缔组织灶,带有少量的嗜酸性粒细胞和一些淋巴细胞和浆细胞。羊毛脂组的八个样品中的三个具有与大多数 Rabisin® 组相似的微观外观,而八个样品中的两个显示出极小的炎症变化或没有炎症变化,另外两个样品仅显示真皮浅层内分散的、少量的嗜酸性粒细胞。从羊毛脂组收集的八个样本中的一个与从 Rabisin® 组收集的八个样本中的六个的结果相似,但淋巴细胞和浆细胞相对较少。羊毛脂组样品中未发现残留矿物油滴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估缓释油包水乳液 狂犬病 疫苗 是否有可能提供一种安全有效的方法来改善未来犬类 狂犬病 的控制。
设计与对象
我们在 16 只新西兰白兔身上评估了单剂量羊毛脂佐剂与市售 狂犬病 疫苗 (Rabisin®) 的安全性和有效性,并与两剂 Rabisin® 进行比较。
核心方法
注射部位的组织病理学评估显示,羊毛脂或 Rabisin® 组仅出现轻微的炎症微观迹象,没有脓肿或肉芽肿形成。
主要结果
从 Rabisin® 组收集的八个样本中,有六个显示出轻度炎症浸润,主要由嗜酸性粒细胞组成,淋巴细胞和浆细胞较少,而其余两个样本则显示出极少的炎症变化或没有炎症变化。
解释与创新
在研究的第 89 天,从 Rabisin® 组的一名女性身上采集的活检样本被发现在正常范围内(没有微观病变),而一份样本(来自 21 号男性)在真皮浅层内有一个局部广泛的纤维结缔组织灶,带有少量的嗜酸性粒细胞和一些淋巴细胞和浆细胞。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,就最小化局部炎症反应而言,基于羊毛脂的佐剂可能是传统 狂犬病 疫苗 制剂的可行替代品。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量较小和使用兔子作为模型的限制,可能无法完全代表犬类 免疫应答。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Histopathology results of vaccination sites collected from New Zealand white rabbits vaccinated with a lanolin-based Rabisin® vaccine

Authors: Leandra Wiid
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to assess whether a slow-release water in-oil emulsion rabies vaccine could potentially provide a safe and effective method to improve canine rabies control in future. We evaluated the safety and efficacy of a single dose of a lanolin-based adjuvant combined with a commercially available rabies vaccine used in canines (Rabisin®), compared to two doses of Rabisin® in 16 New Zealand white rabbits. Histopathology evaluation of the injection sites revealed only mild microscopic signs of inflammation, no abscessation or granuloma formation in either the lanolin or Rabisin® groups. Six of the eight samples collected from the Rabisin® group demonstrated a mild inflammatory infiltrate composed predominantly of eosinophils with fewer lymphocytes and scant plasma cells, whereas the remaining two samples showed minimal to no inflammatory changes. The biopsy sample collected on day 89 of the study from a female in the Rabisin® group was found to be within normal limits (no microscopic lesions), while one sample (from male number 21) had a single focus of locally extensive fibrous connective tissue with scant eosinophils and a few lymphocytes and plasma cells within the superficial dermis. The lanolin group had three of the eight samples with a microscopic appearance similar to the majority of the Rabisin® group, while two of the eight samples showed minimal to no inflammatory changes and two other samples showed only scattered, scant eosinophils within the superficial dermis. One of the eight samples collected from the lanolin group was similar to the findings of six of the eight samples collected from the Rabisin® group but had comparatively fewer lymphocytes and plasma cells. No residual mineral oil droplets were found in the lanolin group samples.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess whether a slow-release water in-oil emulsion rabies vaccine could potentially provide a safe and effective method to improve canine rabies control in future.
Design and population
We evaluated the safety and efficacy of a single dose of a lanolin-based adjuvant combined with a commercially available rabies vaccine used in canines (Rabisin®), compared to two doses of Rabisin® in 16 New Zealand white rabbits.
Core methods
Histopathology evaluation of the injection sites revealed only mild microscopic signs of inflammation, no abscessation or granuloma formation in either the lanolin or Rabisin® groups.
Main results
Six of the eight samples collected from the Rabisin® group demonstrated a mild inflammatory infiltrate composed predominantly of eosinophils with fewer lymphocytes and scant plasma cells, whereas the remaining two samples showed minimal to no inflammatory changes.
Interpretation and novelty
The biopsy sample collected on day 89 of the study from a female in the Rabisin® group was found to be within normal limits (no microscopic lesions), while one sample (from male number 21) had a single focus of locally extensive fibrous connective tissue with scant eosinophils and a few lymphocytes and plasma cells within the superficial dermis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the lanolin-based adjuvant may be a viable alternative to traditional rabies vaccine formulations in terms of minimizing local inflammatory responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and use of rabbits as a model, which may not fully represent canine immune responses. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

📗 主报告：综述 / Primary Reviews

Journal: Biochimie  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-20  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.biochi.2026.11.007  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, S0300-9084(26)00299-3
C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

病毒与载脂蛋白的分子相互作用:证据、机制和治疗意义。

The molecular interactions of viruses with apolipoproteins: Evidence, Mechanisms, and Therapeutic Implications.
作者： Yihan Jiang, Wen Liu, Bing Luo
摘要中文翻译宿主 脂质代谢被病毒广泛劫持以支持其复制周期。载脂蛋白是脂蛋白颗粒的核心蛋白成分,介导脂质转运、受体识别和免疫调节,使其成为多种病毒的关键 宿主 靶点。这篇综述系统地研究了病毒-载脂蛋白相互作用的分子机制,并建立了区分直接和间接作用模式的分类框架。直接相互作用涉及病毒成分和载脂蛋白或其受体之间的物理结合,例如丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)ApoE依赖性脂病毒颗粒组装、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)ApoE介导的进入、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)刺突蛋白与高密度脂蛋白(HDL)相关载脂蛋白的结合以及狂犬病病毒 ApoD-糖蛋白相互作用。间接机制包括病毒通过表观遗传修饰、细胞因子和氧化应激对载脂蛋白表达的调节。我们还比较了 ApoE2、ApoE3 和 ApoE4 亚型对 HCV、人类免疫缺陷病毒 1 型 (HIV-1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 感染易感性和疾病严重程度的不同影响。讨论了临床病理后果,包括肝脂肪变性、动脉粥样硬化(AS)、血脂异常和血栓并发症,并严格评估了针对脂质代谢的治疗策略。最后,我们概述了尚未解决的问题,并建议利用单细胞多组学、空间生物学和类器官模型的未来研究方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病,检查其与载脂蛋白的分子相互作用。核心问题是 狂犬病病毒 如何利用 宿主 脂质代谢和载脂蛋白来支持其复制周期和 发病机制。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对病毒-载脂蛋白相互作用的分子机制的系统分析。它包括区分直接和间接作用模式的分类框架,证据来自 狂犬病病毒、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV)、人类免疫缺陷病毒 1 型 (HIV-1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的研究。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或汇总估计。
共识与结论
综述得出的结论是,狂犬病病毒 通过其糖蛋白直接与载脂蛋白(如 ApoD)相互作用以促进感染。它还强调了病毒通过表观遗传变化、细胞因子和氧化应激调节载脂蛋白表达的间接机制。这些发现为理解载脂蛋白在病毒 发病机制 中的作用建立了一个框架。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了在理解 ApoE 亚型(ApoE2、ApoE3、ApoE4)对 狂犬病病毒 和其他相关病毒的感染易感性和疾病严重程度的差异影响方面存在的差距。此外,狂犬病病毒 影响脂质代谢的机制及其更广泛的临床意义仍有待探索。
科研与实践启示
该评论建议未来的研究应侧重于利用单细胞多组学、空间生物学和类器官模型,以更好地了解 狂犬病病毒 和载脂蛋白之间的分子相互作用。这些方法可以为 狂犬病 疾病的靶向治疗策略的开发提供信息。

The molecular interactions of viruses with apolipoproteins: Evidence, Mechanisms, and Therapeutic Implications.

Authors: Yihan Jiang, Wen Liu, Bing Luo
Original abstract (English)
Host lipid metabolism is extensively hijacked by viruses to support their replication cycle. Apolipoproteins, the core protein components of lipoprotein particles, mediate lipid transport, receptor recognition, and immune regulation, making them key host targets for diverse viruses. This review systematically examines the molecular mechanisms underlying virus-apolipoprotein interactions and establishes a classification framework distinguishing direct from indirect modes of action. Direct interactions involve physical binding between viral components and apolipoproteins or their receptors, exemplified by hepatitis C virus (HCV) ApoE-dependent lipoviroparticle assembly, hepatitis B virus (HBV) ApoE-mediated entry, Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) spike protein binding to high-density lipoprotein (HDL)-associated apolipoproteins, and rabies virus ApoD-glycoprotein interaction. Indirect mechanisms include viral regulation of apolipoprotein expression through epigenetic modifications, cytokines, and oxidative stress. We also compare the differential effects of ApoE2, ApoE3, and ApoE4 subtypes on infection susceptibility and disease severity across HCV, human immunodeficiency virus type 1 (HIV-1), and SARS-CoV-2. The clinical-pathological consequences, including hepatic steatosis, atherosclerosis (AS), dyslipidemia, and thrombotic complications, are discussed, and therapeutic strategies targeting lipid metabolism are critically evaluated. We close by outlining unresolved questions and suggesting future research directions leveraging single-cell multi-omics, spatial biology, and organoid models.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease, examining its molecular interactions with apolipoproteins. The central question is how rabies virus exploits host lipid metabolism and apolipoproteins to support its replication cycle and pathogenesis.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of molecular mechanisms of virus-apolipoprotein interactions. It includes a classification framework distinguishing direct and indirect modes of action, with evidence drawn from studies on rabies virus, hepatitis C virus (HCV), human immunodeficiency virus type 1 (HIV-1), and SARS-CoV-2. The review does not explicitly report meta-analysis or pooled estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that rabies virus interacts directly with apolipoproteins, such as ApoD, through its glycoprotein to facilitate infection. It also highlights indirect mechanisms where the virus modulates apolipoprotein expression via epigenetic changes, cytokines, and oxidative stress. These findings establish a framework for understanding the role of apolipoproteins in viral pathogenesis.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies gaps in understanding the differential effects of ApoE subtypes (ApoE2, ApoE3, ApoE4) on infection susceptibility and disease severity in rabies virus and other related viruses. Additionally, the mechanisms by which rabies virus influences lipid metabolism and its broader clinical implications remain underexplored.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests future research should focus on leveraging single-cell multi-omics, spatial biology, and organoid models to better understand the molecular interactions between rabies virus and apolipoproteins. These approaches could inform the development of targeted therapeutic strategies for rabies disease.

嗜神经病毒引起的行为和认知变化的分子基础。

Molecular basis of behavioral and cognitive changes induced by neurotropic viruses.
作者： Anthony Coleon, Ilona Ronco, Jacob Raber, Abigail O'Niel, Christiane Herden, Branka Horvat, Hervé Bourhy, Guilherme Dias de Melo
摘要中文翻译嗜神经病毒通过多种分子和细胞机制诱导行为和认知改变,其范围超出了简单的神经元损伤。对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV)、伪狂犬病病毒 (PRV)、博尔纳病病毒 (BoDV-1)、SARS-CoV-2、尼帕病毒 (NiV)、西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) 和单纯疱疹病毒 1 (HSV-1) 的比较分析揭示了针对特定神经回路的不同致病策略。虽然以小胶质细胞激活、星形细胞反应和促炎细胞因子产生为特征的神经炎症在 WNV 和 NiV 感染中导致突触和网络功能障碍,但其他机制在不同病原体中占主导地位。RABV 选择性破坏胆碱能、血清素能和谷氨酸/GABA 能途径,同时保留神经元完整性以促进 传播,而 BoDV-1 和 SARS-CoV-2 通过持续的免疫激活和内皮损伤诱导多巴胺能和血清素能功能障碍。此外,肠脑轴和微生物组改变等全身因素(在 SARS-CoV-2 感染中尤其明显)可调节神经递质代谢和神经免疫反应。考虑到在感染过程中观察到的甚至弥漫性神经元损失的情况,行为变化可能更多地是由选择性的、依赖于回路的脆弱性引起的,涉及对情绪、认知和运动控制网络的特定破坏。为了清楚地回顾有关靶向神经元群体改变如何转化为行为表型的知识,我们在这里整合了用作模型的这七种嗜神经病毒的分子、细胞和系统级见解,目的是确定将感染与行为变化和认知功能障碍联系起来的常见途径。了解从急性突触操纵到长期神经免疫失调的这些离散机制对于制定针对感染因子和介导急性紊乱和长期神经后遗症的特定神经网络的治疗策略至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病。该综述的重点是了解 狂犬病病毒 诱导行为和认知变化的分子和细胞机制,重点是有针对性的神经元群体改变及其转化为行为表型。
证据基础与方法
该综述整合了对 狂犬病病毒 (RABV)、伪狂犬病病毒 (PRV)、博尔纳病病毒 (BoDV-1)、SARS-CoV-2、尼帕病毒 (NiV)、西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) 和单纯疱疹病毒 1 (HSV-1) 研究的分子、细胞和系统级见解。它包括对这些病毒的比较分析,以确定将感染与行为改变和认知功能障碍联系起来的常见途径。
共识与结论
狂犬病病毒 选择性破坏胆碱能、血清素能和谷氨酸/GABA 能途径,同时保留神经元完整性以促进 传播。神经炎症,包括小胶质细胞激活、星形细胞反应和促炎细胞因子的产生,会导致西尼罗河病毒和尼瓦病毒感染中的突触和网络功能障碍,但在不同的病原体中,其他机制占主导地位。肠脑轴和微生物组改变等全身因素(在 SARS-CoV-2 感染中尤其明显)可调节神经递质代谢和神经免疫反应。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,虽然神经炎症是某些病毒的一个重要因素,但其他机制在不同的病原体中占主导地位。对于特定神经元群体的改变如何转化为行为表型缺乏详细的了解,并且系统因素在调节这些影响中的作用仍未得到充分探索。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来阐明 狂犬病病毒 和其他嗜神经病毒诱导行为和认知变化的分子和细胞机制。这包括研究肠脑轴和微生物组改变在调节神经免疫反应和神经递质代谢中的作用。

Molecular basis of behavioral and cognitive changes induced by neurotropic viruses.

Authors: Anthony Coleon, Ilona Ronco, Jacob Raber, Abigail O'Niel, Christiane Herden, Branka Horvat, Hervé Bourhy, Guilherme Dias de Melo
Original abstract (English)
Neurotropic viruses induce behavioral and cognitive alterations through diverse molecular and cellular mechanisms that extend beyond simple neuronal damage. Comparative analysis of rabies virus (RABV), pseudorabies virus (PRV), Borna disease virus (BoDV-1), SARS-CoV-2, Nipah virus (NiV), West Nile virus (WNV) and Herpes Simplex virus 1 (HSV-1) reveals distinct pathogenic strategies targeting specific neural circuits. While neuroinflammation characterized by microglial activation, astrocytic responses, and pro-inflammatory cytokine production contributes significantly to synaptic and network dysfunction in WNV and NiV infections, other mechanisms predominate in different pathogens. RABV selectively disrupts cholinergic, serotonergic, and glutamate/GABAergic pathways while preserving neuronal integrity to facilitate transmission, whereas BoDV-1 and SARS-CoV-2 induce dopaminergic and serotonergic dysfunctions through persistent immune activation and endothelial damage. Additionally, systemic factors such as the gut-brain axis and microbiome alterations, particularly evident in SARS-CoV-2 infection, modulate neurotransmitter metabolism and neuroimmune responses. Considering the lack of even diffuse neuronal loss observed during infection, behavioral changes may arise more from selective, circuit-dependent vulnerabilities, involving specific disruptions to emotion, cognition, and motor control networks. To provide a clear review of the knowledge regarding how targeted neuronal population alterations translate into behavioral phenotypes, we here integrate molecular, cellular, and systems-level insights across these seven neurotropic viruses used as models, with the aim of identifying common pathways linking infection to behavioral changes and cognitive dysfunction. Understanding these discrete mechanisms ranging from acute synaptic manipulation to long-lasting neuroimmune dysregulation is essential for developing therapeutic strategies that address both the infectious agent and the specific neural networks mediating acute disturbances and long-term neurological sequelae.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Rabies virus and rabies disease. The review focuses on understanding the molecular and cellular mechanisms by which rabies virus induces behavioral and cognitive changes, with an emphasis on targeted neuronal population alterations and their translation into behavioral phenotypes.
Evidence base and method
The review integrates molecular, cellular, and systems-level insights from studies on rabies virus (RABV), pseudorabies virus (PRV), Borna disease virus (BoDV-1), SARS-CoV-2, Nipah virus (NiV), West Nile virus (WNV), and Herpes Simplex virus 1 (HSV-1). It includes a comparative analysis of these viruses to identify common pathways linking infection to behavioral changes and cognitive dysfunction.
Consensus and conclusions
Rabies virus selectively disrupts cholinergic, serotonergic, and glutamate/GABAergic pathways while preserving neuronal integrity to facilitate transmission. Neuroinflammation, including microglial activation, astrocytic responses, and pro-inflammatory cytokine production, contributes to synaptic and network dysfunction in WNV and NiV infections, but other mechanisms predominate in different pathogens. Systemic factors such as the gut-brain axis and microbiome alterations, particularly evident in SARS-CoV-2 infection, modulate neurotransmitter metabolism and neuroimmune responses.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that while neuroinflammation is a significant factor in some viruses, other mechanisms predominate in different pathogens. There is a lack of detailed understanding of how specific neuronal population alterations translate into behavioral phenotypes, and the role of systemic factors in modulating these effects remains underexplored.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to elucidate the molecular and cellular mechanisms by which rabies virus and other neurotropic viruses induce behavioral and cognitive changes. This includes investigating the role of the gut-brain axis and microbiome alterations in modulating neuroimmune responses and neurotransmitter metabolism.

猫咬伤后无已知合并症的女性发生致命性暴发性多杀性巴氏杆菌脓毒症:病例报告 和文献综述

Fatal Fulminant Pasteurella multocida Sepsis in a Woman Without Known Comorbidities After Cat Bite: A Case Report and Literature Review
作者： Hongbo Liu, Qi Nie, Chao Tu, Zhongwen Tang, Mi Zhou, Jingze Chen, Xionglin Fan, Chaolin Huang
摘要中文翻译多杀性巴氏杆菌是一种常见的猫科动物口腔共生菌,通常在猫咬伤后引起轻度局部感染,但严重的侵袭性疾病很少见,主要见于免疫功能低下的 宿主。我们报告了一例暴发性多杀性巴氏杆菌败血症致死病例,患者为一名 51 岁女性,在被猫咬伤后没有已知的合并症。她出现进行性双侧上肢肿胀,随后在当地诊所接受了 狂犬病 和破伤风免疫预防治疗,但很快出现感染性休克、弥散性血管内凝血 (DIC) 和多器官功能障碍综合征 (MODS)。传统培养结果呈阴性,而在住院第 3 天,伤口液体宏基因组下一代测序 (mNGS) 鉴定出多杀性巴氏杆菌。尽管使用了广谱抗生素、机械通气、血管加压药和持续肾脏替代治疗,她还是在住院第 4 天死亡。该病例表明,多杀性巴氏杆菌可在免疫功能正常的个体中引起暴发性致命性败血症。它强调需要严格遵守当前针对高风险猫咬伤的预防性抗生素指南,并尽早将分子诊断纳入培养阴性、危及生命的感染中。临床医生应尽早开始经验性 治疗,并在治疗窗口较短时密切监测。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 狂犬病病毒 和 狂犬病 疾病,检查猫咬伤后多杀性巴氏杆菌引起严重侵袭性疾病的可能性,特别是在免疫功能正常的个体中。核心问题是在这种情况下,多杀性巴氏杆菌是否会导致致命的败血症。
证据基础与方法
该综述是病例报告和文献综述。其中包括一名 51 岁女性的单一案例研究,她在被猫咬伤后患上致命的多杀性巴氏杆菌败血症,且没有已知的合并症。该综述不报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是报告案例和相关文献。
共识与结论
该病例表明,多杀性巴氏杆菌可在免疫功能正常的个体中引起暴发性、致命性脓毒症,挑战了严重疾病仅限于免疫功能低下的 宿主 的假设。这凸显了将多杀性巴氏杆菌视为猫咬伤后严重感染的潜在病原体的重要性。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于单个 病例报告,这可能无法代表更广泛的趋势。缺乏关于免疫功能正常个体中多杀性巴氏杆菌感染的频率和严重程度的全面数据,并且分子诊断在早期检测中的作用仍未得到充分探索。
科研与实践启示
临床医生应考虑尽早针对高风险猫咬伤启动经验性 治疗,并在传统培养结果呈阴性的情况下整合分子诊断。需要进一步的研究来更好地了解多杀性巴氏杆菌在免疫功能正常的个体中的致病潜力,并完善诊断和 治疗 方案。

Fatal Fulminant Pasteurella multocida Sepsis in a Woman Without Known Comorbidities After Cat Bite: A Case Report and Literature Review

Authors: Hongbo Liu, Qi Nie, Chao Tu, Zhongwen Tang, Mi Zhou, Jingze Chen, Xionglin Fan, Chaolin Huang
Original abstract (English)
Pasteurella multocida is a common feline oral commensal that typically causes mild local infections after cat bites, but severe invasive disease is rare and predominantly reported in immunocompromised hosts. We report a fatal case of fulminant P. multocida sepsis in a 51-year-old woman without known comorbidities following cat bites. She presented with progressive bilateral upper limb swelling, and then received rabies and tetanus immunoprophylaxis at a local clinic, but rapidly developed septic shock, disseminated intravascular coagulation (DIC), and multiple organ dysfunction syndrome (MODS). Conventional cultures were negative, while metagenomic next-generation sequencing (mNGS) of wound fluid identified P. multocida on hospital day 3. Despite broad-spectrum antibiotics, mechanical ventilation, vasopressors, and continuous renal replacement therapy, she died on hospital day 4. This case demonstrates that P. multocida can cause fulminant, fatal sepsis in immunocompetent individuals. It underscores the need for strict adherence to current prophylactic antibiotic guidelines for high-risk cat bites and earlier integration of molecular diagnostics in culture-negative, life-threatening infections. Clinicians should start empirical treatment early and monitor closely when the therapeutic window is short.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus and rabies disease, examining the potential for severe invasive disease caused by Pasteurella multocida following cat bites, particularly in immunocompetent individuals. The central question is whether P. multocida can lead to fatal sepsis in such cases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a case report and literature review. It includes a single case study of a 51-year-old woman who developed fatal P. multocida sepsis after cat bites, with no known comorbidities. The review does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates, but rather on the case and related literature.
Consensus and conclusions
The case demonstrates that Pasteurella multocida can cause fulminant, fatal sepsis in immunocompetent individuals, challenging the assumption that severe disease is limited to immunocompromised hosts. This highlights the importance of considering P. multocida as a potential pathogen in severe infections following cat bites.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to a single case report, which may not be representative of broader trends. There is a lack of comprehensive data on the frequency and severity of P. multocida infections in immunocompetent individuals, and the role of molecular diagnostics in early detection remains underexplored.
Research and practice implications
Clinicians should consider initiating empirical treatment early for high-risk cat bites and integrate molecular diagnostics in cases where conventional cultures are negative. Further research is needed to better understand the pathogenic potential of P. multocida in immunocompetent individuals and to refine diagnostic and treatment protocols.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Published: 2026-11-20  |  Publisher: Guilford County (.gov)
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

吉尔福德县确认 2026 年第十八起动物 狂犬病 病例 - 吉尔福德县 (.gov)

Guilford County Confirms Eighteenth Case of Animal Rabies in 2026 - Guilford County (.gov)
新闻简报北卡罗来纳州吉尔福德县于2026年11月19日确认了2026年第18起动物狂犬病病例,涉及在斯托克斯代尔麦克罗里路发现的一只臭鼬。该病例由吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务局公共卫生部确认。该报告强调了当地野生动物中持续存在的狂犬病,并敦促居民采取预防措施,例如为家养宠物接种疫苗和避免与野生动物接触。公共卫生官员提供了动物服务部门的联系方式,并强调报告流浪动物以降低风险的重要性。确诊病例强调了预防 狂犬病 的必要性,因为该病毒全年在该地区传播。报告中没有具体说明传播或卫生系统影响的更多细节。
查看新闻五要素
时间
吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务机构于 2026 年 11 月 19 日报告了该病例,事件发生于 2026 年 11 月 18 日。
地点与对象
吉尔福德县,北卡罗来纳州,美国受影响的人群包括该地区的居民和宠物。
事件
吉尔福德县于 2026 年确认了第 18 起动物 狂犬病 病例,涉及斯托克斯代尔麦克罗里路 (McCrory Road) 的一只臭鼬。该报告由吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务机构 - 公共卫生部发布。
规模、影响与风险
这是2026年第18例动物狂犬病确诊病例。通过公共卫生指导强调该风险,以防止人类接触并确保家养宠物适当的狂犬病。
应对、状态与不确定性
该县正在积极提供公共卫生指导,包括动物服务部门的联系信息,并敦促居民报告流浪动物。该病例已通过检测得到确认,但没有提供有关传播或卫生系统影响的进一步细节。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Guilford County Confirms Eighteenth Case of Animal Rabies in 2026 - Guilford County (.gov)

News Brief
Guilford County, North Carolina, confirmed its eighteenth case of animal rabies in 2026 on November 19, 2026, involving a skunk found on McCrory Road in Stokesdale. The case was identified by the Guilford County Health and Human Services Agency - Department of Public Health. The report highlights the ongoing presence of rabies in the local wildlife and urges residents to take preventive measures, such as vaccinating domestic pets and avoiding contact with wild animals. Public health officials have provided contact details for Animal Services and emphasized the importance of reporting stray animals to mitigate risks. The confirmed case underscores the need for vigilance in rabies prevention, as the virus circulates throughout the year in the area. No further details on the spread or health-system impact were specified in the report.

This is the eighteenth confirmed case of animal rabies in 2026. North Carolina law requires that all domestic pets (cats, dogs and ferrets), whether living inside or outside, aged four months or older be vaccinated for rabies. Animals that are confined in outdoor fenced areas should also have current rabies vaccinations. Supervising pets when they are outdoors could prevent contact with wild animals and lessen the possibility of rabies exposure. Guilford County Animal Services may be contacted for more information on rabies prevention at 336-641-2506 or visit our Animal Services website. For information regarding the current rabies cases, please contact Jorge Ortega at 336-641-3409 or jortega1@guilfordcountync.gov. Rabies circulates within our wildlife population throughout the entire year. Here are some tips to help prevent you or your family from being exposed to the rabies virus: Avoid direct contact with wildlife, dead or alive. Do not approach, try to play with, touch, rescue or treat any wildlife. If you find a sick or injured animal, contact Animal Control at 336-641-5990. Avoid any animal displaying unnatural behavior. Wild animals are not usually friendly so be very careful if they approach you. Do not try to separate fighting animals. Feed your pets indoors. Leaving food outside will attract dogs, cats and other wildlife into your yard. Report all stray animals to Animal Control at 336-641-5990 in Greensboro and 336-883-3224 in High Point. Stray animals may not have current rabies vaccinations. If a person is bitten by an animal (domestic or stray), wash the bitten area immediately with soap and water, seek medical attention, and report the bite to local Animal Control Officers. 卫生部调查人类 狂犬病 病例和可能的暴露 - 耶路撒冷邮报

Health Ministry investigates human rabies case, possible exposures - The Jerusalem Post
新闻简报以色列卡法鲁斯 (Kfar Ruth) 确诊了一例人类 狂犬病 病例,这名妇女据信 30 多岁,目前在茨里芬 (Tzrifin) 的沙米尔医疗中心 (Shamir Medical Center),情况危急。卫生部正在调查可能的接触情况,确定可能与受感染患者接触过的个人,并为他们接种疫苗。疑似暴露源是2026年8月27日在Kfar Ruth地区附近发现的一只母狗,后来被证实感染了狂犬病,并在被捡起几天后死亡。2026年8月26日至27日,这只狗出现在卡法鲁斯Keshet幼儿园附近的操场上。卫生部敦促自2026年8月17日以来怀疑接触过这只动物或流浪动物的任何人立即就医。该应对措施包括针对暴露个体的 暴露后预防 (PEP),其中涉及伤口护理和 狂犬病 疫苗,如果在症状出现之前施用,则几乎普遍有效。卫生部强调,狂犬病 不是通过空气或完整的皮肤传播,而是通过唾液传播,通常是通过咬伤、抓伤或接触体液传播。调查正在进行中,卫生部正在努力查明所有潜在的接触情况,农业部正在检查可能的动物接触。
查看新闻五要素
时间
卫生部于2026年11月18日公布了此病例,据报道该妇女于2026年11月19日情况危急。小狗于2026年8月27日被发现,在被抱起几天后死亡。该部正在努力查明自 2026 年 8 月 17 日以来接触过病毒的人员。
地点与对象
该病例发生在以色列卡法鲁斯,在该地区附近发现了这只母狗。这名女子据信年龄 30 多岁,是茨里芬沙米尔医疗中心的一名患者。2026年8月26日至27日,这只狗出现在卡法鲁斯凯谢特幼儿园附近的操场上。
事件
确诊一例人类 狂犬病 病例,卫生部正在调查可能的暴露情况。该妇女情况危急,卫生部正在努力查明其他可能接触过该感染患者的人。
规模、影响与风险
已报告一例确诊的人类 狂犬病 病例。卫生部正在为可能接触过病毒的个人接种疫苗。尽管人与人之间的 传播 并不常见,但仍强调了 传播 的风险。该部还在调查与其他动物的可能接触。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部正在开展流行病学调查,确定可能的接触情况,并为个人接种疫苗。农业部也参与了动物接触调查。回应正在进行中,调查状态仍然活跃。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Health Ministry investigates human rabies case, possible exposures - The Jerusalem Post

News Brief
A human rabies case has been diagnosed in Kfar Ruth, Israel, with the woman, believed to be in her 30s, currently in critical condition at Shamir Medical Center in Tzrifin. The Health Ministry is investigating possible exposures, identifying individuals who may have come into contact with the infected patient, and vaccinating them. The suspected source of exposure is a female dog found on August 27, 2026, near the Kfar Ruth area, which was later confirmed to be rabies-infected and died several days after being picked up. The dog was present at the playground adjacent to Keshet Kindergarten in Kfar Ruth on August 26 and 27, 2026. The ministry is urging anyone who suspects contact with the animal or stray animals since August 17, 2026, to seek medical attention immediately. The response includes post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) for exposed individuals, which involves wound care and a rabies vaccine, and is almost universally effective if administered before symptoms appear. The ministry emphasized that rabies is not transmitted through the air or unbroken skin, but through saliva, typically via bites, scratches, or contact with bodily fluids. The investigation is ongoing, with the Health Ministry working to identify all potential exposures and the Agriculture Ministry examining possible animal contact.
Original source text (English)
CONFERENCES ISRAEL NEWS WORLD NEWS MIDDLE EAST BUSINESS & INNOVATION DEFENSE & TECH OPINION JP STORE Login LoginSubscribe HOME PAGE JPOST DIGITAL LIBRARY THE JERUSALEM REPORT JERUSALEM POST CONFERENCES REAL ESTATE LISTINGS JP STORE JPOST HIGHER EDUCATION BUSINESS & INNOVATION DEFENSE & TECH PREMIUM Categories ISRAEL NEWS MIDDLE EAST OPINION WORLD NEWS ENVIRONMENT & CLIMATE CHANGE HEALTH & WELLNESS ALIYAH PODCAST US POLITICS DIASPORA JUDAISM CHRISTIAN WORLD FOOD & RECIPES ARCHAEOLOGY SCIENCE HISTORY OMG KABBALAH LAW SPONSORED CONTENT ADVERTISE WITH US TERMS OF USE PRIVACY POLICY CONTACT US CANCEL SUBSCRIPTION CUSTOMER SERVICE ABOUT US Jerusalem Post/Health & Wellness Human rabies case reported in Kfar Ruth, Health Ministry working to identify any others exposed The woman, believed to be in her 30s, is in critical condition, Shamir Medical Center in Tzrifin said. Follow us on Google Female dog infected with rabies found near Kfar Ruth on August 27, 2026. Anyone who has had contact with the animal is urged to immediately seek medical attention. (photo credit: HEALTH MINISTRY) ByDANYA SAPERSTEIN NOVEMBER 18, 2026 22:09 Updated: NOVEMBER 19, 2026 17:59 A person has been diagnosed with rabies, and the Health Ministry has begun an investigation to identify any other cases of infection within the Kfar Ruth area, the ministry announced on Wednesday. The woman, believed to be in her 30s, is in critical condition, Shamir Medical Center in Tzrifin said on Thursday morning. The epidemiological investigation found that the possible source of exposure was a female puppy found on August 27, 2026, on the side of the road in Kfar Ruth. The puppy died several days after being picked up. The ministry is working to identify people who may have been exposed and has begun vaccinating them, while the Agriculture Ministry is investigating possible contact with any other animals. Anyone who suspects they may have come into contact with the puppy in the Kfar Ruth area or its surroundings since 10 days before the puppy was found, August 17, is urged to contact the nearest Health Ministry district office as soon as possible to assess the need for preventative treatment against rabies. The dog is known to have been present at the playground adjacent to Keshet Kindergarten in Kfar Ruth on August 26 and 27, 2026. The entrance to the Emergency room at the Hadassah Ein Kerem Hospital in Jerusalem on November 1, 2020. (credit: YONATAN SINDEL/FLASH90) Those suspecting they have been exposed should immediately contact the Health Ministry Outside regular office hours and on weekends, individuals should go to a hospital emergency room. Parents of children who were in the area are asked to check with their children whether they came into contact with the puppy or with stray animals and contact the local health office if necessary, while pet owners whose animals may have come into contact with the puppy should contact their veterinarian for instructions on appropriate treatment. The ministry emphasized that human-to-human transmission of rabies is uncommon, and that the disease is not transmitted through the air or through contact with unbroken skin. Nevertheless, the ministry is working to identify all individuals who may have been exposed to the infected patient in circumstances that could allow the transmission of the virus and to vaccinate them as soon as possible. Rabies is transmitted through saliva, generally through bites, scratches, or direct contact with bodily fluids such as in the eyes, mouth, or open wounds. After a person is exposed to the rabies virus, an incubation period can last from a few days to about a year before the virus infects the central nervous system and causes symptoms. To prevent the virus from entering the nervous system, an emergency response called post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) is performed. PEP involves wound care and a course of rabies vaccine and may include administering rabies immunoglobulin or monoclonal antibodies into the wound. PEP is almost universally effective if it is given before the disease has infected the central nervous system. Once it does and symptoms appear, rabies is almost universally fatal. Guilford County confirms 18th animal rabies case of 2026 after skunk tests positive - WXLV

查看新闻五要素
时间
吉尔福德县卫生官员于 2026 年 11 月 19 日确认了 2026 年第 18 例动物 狂犬病 病例,此前一只臭鼬于 2026 年 11 月 19 日检测出 狂犬病 呈阳性。

吉尔福德县斯托克斯代尔发现一只臭鼬患有狂犬病,并检测出 狂犬病病毒 呈阳性。据吉尔福德县卫生与公众服务局公共卫生部报告,这是该县 2026 年第 18 例确诊的动物 狂犬病 病例。

Guilford County confirms 18th animal rabies case of 2026 after skunk tests positive - WXLV

News Brief
Guilford County, North Carolina, confirmed its 18th animal rabies case of 2026 after a skunk tested positive for the virus on November 19, 2026. The case was reported by the Guilford County Health and Human Services Agency’s Department of Public Health. The skunk was found on McCrory Road in Stokesdale, prompting officials to remind residents to vaccinate their pets and avoid contact with wildlife. Rabies circulates in the local wildlife population year-round, and health authorities emphasized the importance of vaccination and vigilance to prevent exposure. Residents are advised to contact Animal Control at 336-641-5990 if they encounter a sick or injured animal. The county is actively monitoring the situation and providing guidance to reduce the risk of rabies transmission.

欧盟批准赛诺菲 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于所有年龄段 - Contagion Live

EU Approves Sanofi's Rabies Vaccine VaxRabeo for All Age Groups - Contagion Live
新闻简报据 Contagion Live 报道,欧盟已批准赛诺菲的 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于所有年龄段。这一决定标志着 狂犬病 预防的重大发展,将 疫苗 的可及性扩大到更广泛的人群。此次批准是基于 疫苗 的功效和安全性概况,并通过严格的方法进行评估。虽然证据没有具体说明与此批准相关的病例数、死亡数或暴露量,但它强调了监管机构对 疫苗 在不同年龄组中预防 狂犬病 的能力的信心。响应状态表明 疫苗 现已可供使用,并且随着批准的确认,不确定性已解决。这一进展是加强针对 狂犬病 的公共卫生措施的持续努力的一部分,以确保所有人群都免受这种致命疾病的侵害。

所提供的证据中未报告。

该批准得到了欧盟的确认,疫苗 现已适用于所有年龄段。

EU Approves Sanofi's Rabies Vaccine VaxRabeo for All Age Groups - Contagion Live

News Brief
The European Union has approved Sanofi's Rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for use in all age groups, according to Contagion Live. This decision marks a significant development in rabies prevention, expanding the vaccine's accessibility to a broader demographic. The approval was based on the vaccine's efficacy and safety profile, which were evaluated through rigorous methods. While the evidence does not specify the number of cases, deaths, or exposures related to this approval, it highlights the regulatory body's confidence in the vaccine's ability to prevent rabies across different age groups. The response status indicates that the vaccine is now available for use, and the uncertainty is resolved as the approval is confirmed. This development is part of ongoing efforts to enhance public health measures against rabies, ensuring that all populations are protected from this deadly disease.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

欧盟批准赛诺菲 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 适用于所有年龄段

EU Approves Sanofi's Rabies Vaccine VaxRabeo for All Age Groups
新闻简报The European Commission has approved Sanofi's rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for use in all age groups, marking the first marketing authorization for the vaccine. The approval follows a positive opinion from the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026, and is valid in all EU member states, as well as Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway. VaxRabeo is designed to minimize allergic reactions and aligns with WHO-recommended dosing schedules for pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis. The vaccine uses the same Wistar rabies strain as Sanofi's existing vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies, and is part of a clinical program that compared it with these vaccines in both PrEP and PEP settings. While the studies showed comparable immunogenicity and safety, the release did not include specific study names, enrollment figures, or peer-reviewed results, making it difficult to independently assess the scale of each comparison. The approval highlights the importance of timely rabies vaccination, as the disease remains a significant global health threat, with an estimated 59,000 deaths annually and 29 million people receiving PEP yearly. The announcement did not specify launch timing for individual EU countries, but the vaccine is now available for use across the region.
查看新闻五要素
时间
继人用药品委员会于 2026 年 9 月 17 日提出积极意见后,欧盟委员会于 2026 年 11 月 19 日批准了 VaxRabeo。疫苗 的批准在所有欧盟成员国以及冰岛、列支敦士登和挪威有效。
地点与对象
该批准适用于所有欧盟成员国、冰岛、列支敦士登和挪威。疫苗 适合所有年龄段的个人,包括需要 暴露后预防 的旅行者和患者。
事件
欧盟委员会批准赛诺菲的 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于所有年龄段个体的暴露前预防 (PrEP) 和 暴露后预防 (PEP)。这是基于人用药品委员会的积极意见,疫苗 的首次营销授权。
规模、影响与风险
该批准引入了一种新的疫苗选项用于狂犬病的预防,这可能会对医疗系统和患者产生影响,通过提供新一代的疫苗。该疫苗设计用于最小化过敏反应,并符合世界卫生组织推荐的给药方案。全球范围内狂犬病的风险依然很高,每年估计有59,000人死亡,29 million人接受暴露后预防。
应对、状态与不确定性
此次批准基于 VaxRabeo 与现有 疫苗 的临床研究,显示出可比的免疫原性和安全性。该新闻稿不包括个别研究名称、入学人数或同行评审结果,因此无法独立评估每次比较的规模。疫苗 的营销授权在欧盟及其相关国家/地区有效,但未指定各个成员国的启动时间。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

EU Approves Sanofi's Rabies Vaccine VaxRabeo for All Age Groups

News Brief
The European Commission has approved Sanofi's rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for use in all age groups, marking the first marketing authorization for the vaccine. The approval follows a positive opinion from the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026, and is valid in all EU member states, as well as Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway. VaxRabeo is designed to minimize allergic reactions and aligns with WHO-recommended dosing schedules for pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis. The vaccine uses the same Wistar rabies strain as Sanofi's existing vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies, and is part of a clinical program that compared it with these vaccines in both PrEP and PEP settings. While the studies showed comparable immunogenicity and safety, the release did not include specific study names, enrollment figures, or peer-reviewed results, making it difficult to independently assess the scale of each comparison. The approval highlights the importance of timely rabies vaccination, as the disease remains a significant global health threat, with an estimated 59,000 deaths annually and 29 million people receiving PEP yearly. The announcement did not specify launch timing for individual EU countries, but the vaccine is now available for use across the region.

Coli Food Safety Listeria Salmonella Fungal Infections Candida auris Gastrointestinal Infections C. Difficile H. Pylori Norovirus Healthcare Associated Infections (HAI) Prevention Antibiotics Immunizations Vaccines Respiratory Infections Community-Acquired Pneumonia Measles Influenza Sexually Transmitted Diseases HIV / AIDS HPV HSV PrEP Skin & Soft Tissue Diseases MRSA Zoonotic & Vector-Borne Diseases Lyme Disease ZIKA Choose Topic Spotlight - Vaccines in America| Antibiotics Deconstructed| Bench to Bedside With SIDP| Antimicrobial Stewardship| Hepatitis Advertisement News|Articles|November 20, 2026 EU Approves Sanofi's Rabies Vaccine VaxRabeo for All Age Groups Advertisement The European Commission has approved VaxRabeo (rabies virus, inactivated, Wistar strain; Sanofi) for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and postexposure prophylaxis (PEP) against rabies in individuals of all ages. The decision, announced November 19, 2026, is the first marketing authorization for the vaccine and follows a positive opinion from the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026.1,2 VaxRabeo is produced with Vero cell technology and uses the same Wistar rabies strain as Sanofi's existing vaccines Verorab and Imovax Rabies. The company said the formulation is designed to minimize the risk of allergic reactions.1 The vaccine can be given on the 2-dose PrEP schedule and the 4-dose PEP schedule recommended by the World Health Organization (WHO), with or without rabies immunoglobulin.1 A European Commission marketing authorization is valid in all European Union member states, as well as in Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway. For clinicians, the decision adds a newer-generation option from the same Wistar strain lineage for both travelers seeking PrEP and patients who need PEP after a potential exposure.1,2 Clinical Program Compared VaxRabeo With Existing Vaccines Sanofi's clinical development program compared VaxRabeo with Verorab and Imovax Rabies in both PrEP and PEP settings. In PrEP studies, 2-dose and 3-dose regimens produced comparable protective antibody levels in adults and children.1 Booster doses given at 12 months or at 24 to 36 months after primary vaccination produced strong responses, which the company described as high boostability. In PEP studies, VaxRabeo showed immunogenicity similar to the comparator vaccines, and 100% of participants receiving intramuscular doses reached protective antibody levels.1 The safety profile was consistent with existing rabies vaccines, and no significant safety concerns emerged across the program.1 VaxRabeo has a shelf life of 60 months and remains stable at temperatures up to 27 °C for 96 hours before reconstitution.1 The release did not include individual study names, enrollment figures, or a citation to peer-reviewed results, so the size of each comparison cannot yet be assessed independently. Recent Rabies Commentary, Resources On World Rabies Day, When to Seek Treatment, Clinical Considerations for Post Exposure Prophylaxis FAQ: Rising Rabies Exposure Calls PEP Decisions Back in Focus Global Rabies Burden Remains High Rabies is present in 150 countries and causes an estimated 59,000 deaths each year, according to WHO figures cited by Sanofi.1 About 29 million people receive PEP annually, and roughly 40% of people bitten by animals suspected of having rabies are children younger than 15 years.1 Travelers also face exposure risk, estimated at about 0.4% per month of stay in areas where rabies is present.1 Sanofi has supplied rabies vaccines for 5 decades, and the company describes VaxRabeo as a next-generation vaccine built on this experience.1 Timely PEP after a bite or scratch from a suspect animal remains the main way to prevent death once exposure occurs.1 "Death from rabies should be prevented at all costs; it is entirely preventable with vaccination provided on time," Thomas Triomphe, head of vaccines at Sanofi, said in a statement. "This first approval of VaxRabeo is a proud milestone for Sanofi and reflects our commitment to rabies protection, built on five decades of expertise and innovation."1 "With VaxRabeo, we are bringing a next-generation rabies vaccine to healthcare systems and patients," Triomphe said.1 The announcement did not include launch timing for individual European Union member states. View five news elements
Time
The European Commission approved VaxRabeo on November 19, 2026, following a positive opinion from the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026. The vaccine's approval is effective in all European Union member states, as well as in Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway.
Location and population
The approval applies to all European Union member states, Iceland, Liechtenstein, and Norway. The vaccine is intended for individuals of all ages, including travelers and patients requiring post-exposure prophylaxis.
Event
The European Commission approved Sanofi's rabies vaccine VaxRabeo for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) in individuals of all ages. This is the first marketing authorization for the vaccine, based on a positive opinion from the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use.
Scale, impact and risk
The approval introduces a new vaccine option for rabies prevention, which could impact healthcare systems and patients by providing a newer-generation vaccine. The vaccine is designed to minimize allergic reactions and aligns with WHO-recommended dosing schedules. The risk of rabies remains high globally, with an estimated 59,000 deaths annually, and 29 million people receiving PEP yearly.
Response, status and uncertainty
The approval is based on clinical studies comparing VaxRabeo with existing vaccines, showing comparable immunogenicity and safety. The release did not include individual study names, enrollment figures, or peer-reviewed results, so the scale of each comparison cannot be independently assessed. The vaccine's marketing authorization is valid across the EU and associated countries, but launch timing for individual member states was not specified.

新闻稿:赛诺菲的下一代 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 在欧盟获得批准,适合所有年龄段

Press Release: Sanofi’s next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, approved in the EU for all ages
新闻简报赛诺菲宣布欧盟委员会批准其下一代 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 用于欧盟所有年龄段。此前,欧洲药品管理局人用医药产品委员会于 2026 年 9 月 17 日发表了积极意见。疫苗 专为预暴露和 暴露后预防 设计,并采用 Vero-cell 技术制造。它在暴露前和暴露后设置中均表现出与现有赛诺菲 疫苗(如 Verorab 和 Imovax 狂犬病)相当的保护性抗体水平,且安全性符合当前标准。VaxRabeo 具有 60 个月的保质期和改进的热稳定性,使其更方便在各种医疗保健环境中使用。狂犬病 是一种病毒性人畜共患疾病,仍然是一个严重的全球健康问题,每年导致约 59,000 人死亡,主要是由于狗咬伤。尽管通过及时的 疫苗接种 完全可以预防,但 狂犬病 仍然构成重大威胁,特别是在亚洲和非洲等地区。欧盟的批准反映了赛诺菲对 狂犬病 预防的承诺,并强调了 狂犬病 在降低这种致命疾病风险方面的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
在欧洲药品管理局人用医药产品委员会于 2026 年 9 月 17 日提出积极意见后,欧盟委员会于 2026 年 11 月 19 日批准了 VaxRabeo。
地点与对象
该批准与欧盟相关,其中包括所有有 狂犬病 暴露风险的年龄组和人群,特别是流行地区的人群。
事件
赛诺菲宣布其下一代 狂犬病 疫苗、VaxRabeo(用于预暴露)和 暴露后预防 在欧盟获得批准。这是一个监管里程碑,而不是 暴发 或健康事件。
规模、影响与风险
狂犬病 仍然是一个重大的全球公共卫生挑战,每年在全球造成约 59,000 人死亡。它主要通过受感染动物的唾液传播,其中狗是主要来源。
应对、状态与不确定性
此次批准基于一项综合临床计划,将 VaxRabeo 与现有的赛诺菲 疫苗 进行比较,显示出可比的保护性抗体水平以及与当前 疫苗 一致的安全性。[N14]、[N15]、[N16]、[N27]、[N28]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Press Release: Sanofi’s next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, approved in the EU for all ages

News Brief
Sanofi has announced the approval of its next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, by the European Commission for use in all age groups across the EU. This follows a positive opinion from the European Medicines Agency’s Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026. The vaccine is designed for both pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis and is manufactured using Vero-cell technology. It demonstrates comparable protective antibody levels to existing Sanofi vaccines like Verorab and Imovax Rabies in both pre-exposure and post-exposure settings, with a safety profile that aligns with current standards. VaxRabeo has a 60-month shelf life and improved thermostability, making it more convenient for use in various healthcare settings. Rabies, a viral zoonotic disease, remains a critical global health issue, causing around 59,000 deaths annually, primarily through dog bites. Despite being entirely preventable with timely vaccination, rabies continues to pose a significant threat, especially in regions like Asia and Africa. The EU approval reflects Sanofi’s commitment to rabies prevention and highlights the importance of vaccination in reducing the risk of this fatal disease.
Original source text (English)
My Markets Watchlist Markets News Search Business Strategy Economy Finance Retail Advertising Careers Law Media Real Estate Small Business The Better Work Project Personal Finance Tech Science AI Enterprise Transportation Startups Innovation Markets News Stocks Indices Commodities Crypto Currencies ETFs Lifestyle Entertainment Culture Travel Food Health Education Parenting Military & Defense Politics Reviews Home Kitchen Style Streaming Pets Tech Deals Gifts Tickets Video Big Business So Expensive View From Above Small Business Authorized Account Risky Business Boot Camp Still Standing How Crime Works Life Lessons Level Up Community Games Boxed Pipeline Knighthop Tally Press Release: Sanofi’s next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, approved in the EU for all ages PRESS RELEASE GlobeNewswire Nov. 19, 2026, 01:00 AM Sanofi’s next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, approved in the EU for all ages Paris, November 19, 2026. Sanofi announced today that the European Commission has approved VaxRabeo (rabies virus inactivated strain Wistar) for pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) against rabies for all age groups. This is the first marketing authorisation approval for VaxRabeo and follows the positive opinion adopted by the European Medicines Agency’s Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on September 17, 2026. VaxRabeo is manufactured using state of the art Vero-cell technology. It is formulated to minimize the risk of allergic reactions and has a longer shelf life than current Sanofi rabies vaccines with an improved thermostability. Rabies is a viral zoonotic disease present in 150 countries, causing approximately 59,000 deaths worldwide annually, according to World Health Organization (WHO) estimates. No treatment exists and it is only preventable by vaccines (in combination with immunoglobulins in post-exposure prophylaxis); once clinical symptoms appear, the disease is almost always fatal. It is transmitted primarily through the saliva of infected animals, most commonly via dog bites, and remains a significant global public health challenge despite being entirely vaccine preventable. According to the WHO, approximately 29 million people receive post-exposure vaccination each year. "Death from rabies should be prevented at all costs; it is entirely preventable with vaccination provided on time. This first approval of VaxRabeo is a proud milestone for Sanofi and reflects our commitment to rabies protection, built on five decades of expertise and innovation. With VaxRabeo, we are bringing a next-generation rabies vaccine to healthcare systems and patients," said Thomas Triomphe, Head of Vaccines, Sanofi. The EU approval is supported by a comprehensive clinical programme comparing VaxRabeo with Sanofi’s current standard of care vaccines (Verorab and Imovax Rabies) in both PrEP and PEP settings, across all age groups and WHO recommended schedules. VaxRabeo showed comparable protective antibody levels in PrEP, a similar immunogenicity profile in PEP, and high boostability of the booster dose given 12 or 24-36 months after PrEP. VaxRabeo showed a safety profile in line with existing vaccines and no significant safety concerns. About rabies Rabies is a viral zoonotic disease that affects the central nervous system. Transmission occurs predominantly through the saliva of infected animals, with dogs responsible for most human cases, typically through bites, scratches, and contact with broken skin. Other mammals, including cats, bats, foxes, raccoons, skunks, jackals, and mongooses can also transmit the virus to humans. Children bear a disproportionate burden: 40% of people bitten by suspect rabid animals are under 15 years of age. Despite being entirely vaccine preventable rabies continues to cause preventable deaths, particularly in Asia and Africa. For travellers visiting endemic regions, potential rabies exposure occurs in approximately 0.4% of travellers per month of stay, making pre-travel vaccination an important consideration. About VaxRabeo VaxRabeo (rabies virus inactivated strain Wistar) is produced using Vero cell technology and shares the same viral strain as Verorab and Imovax Rabies vaccines, which have demonstrated proven safety and efficacy over more than five decades. It is indicated for pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis against rabies for all age groups, and offers flexible vaccination schedules, including reduced WHO-recommended 2-dose PrEP, and 4-dose PEP for unvaccinated individuals, administered with or without rabies immunoglobulin. In PrEP studies, two-dose and three-dose regimens of VaxRabeo achieved protective antibody levels comparable to those of Verorab and Imovax Rabies in both adults and children. Booster doses administered 12 or 24–36 months after the last PrEP dose demonstrated high boostability, supporting the lasting immunogenicity of VaxRabeo. In PEP studies, VaxRabeo demonstrated a similar immunogenicity profile to current Sanofi SoC vaccines when administered with or without rabies immunoglobulins, with 100% of participants achieving protective antibody levels intramuscularly. Across all studies, VaxRabeo showed an acceptable safety profile like existing vaccines, was well tolerated, and no significant safety concerns were identified when administered alone or with rabies immunoglobulins. VaxRabeo has a 60-month (five years) shelf-life and maintains stability at temperatures up to 27°C for 96 hours before reconstitution. About Sanofi Sanofi is an R&D driven, AI-powered biopharma company committed to improving people’s lives and delivering compelling growth. We apply our deep understanding of the immune system to invent medicines and vaccines that treat and protect millions of people around the world, with an innovative pipeline that could benefit millions more. Our team is guided by one purpose: we chase the miracles of science to improve people’s lives; this inspires us to drive progress and deliver positive impact for our people and the communities we serve, by addressing the most urgent healthcare, environmental, and societal challenges of our time. Carteret Animal Control 提供免费的 狂犬病 疫苗接种 诊所 - 新伯尔尼太阳报

Carteret Animal Control offers free rabies vaccination clinic - New Bern Sun Journal
新闻简报继美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 警告全国范围内人类接触 狂犬病 的情况增加后,北卡罗来纳州卡特雷特县动物控制服务中心于 2026 年 11 月 19 日为宠物举办了免费的 狂犬病 狂犬病 诊所。该诊所以得来速形式组织,要求将宠物关在笼子里或用皮带拴住,并向所有持有居住证明的卡特雷特县居民开放。CDC 强调了人类 狂犬病 暴露的局部增加和几起备受瞩目的 暴发,包括多个县涉及小山羊的大规模事件。2026 年 11 月 10 日,与克雷文县接壤的皮特县报告了一例浣熊确诊的 狂犬病 病例,强调了通过咬伤或接触唾液感染 狂犬病 传播 的风险。这种疾病会导致严重的脑部炎症,一旦出现症状,死亡率接近 100%,可以通过 疫苗接种 来预防。敦促居民给宠物接种疫苗,避免接触野生动物,并向当局报告任何异常行为或潜在的接触情况。该诊所的目的是确保适当的免疫接种,宠物需要间隔一年进行两次初始 疫苗接种,然后每三年进行一次。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件于2026年11月19日报道,CDC于2026年11月10日发出警告,并于2026年8月29日确认了大规模狂犬病暴露事件。
地点与对象
该事件发生在北卡罗来纳州卡特雷特县,涉及饲养宠物(特别是狗、猫和雪貂)的居民,并影响了伯克、戴维森、戴维、达勒姆、艾雷德尔、梅克伦堡和沃托加等多个县。
事件
继 CDC 发出人类接触 狂犬病 的警告以及皮特县一只浣熊确诊 狂犬病 病例后,卡特雷特县动物控制服务中心为宠物举办了一个免费的 狂犬病 狂犬病 诊所。
规模、影响与风险
CDC 报告了人类 狂犬病 暴露的局部增加以及一些备受瞩目的 暴发。大规模 狂犬病 暴露事件涉及多个县的多只小山羊。皮特县确诊的浣熊病例凸显了通过咬伤或接触唾液感染 狂犬病 传播 的风险,一旦出现症状,死亡率接近 100%。
应对、状态与不确定性
该诊所以免下车活动的形式举办,宠物需要被关在笼子里或用皮带拴着。鼓励居民给宠物接种疫苗,避免接触野生动物,并报告被咬伤的情况。该诊所向所有卡特雷特县居民开放,每辆车最多可携带三只宠物,并需要提供居住证明。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Carteret Animal Control offers free rabies vaccination clinic - New Bern Sun Journal

News Brief
Carteret County Animal Control Services in North Carolina hosted a free rabies vaccination clinic for pets on November 19, 2026, following a Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) warning about a nationwide increase in human exposures to rabies. The clinic was organized in a drive-thru format, requiring pets to be controlled in cages or on leashes, and was open to all Carteret County residents with proof of residency. The CDC highlighted local increases in human rabies exposures and several high-profile outbreaks, including a mass event involving baby goats in multiple counties. A confirmed rabies case in a raccoon in Pitt County, which borders Craven County, was reported on November 10, 2026, emphasizing the risk of rabies transmission through bites or contact with saliva. The disease, which causes severe brain inflammation and has a nearly 100% death rate once symptoms appear, is preventable through vaccination. Residents were urged to vaccinate their pets, avoid contact with wild animals, and report any unusual behavior or potential exposures to authorities. The clinic aimed to ensure proper immunization, with pets needing two initial vaccinations one year apart and then one every three years.

Contributed photo Facebook Twitter WhatsApp SMS Email Print Copy article link Save Carteret Animal Control offers free rabies vaccination clinic By Kira Blankenship kblankenship@newbernsj.com Kira Blankenship Author email Nov 19, 2026 Nov 19, 2026 Facebook Twitter WhatsApp SMS Email 1 of 2 A girl embraces a dog in a photo provided by Carteret County Health and Human Services.
