Contributed photo Facebook Twitter WhatsApp SMS Email Print Copy article link Save Facebook Twitter WhatsApp SMS Email Print Copy article link Save Carteret County Animal Control Services will host a free rabies vaccination clinic for residents with pets this weekend following the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention issuing a warning regarding a nationwide increase in human exposures to rabid or potentially rabid animals and a rise in post-exposure treatments. According to the N.C. Department of Health and Human Services, rabies is a fatal viral disease that attacks the brain and nerves of mammals, leading to severe brain inflammation and a nearly 100% death rate once clinical symptoms appear. Rabies is transmitted through the saliva of an infected animal, such as through a bite or contact with broken skin, eyes, nose or mouth. On Sept. 10, the CDC said in a health advisory that multiple U.S. jurisdictions have reported local increases in human rabies exposures, and that several high-profile rabies outbreaks and mass rabies exposure events have highlighted the importance of performing careful rabies risk assessments and following evidence-based recommendations. The DHHS reported a mass rabies exposure event following confirmed positive cases of rabies on Aug. 29 involving multiple baby goats from the Farm Adventures mobile petting zoo, which came into contact with numerous adults and children in Burke, Davidson, Davie, Durham, Iredell, Mecklenburg and Watauga counties. More recently, on Nov. 10, a public health report from Pitt County, which borders Craven County, stated that Pitt County Animal Services had identified a positive rabies case in a raccoon. The raccoon, which had attacked a dog and was captured in Winterville, was submitted for testing at the DHHS, and laboratory results confirmed it was positive for rabies. “Rabies is a serious but preventable public health concern, and this positive case is an important reminder of the need to protect both people and pets,” said Kayla Manning, interim health director of the CHS Public Health Division. “We are relieved that the dog involved in this incident was current on its rabies vaccination, which is a critical layer of protection against this disease. “We encourage residents to vaccinate their pets, avoid contact with wild animals and report any unusual behavior or potential exposures involving wildlife to the appropriate authorities.” On Saturday, Carteret County’s free rabies vaccination clinic event will take place in the parking lot of the Carteret County Health Department at 3820 Bridges St. in Morehead City from either 8 to 11 a.m. or while supplies last. According to Rachel Johnson, communications specialist and deputy clerk for Carteret County government, the clinic will be organized in a drive-thru format, which will require both pets and their owners to stay in their vehicles throughout the duration of the vaccination process. Pets must remain controlled at all times. For dogs, this could either be with a cage or on a leash with a maximum length of 6 feet, and all cats and ferrets are required to be transported in carriers. The clinic is open to all Carteret County residents, and a maximum of three pets per vehicle is allowed. Proof of residency will also be required of pet owners who wish to receive a free rabies vaccination for a dog, cat or ferret. Anyone interested who may be feeling unwell is asked to refrain from attending the clinic, and the same goes for all pets who appear to be unwell. According to N.C. Gen. Stat. 130A-185, owners of a dog, cat or ferret that is more than 4 months old are required to vaccinate them for rabies. To remain properly immunized, pets must receive two rabies vaccinations one year apart, then one vaccination every three years after. Any animal that is diagnosed with rabies by a licensed veterinarian is required under state law to be euthanized. All animal bites, whether from an unknown domestic, stray or feral animal, should be reported to a local animal control agency as soon as possible. To report an animal bite or seek guidance concerning potential exposures to rabies, Craven County residents can call the Craven County Health Department. 狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(四)人狂犬病及其控制

B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(四)人狂犬病及其控制
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly identify the location or affected population. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
前记 : 目前国际上关于狂犬病研究最权威、最全面的大型学术专著是 《 狂犬病的科学基础和管控( RABIES: SCIENTIFIC BASIS OF THE DISEASE AND ITS MANAGEMENT) 》,简称《 狂犬病 (RABIES) 》。该专著最新版( 第 5 版 )已于 2025年7月 面世。 本公众号已陆续将该专著第 4版(2020年版)的绝大部分内容翻译成中文。该专著最新版对旧版有较多更新和改进。本公众号将继续全文翻译该专著最新版的内容,将优先翻译新增加或更新较多章节的内容。 以下为该专著最新版的第 21章: ( Future developments and challenges ) 作者: Anthony R. Fooks , Alan C. Jackson 由于狂犬病在向国际组织报告之前应当通过临床样本的 实验室诊断 加以确认,用于确认 RABV 存在的诊断工具已发展到最先进的分子检测水平 (见第 13 章)。用于测定 病毒中和抗体( VNA ) 的血清学工具的开发也取得了进展(见第 14 章)。 在非洲大陆的狂犬病流行国家,由于文化、宗教和后勤方面的原因,人狂犬病仍是一种被忽视的热带病 (Fooks, 2005) 。从文化和宗教角度看,表现为脑炎型疾病的人狂犬病临床表现,常由 传统治疗者( traditional healers )处置。在喀麦隆的一项知识调查中,财富评分显著较低、但知识评分无显著差异的受访者表示,他们会求助于传统治疗者 (Barbosa Costa et al., 2018) 。因此,基于这样一种理解 —— 即由巫术所致之病无法用常规 " 西化 " 医学治疗 —— 脑炎型疾病的治疗便会求助于社区中的 " 传统 " 治疗者,使用本地药材 (Beasley et al., 2022) 。有必要提升卫生从业者以及他们执业所在地对狂犬病的认识。也 应当贬抑采用传统疗法治狂犬病 。在卫生保健体系薄弱的国家,无论患者是在医院还是在家中,都应为绝大多数狂犬病受害者实施姑息措施(见第 17 章)。 认识到可用于预防狂犬病的干预工具,对于预防狂犬病死亡具有至高无上的重要性 (见第 15 章和第 16 章)。在可能的情况下,建议前往流行地区的某些旅行者接受狂犬病 暴露前免疫( Pr-EP ) ,尽管此类建议的采纳未必总能得到落实。无论是否施用 PrEP ,适当的伤口处理和 PEP 的实施在预防死亡方面几乎 100% 有效。 狂犬病生物制品的高成本,始终是更广泛使用 Pr-EP 和狂犬病 PEP 措施的主要障碍。 供肌肉注射施用的疫苗,从早期粗糙的动物神经组织制剂发展到更安全的细胞培养衍生疫苗,提高了疫苗在 PrEP 和 PEP 两种情形下的使用接受度。 WHO 强烈建议停止生产和使用狂犬病 神经组织疫苗( NTV ),因其与不可接受的神经麻痹性并发症风险相关。然而在一些国家,特别是阿尔及利亚、阿根廷、玻利维亚和埃塞俄比亚,仍在生产供人用的 NTV 。浓缩、纯化的细胞培养和鸡胚疫苗 —— 合称 CCEEVs—— 已被证明对狂犬病安全有效 (Amicizia et al., 2024) 。人用狂犬病疫苗(主要为细胞培养来源)价格昂贵, PEP 需要四剂,再加上人狂犬病免疫球蛋白的费用, 施用于一名个体至少需 5000 美元 ,尚不含相关医疗成本。显然,这一量级的费用对世界上许多国家的大多数人而言完全无法企及。 迫切需要新型、低成本的人用狂犬病疫苗 (见第 15 章和第 20 章)。 所有获 WHO 认可的人用狂犬病疫苗都是灭活的、冻干的、安全且有效的,其中三种疫苗获 WHO 推荐。 关键在于, 所有获得许可的狂犬病疫苗都有堪称典范的安全记录,接种后出现不良反应的情况极为罕见 (Giesen et al., 2015a,b) 。由于儿童可能存在未被识别的狂犬病暴露、风险高于成人,并且承受着不成比例的狂犬病负担(几乎占全部病例的一半),在犬狂犬病呈地方性流行的国家,儿童应接受 Pr-EP 免疫接种。 15 岁以下儿童狂犬病感染的高关联性,促使有人提议在高风险区域应将 Pr-EP 作为儿科免疫方案的组成部分加以考虑 (WHO, 2013) 。这将简化所需的 PEP 措施,并且也可能针对未被察觉和未报告的暴露提供一定保护。可以有把握地假定,这一高度易感人群中只有少数接受了暴露前免疫接种,而经济因素是主要障碍。进一步的研究尝试将 PEP 方案缩短至 1 周,使 PEP 更可负担、更可及 (Wilde et al., 2016) 。疑似狂犬病暴露后可获得的暴露后工具包括接种疫苗和施用 RIG 。其目标是诱导 VNA (见第 12 章),作为 PEP 方案的组成部分提供针对 RABV 感染的保护 (Rupprecht et al., 2016) 。 VNA 抑制病毒复制的确切位置和机制仍然未知 。虽然皮内狂犬病免疫接种方案的使用正在全球范围内扩展 (Brown et al., 2008, 2011; Warrell et al., 2008) ,但 WHO 关于复溶疫苗须在 6 小时内使用的要求,将这一做法限制在每日接诊多例 PEP 病例的较大诊所。 需要更多研究来评估能够节省成本的免疫接种方案。 研究成果聚焦于新型疫苗的开发 —— 通过过表达病毒糖蛋白,可能减少所需的就诊次数。一种减毒活的三重糖蛋白 RABV 变异体已被证明在小鼠和犬模型中作为 Pr-EP 有效。这种减毒病毒疫苗在小鼠鞘内给药后能够在 脑脊液( CSF ) 中诱导狂犬病 VNA ,未来在 PEP 情形下可能具有价值 (Faber et al., 2002, 2009; Gnanadurai et al., 2015) 。 将 Pre-P 狂犬病疫苗作为多价儿科疫苗的一部分、纳入 扩大免疫规划( EPI ) ,应用于狂犬病流行国家,将加速减少人狂犬病死亡(见第 15 章)。主要挑战在于改变现行的儿科疫苗方案 (Chantasrisawad et al., 2022) 。此外,还存在疫苗生产商合理的成本考量和消极态度,特别是因为狂犬病流行区域主要是国内生产总值较低的国家。因此,人用疫苗的成本必须低廉,这就压缩了大型疫苗生产商为开发多价儿科疫苗支付费用时的利润空间 —— 而这也未能领会整合狂犬病干预策略所带来的利他性收益。 开发用于被动免疫的单克隆抗体,最终应当成为人狂犬病免疫球蛋白的一种便宜得多的替代品 —— 后者非常昂贵,并且在许多犬狂犬病流行的国家不易获得。 (未完待续) 本公众号相关文章的链接 权威的大型学术专著《狂犬病(Rabies)》最新版已面世 2020-06-30 一部跨越二十三年的"狂犬病百科全书" 2026-10-03 大型学术专著《狂犬病》2025年最新版的更新 2026-10-19 当代最著名狂犬病专家为学术专著《狂犬病》写的序言 2026-10-21 狂犬病毒的分类学(七) 2026-11-06 狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(一)引言+流行病学 2026-11-10 狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(二)免疫学+发病机制 2026-11-14 狂犬病未来的发展与面临的挑战(三)免疫学+发病机制 2026-11-16

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

赛诺菲新药 VaxRabeo 获得欧洲批准 狂犬病 疫苗 - Idéal Investisseur

Sanofi Receives European Approval for VaxRabeo, Its New Rabies Vaccine - Idéal Investisseur
新闻简报Sanofi has received European approval for its new rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, which is designed for both pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis against rabies for all age groups. The approval was granted on 19 November 2026, following a positive opinion from the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on 17 September 2026. VaxRabeo is produced using Vero cell technology and shares the same viral strain as Sanofi's existing vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies. The vaccine's safety profile is described as acceptable and comparable to current treatments, with no significant safety concerns identified. According to WHO estimates, rabies is present in 150 countries and causes approximately 59,000 deaths annually, with around 29 million people receiving post-exposure vaccination each year. The disease has no treatment and can only be prevented through timely vaccination. Sanofi's Vaccines division emphasizes the importance of preventing rabies-related deaths through its new vaccine.
查看新闻五要素
时间
欧盟委员会于 2026 年 11 月 19 日授予 VaxRabeo 上市许可。该决定是在欧洲药品管理局人用药品委员会于 2026 年 9 月 17 日发表积极意见之后做出的。
地点与对象
该批准与欧盟相关,根据赛诺菲引用的世界卫生组织估计,狂犬病 在欧盟 150 个国家/地区存在,每年在全球造成约 59,000 人死亡。
事件
赛诺菲的新型 狂犬病 疫苗、VaxRabeo 获得欧洲批准,该产品专为所有年龄段的预暴露而设计,而 暴露后预防 则针对 狂犬病。此次批准基于 VaxRabeo 与赛诺菲现有 疫苗、Verorab 和 Imovax 狂犬病 的临床试验。
规模、影响与风险
Rabies is present in 150 countries and causes approximately 59,000 deaths annually. Around 29 million people receive post-exposure vaccination each year. The disease has no treatment and can only be prevented through vaccination. VaxRabeo is presented as a next-generation vaccine with an acceptable safety profile and no significant safety concerns identified.
应对、状态与不确定性
该批准基于 VaxRabeo 与赛诺菲目前的 疫苗 的临床试验。安全性被描述为可接受的,并且与现有的 疫苗 类似。没有提及未解决的信息。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Sanofi Receives European Approval for VaxRabeo, Its New Rabies Vaccine - Idéal Investisseur

News Brief
Sanofi has received European approval for its new rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, which is designed for both pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis against rabies for all age groups. The approval was granted on 19 November 2026, following a positive opinion from the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on 17 September 2026. VaxRabeo is produced using Vero cell technology and shares the same viral strain as Sanofi's existing vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies. The vaccine's safety profile is described as acceptable and comparable to current treatments, with no significant safety concerns identified. According to WHO estimates, rabies is present in 150 countries and causes approximately 59,000 deaths annually, with around 29 million people receiving post-exposure vaccination each year. The disease has no treatment and can only be prevented through timely vaccination. Sanofi's Vaccines division emphasizes the importance of preventing rabies-related deaths through its new vaccine.

Connexion Connexion CAC 40 : Market closed 7 927,75 pts -1.89% Last updated : 20/11/2026 - 17h35 (last close) Last close data Top performers: CAPGEMINI +1,42% SANOFI +1,35% DANONE +0,74% ORANGE +0,53% PERNOD RICARD +0,42% Worst performers: STMICROELECTRONICS -10,81% ARCELORMITTAL -5,87% LEGRAND -4,64% AIRBUS -4,13% KERING -3,90% 🏠 Home ➤ Stock news Sanofi Receives European Approval for VaxRabeo, Its New Rabies Vaccine The European Commission has granted VaxRabeo its first marketing authorisation. It covers pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis against rabies for all age groups. Announced by Sanofi on 19 November 2026, the decision follows the positive opinion issued on 17 September 2026 by the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use of the European Medicines Agency. Published on Nov 19, 2026 at 08:47 Share X LinkedIn Facebook Copy link SANOFI 84.92 € +1.35 % Add us as a preferred source on Google A Vaccine Derived from the Same Strain as Verorab and Imovax Rabies VaxRabeo (inactivated rabies virus, Wistar strain) is produced using Vero cell technology. It shares the same viral strain as Verorab and Imovax Rabies, the rabies vaccines already marketed by Sanofi, which the company presents as a “next-generation” vaccine. The laboratory states that its formulation is designed to minimise the risk of allergic reactions. It also claims a longer shelf life and better thermostability than its current rabies vaccines: 60 months of storage and stability up to 27 °C for 96 hours before reconstitution. The approved vaccination schedules include a two-dose pre-exposure prophylaxis, in line with World Health Organization (WHO) recommendations. They also provide for a four-dose post-exposure prophylaxis for unvaccinated individuals, with or without rabies immunoglobulins. According to WHO estimates cited by Sanofi, rabies is present in 150 countries and causes approximately 59,000 deaths per year worldwide. Around 29 million people receive post-exposure vaccination each year. The disease has no treatment and can only be prevented through vaccination. Antibody Levels Comparable to the Group's Current Vaccines The authorisation is based on a clinical programme that compared VaxRabeo with Sanofi's current standard treatments, in both pre-exposure and post-exposure settings, for all age groups and according to WHO schedules. The comparator is therefore the group's existing portfolio, and the published results concern the antibody response. In pre-exposure settings, the two- and three-dose regimens achieved protective antibody levels comparable to those of the two reference vaccines, in adults as well as children. Booster doses administered 12 months, or 24 to 36 months, after the last dose produced a strong boost of the immune response, according to the press release. In post-exposure settings, the immunogenicity profile is described as similar, with 100% of participants reaching protective antibody levels after intramuscular administration. The group reports an acceptable safety profile, similar to that of existing vaccines, with no significant safety concern identified, whether the vaccine is administered alone or with immunoglobulins. Thomas Triomphe, head of Sanofi's Vaccines division, stated: “Deaths from rabies must be prevented at all costs; they are entirely preventable through timely vaccination.” Free · Every morning Technical market signals, before the opening bell. Andaman Media Delegation Visits Pasteur Institute of India in Coonoor; Experts Share Insights on Rabies Prevention for Andaman and Vaccine Research – ANDAMAN SHEEKHA - ANDAMAN SHEEKHA

News Brief
A media delegation from the Andaman and Nicobar Islands visited the Pasteur Institute of India in Coonoor on 12 March 2026 to learn about rabies prevention and vaccine research. The visit was part of a study tour aimed at understanding the institute’s work in disease prevention and vaccine development. The experts highlighted that rabies has not been reported in the Andaman and Nicobar Islands so far, and emphasized the importance of strict preventive measures to maintain this status. They advised that animals brought from the mainland, particularly dogs and cats, must be properly vaccinated and accompanied by valid vaccination certificates. The Pasteur Institute of India, established in 1907, has a long history of contributing to vaccine development and anti-rabies treatment. The delegation gained insights into the institute’s research programs and its role in public health preparedness. The visit underscored the need for vigilance in preventing rabies transmission, especially in regions like the Andaman and Nicobar Islands.
Original source text (English)
Skip to content November 22, 2026 ANDAMAN SHEEKHA The True Mirror of A & N Islands Primary Menu Home City Crime Politics Stories Francis Xavier Dr. Sudip Chakraborty Dr. Dinesh Anadiranjan Biswas Satya B. Dass Entertainment N & M Andaman A&N Police Nicobar District Sports . Search for: Andaman Live Videos Home Andaman Media Delegation Visits Pasteur Institute of India in Coonoor; Experts Share Insights on Rabies Prevention for Andaman and Vaccine Research Discover more Check Voting Info Book Beach Resorts Read Newspapers Andaman Media Delegation Visits Pasteur Institute of India in Coonoor; Experts Share Insights on Rabies Prevention for Andaman and Vaccine Research Sanjib Posted on 8 months ago Spread the love Sheekha Bureau Coonoor, March 13: A delegation of media representatives from the Andaman and Nicobar Islands visited the historic Pasteur Institute of India in Coonoor on 12 March as part of a study tour aimed at understanding the institute’s work in vaccine research, manufacturing and disease prevention. The visit provided the delegation with an opportunity to learn about the institute’s scientific activities and its long-standing contribution to vaccine development in the country. During the visit, officials of the institute briefed the delegation about its research programmes, vaccine manufacturing processes and the role it has played in producing vaccines for major infectious diseases. The delegation also interacted with Dr. S. Sivakumar is the Director of the Pasteur Institute of India and senior scientists, who explained how vaccines were produced in the past and how the institute plans to expand its manufacturing capabilities in the coming years. One of the key topics discussed during the interaction was rabies, a viral disease that affects all warm-blooded animals and is mainly transmitted through the saliva of infected animals, most commonly through dog bites. Experts at the institute explained that once the rabies virus enters the body, it travels through the nervous system to the brain, where it multiplies and damages neural tissue. Once the central nervous system is affected, survival chances are extremely low, making prevention and timely treatment crucial. The experts also spoke about the situation in the Andaman and Nicobar Islands, where rabies has not been reported so far. They advised that strict precautions must be maintained to keep the islands rabies-free. According to them, authorities should ensure that any animals brought from the mainland, particularly dogs and cats, are properly vaccinated and accompanied by valid vaccination certificates from recognized institutions. Proper verification of such certificates is essential before allowing animals to enter the islands. The scientists also highlighted the importance of immediate first aid in the event of an animal bite. Washing the wound thoroughly with plenty of water and seeking prompt medical attention for anti-rabies treatment can significantly reduce the risk of infection. The Pasteur Institute of India, located in Coonoor in Tamil Nadu, is one of the country’s premier government institutions dedicated to vaccine research and production. Functioning as an autonomous body under the Ministry of Health and Family Welfare, Government of India, the institute has played a pioneering role in the development and supply of vaccines for several decades. The institute traces its origins to 1907, when it was established as the Pasteur Institute of Southern India following the death of a young woman who could not receive timely anti-rabies treatment. Over the years, it has achieved several milestones in vaccine research and manufacturing, including the production of anti-rabies vaccines, influenza vaccines and oral polio vaccines. In 2001, the institute developed a purified rabies vaccine derived from Vero cells for human use at an affordable cost, becoming the first government-controlled institution in India to do so. The move significantly helped reduce the cost of rabies vaccines in the country. Today, the institute operates modern facilities for the production of DPT group vaccines and has an installed capacity of around 130 million doses. Its laboratories are equipped with advanced technology and equipment used for large-scale vaccine production and research. The institute is also involved in academic activities, including doctoral research in microbiology, biochemistry and biotechnology. In addition, its anti-rabies treatment centre, established in 1907, continues to treat animal bite cases and collect important epidemiological data. Looking ahead, the institute plans to expand its capabilities further by establishing new biosafety level facilities for the production of viral, bacterial, conjugate and recombinant DNA vaccines. These developments are expected to strengthen India’s vaccine security and preparedness for future public health challenges. The visit provided the Andaman media delegation with valuable insights into vaccine research and public health preparedness, particularly in understanding the importance of maintaining strict preventive measures to keep regions like the Andaman and Nicobar Islands free from rabies. Sanofi's Next-Generation Rabies Vaccine Secures Approval in EU - Yahoo! Finance Canada
新闻简报赛诺菲的下一代 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 已获得欧盟委员会 (EC) 的批准,于 2026 年 11 月 19 日在欧洲使用。此次批准是在欧洲药品管理局人用医药产品委员会于 2026 年 9 月发表积极意见之后进行的。疫苗 设计用于暴露前预防 (PrEP)和 暴露后预防 (PEP),为以前未接种疫苗的个体提供两剂 PrEP 方案和四剂 PEP 方案。临床研究表明,VaxRabeo 提供了可比的保护性抗体水平和强大的加强反应,其安全性与赛诺菲现有的 狂犬病 疫苗、Verorab 和 Imovax 狂犬病 一致。狂犬病 是一种通过受感染动物(尤其是狗)的唾液传播的病毒性疾病,一旦出现症状几乎总是致命的,但及时的 狂犬病 是可以预防的。此次批准加强了赛诺菲在 狂犬病 疫苗 市场的地位,并扩大了其在欧洲的产品范围。该公司还在美国寻求 VaxRabeo 的监管批准,计划于 2026 年第四季度提交申请。
查看新闻五要素
时间
继欧洲药品管理局人用医药产品委员会于 2026 年 9 月提出积极意见后,该批准于 2026 年 11 月 19 日宣布。
地点与对象
欧盟委员会 (EC) 批准 疫苗 在欧洲使用,针对所有年龄段的个人,重点关注有 狂犬病 暴露风险的人群,特别是占动物咬伤事件约 40% 的儿童。
事件
赛诺菲的下一代 狂犬病 疫苗 VaxRabeo 在欧盟获得了欧盟委员会的批准。此次批准基于临床研究,评估其针对赛诺菲现有 狂犬病、疫苗、Verorab 和 Imovax 狂犬病 的有效性和安全性。
规模、影响与风险
疫苗 的批准允许预暴露和 暴露后预防,并具有灵活的免疫计划。它表现出可比的保护性抗体水平和强大的加强反应,其安全性与现有的 狂犬病 疫苗 一致。一旦出现症状,狂犬病 几乎总是致命的,但可以通过及时的 狂犬病 来预防。
应对、状态与不确定性
批准已得到确认,没有重大安全问题的报道。赛诺菲正在美国寻求 VaxRabeo 的监管授权,预计于 2026 年第四季度提交申请。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Sanofi's Next-Generation Rabies Vaccine Secures Approval in EU - Yahoo! Finance Canada

News Brief
Sanofi's next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, has secured approval from the European Commission (EC) for use in Europe, effective November 19, 2026. This approval follows a positive opinion from the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use in September 2026. The vaccine is designed for both pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), offering a two-dose regimen for PrEP and a four-dose regimen for PEP in previously unvaccinated individuals. Clinical studies showed that VaxRabeo provides comparable protective antibody levels and a strong booster response, with a safety profile consistent with Sanofi's existing rabies vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies. Rabies, a viral disease transmitted through the saliva of infected animals, particularly dogs, is almost always fatal once symptoms appear, but it is preventable with timely vaccination. The approval strengthens Sanofi's position in the rabies vaccine market and expands its offerings in Europe. The company is also seeking regulatory approval for VaxRabeo in the United States, planning to submit an application in the fourth quarter of 2026.
Original source text (English)

Get Finance Plus Sanofi's Next-Generation Rabies Vaccine Secures Approval in EU Zacks Equity Research Thu, November 19, 2026 at 8:21 a.m. MST 4 min read SNY +1.00% SNY +1.00% ADPT -0.56% ACIU -8.45% AMRX +1.84% Sanofi SNY announced that the European Commission (EC) has approved its next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, for individuals of all ages. This marks the vaccine's first marketing authorization and allows its use for both pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). The approval was largely anticipated following a positive opinion issued by the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use in September 2026, which recommended the vaccine's authorization. The vaccine utilizes the same viral strain as Sanofi's existing rabies vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies. The next-generation vaccine is designed to lower the risk of allergic reactions while offering an extended shelf life and improved temperature stability compared with the company's existing rabies vaccines. The newly approved vaccine provides flexible immunization schedules, including a two-dose regimen for PrEP and a four-dose regimen for PEP in previously unvaccinated individuals. Rabies is a viral disease transmitted mainly through the saliva of infected animals, particularly dogs. Although rabies is preventable through timely vaccination, it is almost invariably fatal once clinical symptoms develop. Children are particularly vulnerable, with individuals below 15 years of age accounting for approximately 40% of people bitten by potentially rabid animals. Year to date, SNY's shares have risen 1.2% compared with the industry's 26.7% growth. Zacks Investment Research Image Source: Zacks Investment Research SNY's Clinical Studies Support VaxRabeo's EU Approval The EC's decision was backed by clinical studies evaluating VaxRabeo against Sanofi's existing rabies vaccines in both PrEP and PEP settings across different age groups. VaxRabeo demonstrated comparable protective antibody levels in pre-exposure vaccination and a similar immune response in post-exposure vaccination. It also showed a strong booster response when administered 12 or 24-36 months after the initial PrEP. The vaccine's safety profile was consistent with that of existing rabies vaccines, with no major safety concerns reported. Sanofi already has an established presence in the rabies vaccine market through its marketed products, Verorab and Imovax Rabies. The approval of VaxRabeo strengthens Sanofi's rabies vaccine portfolio and expands its preventive vaccine offerings in Europe. Beyond Europe, Sanofi is pursuing regulatory authorization for SP0087/VaxRabeo in the United States. The company expects to submit a regulatory application for the vaccine in the fourth quarter of 2026. Story Continues SNY's Zacks Rank & Stocks to Consider Sanofi currently carries a Zacks Rank #3 (Hold). Some better-ranked stocks in the biotech sector are Amneal Pharmaceuticals AMRX, AC Immune ACIU and Adaptive Biotechnologies ADPT, carrying a Zacks Rank #2 (Buy) each. Time
The approval was announced on November 19, 2026, following a positive opinion from the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use in September 2026.
Location and population
The European Commission (EC) approved the vaccine for use in Europe, targeting individuals of all ages, with a focus on populations at risk of rabies exposure, particularly children who account for about 40% of animal bites.
Event
Sanofi's next-generation rabies vaccine, VaxRabeo, secured approval in the EU from the European Commission. The approval was based on clinical studies evaluating its efficacy and safety against Sanofi's existing rabies vaccines, Verorab and Imovax Rabies.
Scale, impact and risk
The vaccine's approval allows for both pre-exposure and post-exposure prophylaxis, with flexible immunization schedules. It demonstrated comparable protective antibody levels and a strong booster response, and its safety profile was consistent with existing rabies vaccines. Rabies is almost invariably fatal once symptoms develop, but it is preventable through timely vaccination.
Response, status and uncertainty
The approval is confirmed, with no major safety concerns reported. Sanofi is pursuing regulatory authorization for VaxRabeo in the United States, expecting to submit an application in the fourth quarter of 2026.

俄罗斯联邦发现另外三件假冒 疫苗 系列 狂犬病 疫苗 «Nobivac» - Ветеринария и жизнь

News article (English original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 18 日,俄罗斯联邦又发现了三个假冒 疫苗 系列 狂犬病 疫苗 «Nobivac»。假冒 疫苗 于 2020 年生产,有效期为 2024 年。另一个系列于 2024 年为泰国市场生产,有效期为 2028 年。俄罗斯联邦,特别是 Rosselkhoznadzor 领导下的 DPR 和 LPR 理事会。受影响的人群包括家畜和农场动物,特别是那些接种了“Nobivac 狂犬病”系列疫苗的动物。另外三个系列的假冒“Nobivac 狂犬病”疫苗 被确定为未正式进口​​到俄罗斯联邦。相关通知发布在DPR和LPR Rosselkhoznadzor董事会网站上。这些假冒 疫苗 是为俄罗斯市场生产的,但生产日期和有效期不正确。假冒 疫苗 是为俄罗斯市场生产的,并进口到俄罗斯联邦境内,生产日期和有效期不正确。该风险与过期 疫苗 的潜在误用或管理有关,这可能会损害动物健康并导致 狂犬病 暴发。DPR 和 LPR 的 Rosselkhoznadzor 理事会报告了这一情况。消息人士表示,假冒 疫苗 并未正式进口​​,但没有提供有关调查或为防止进一步传播而采取的行动的详细信息。内容为完整的原创报告正文。

News article (English original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (English)
Three more counterfeit vaccines series of rabies vaccine «Nobivac» identified in the Russian Federation - Ветеринария и жизнь
News Brief
Three more counterfeit vaccines series of rabies vaccine «Nobivac» identified in the Russian Federation was reported on 18 Nov 2026. The counterfeit vaccines were produced in 2020 and had expiry dates in 2024. Another series was produced for the Thai market in 2024 with an expiry date in 2028. The Russian Federation, specifically the Directorate for the DPR and LPR under Rosselkhoznadzor. The affected population includes domestic and farm animals, particularly those vaccinated with the "Nobivac Rabies" series. Three more series of counterfeit "Nobivac Rabies" vaccines were identified as not officially imported into the Russian Federation. The relevant notice was published on the website of the Rosselkhoznadzor directorate for the DPR and LPR. These counterfeit vaccines were produced for the Russian market but had incorrect production and expiry dates. The counterfeit vaccines were produced for the Russian market and imported into the territory of the Russian Federation with incorrect production and expiry dates. The risk is related to potential misuse or administration of expired vaccines, which could compromise animal health and lead to rabies outbreaks. The situation was reported by the Rosselkhoznadzor directorate for the DPR and LPR. The source indicates that the counterfeit vaccines were not officially imported, but it does not provide details on the investigation or actions taken to prevent further spread. The content is a full original report body.

News, 18 Nov 2026 16:18 Three more counterfeit vaccines series of rabies vaccine «Nobivac» identified in the Russian Federation Three more series of vaccines for veterinary use, "Nobivac Rabies," have been identified, which, according to the official representative of LLC Intervet, were not officially imported into the Russian Federation. The relevant notice was published on the website of the Rosselkhoznadzor directorate for the DPR and LPR. Photo: Alexander Plonsky / «ViZh» This product is used to vaccinate both domestic and farm animals against rabies. It contains inactivated rabies virus (Pasteur RIV strain). The vaccine is produced by the Dutch company Intervet International B.V., a division of the U.S. MSD Animal Health. "veterinary medicinal product 'Nobivac® Rabies,' series A551А01 and series А572В02, was produced for the Russian market and imported into the territory of the Russian Federation, but with production date 08.2020, expiry date: 08.2024 and production date 12.2020, expiry date: 12.2024, respectively. Medicinal products with the same batch numbers but different expiry dates were not imported into the territory of the Russian Federation," the statement said. In addition, "Nobivac® Rabies" series А750A03 with production date 03.2024, expiry date 03.2028 was produced for the Thai market and was not imported into Russia. In October, "Veterinariya i Zhizn" wrote that the Rosselkhoznadzor had received a report from LLC Intervet about the identification of counterfeit products and batches: "Nobivac Rabies" — batch A598А01, expiry date 07.2026 and "Nobivac Rabies" — batch A592В04, expiry date 06.2026. At that time, an illegally imported batch of vaccines against feline calicivirosis, viral rhinotracheitis and panleukopenia, "Nobivac Tricat Trio with Nobivac Diluent," was also found (batch A549F01, expiry date 10.2026).
